### [1] [A Non-CDCL SAT Solver with Early Conflict Detection: The Watched-Literal-Based CSFLOC Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24255)
*Gábor Kusper*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出CSFLOC-WL及其当前实现CSFLOC-WL3，通过将监视文字前缀传播引入全长度有序子句计数框架，减少寻找蕴含子句的开销，并利用早期冲突检测生成新的计数器跳跃原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CSFLOC需要反复搜索能够蕴含当前全长度子句的输入子句，该过程构成主要性能瓶颈。为提高跳跃原因发现效率，本文尝试借鉴监视文字数据结构和原因子句。

Method: CSFLOC-WL3以计数器表示当前全长度子句的否定，并在其公共前缀下执行监视文字传播。当传播为同一变量推出相反的单位结论时，立即解析对应原因子句，将所得归结子句作为新的计数器跳跃原因。该方法仍是基于计数器的全长度子句计数求解器，不采用CDCL决策树、重启或First-UIP回跳机制。

Result: 在选定的SATLIB不可满足实例上，CSFLOC-WL3与CSFLOC21TU及CaDiCaL 3.0.0进行了比较。实验结果不一致：CSFLOC-WL3在若干接近随机3-SAT可满足性阈值的随机实例上表现较强，但在部分结构化实例上不及CSFLOC21TU。后者的优势可能来自更成熟的缓存机制，而该机制尚未集成到CSFLOC-WL3中。

Conclusion: 监视文字前缀传播和早期冲突解析能够有效增强CSFLOC的跳跃原因发现能力，并在特定随机实例上展现竞争力。然而，整体性能仍受缓存机制成熟度和实例结构影响，后续工作应重点完善缓存设计并扩大系统化实验。

Abstract: CSFLOC is a non-CDCL SAT decision procedure based on counting subsumed full-length ordered clauses. The classical CSFLOC loop traverses the ordered space of full-length clauses by a monotone counter: if the current full-length clause is not subsumed by the input formula, its negation is a satisfying assignment; otherwise, a subsuming clause determines a counter jump. The main bottleneck is the repeated search for such a subsuming clause. This paper presents CSFLOC-WL, and its current implementation CSFLOC-WL3, in which this search is replaced by watched-literal prefix propagation over the negation of the current full-length clause represented by the counter. The central mechanism is early conflict detection: if propagation under a common prefix derives opposite unit consequences for the same variable, then the two reason clauses are resolved immediately and the resolvent is used as a new counter-jump cause. The resulting solver is not a CDCL solver: it has no CDCL decision tree, no restart policy, and no first-UIP backjumping loop. It remains a counter-guided full-length-clause-counting solver, but it imports the watched-literal data structure and reason clauses as engineering tools for discovering jumps. Experiments on selected UNSAT SATLIB instances compare CSFLOC-WL3 with CSFLOC21TU and CaDiCaL 3.0.0. The results are mixed: CSFLOC-WL3 is strong on several random 3-SAT instances near the random-3-SAT satisfiability threshold, whereas CSFLOC21TU remains faster on several structured cases, apparently because it contains a more mature cache mechanism that is not yet present in CSFLOC-WL3.

</details>


### [2] [QisMC: A Model Checker for QISKIT Program Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24320)
*Aochu Dai,Mingsheng Ying*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: QisMC是首个专用于调试Qiskit程序的量子模型检测器，支持统一的性质描述、自动且穷尽的验证以及反例生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Qiskit程序调试工具在性质描述、自动化验证和反例分析方面存在不足，因此需要一个能够系统建模并验证量子程序行为的模型检测框架。

Method: 提出量子—经典转换系统和基于Birkhoff-von Neumann逻辑的量子计算树逻辑（qCTL），分别用于建模Qiskit程序行为和描述验证性质；同时实现包含转换系统生成、逻辑公式表示及模型检测算法的端到端框架，并利用决策图高效执行像计算。

Result: 示例和基准测试表明，QisMC能够对真实量子程序进行有效验证，具备良好的实用性、效率和可扩展性，并在统一性质描述、自动化穷尽验证及反例生成方面优于已有Qiskit程序调试器。

Conclusion: QisMC为Qiskit程序提供了完整的量子模型检测与调试方案，其理论模型、逻辑体系和高效实现共同支持对量子程序进行系统、自动和可扩展的正确性验证。

Abstract: We present QisMC, the first quantum model checker dedicated to debugging Qiskit programs. On the theoretical side, we introduce the notion of quantum-classical transition system and a quantum computation tree logic (qCTL) grounded in Birkhoff-von Neumann logic for modeling the behaviors and specifying the properties of Qiskit programs, respectively. On the implementation side, QisMC provides an end-to-end framework encompassing transition system generation, logical formula representation, and model checking algorithms, and it efficiently performs image computation based on decision diagrams. These methods and design principles give QisMC advantages over previous Qiskit program debuggers, including a unified property specification language, a fully automated and exhaustive verification process, and the ability to generate counterexamples. Extensive illustrative examples and benchmark evaluations demonstrate the practicality, efficiency, and scalability of QisMC in verifying realistic quantum programs.

</details>


### [3] [On existential Büchi arithmetic in two coprime bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24410)
*Joris Nieuwveld*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 研究表明：对于互质的自然数α和β，含有两个Büchi谓词V_α和V_β的Presburger算术，其存在片段是可判定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果显示，同时扩展V_α和V_β会使一阶理论不可判定，而仅加入幂谓词α^N和β^N时，存在片段可判定。此前尚不清楚Villemaire原始扩展的存在片段是否可判定，本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 针对互质的α和β，构造量词消去论证，将含有V_α和V_β的存在性公式转化为可判定形式，从而建立相应理论的存在片段的可判定性。

Result: 证明了FO(ℤ;<,+,V_α,V_β)的存在片段是可判定的，其中α和β为互质自然数。该结果补充并拓展了关于Presburger算术与Büchi谓词或幂谓词扩展的既有研究。

Conclusion: 尽管同时加入两个Büchi谓词会导致完整一阶理论不可判定，但在α和β互质的条件下，其存在片段仍保持可判定性。量词消去是获得这一结论的核心工具。

Abstract: For multiplicatively independent natural numbers $α$ and $β$, Villemaire showed in 1992 that the first-order theory of Presburger arithmetic expanded with both Büchi predicates $V_α$ and $V_β$ is undecidable, as it encodes multiplication. In recent years, Hieronymi and Schulz showed that Presburger arithmetic expanded with the weaker power predicates $α^\mathbb{N} = \{α^n: n \in \mathbb{N}\}$ and $β^\mathbb{N}$ is also undecidable, while Karimov et al. showed that the existential fragment of this theory is decidable. These results left open the natural problem of determining the decidability of the existential fragment of Villemaire's original expansion. We settle this question for coprime $α$ and $β$. Specifically, we give a quantifier-elimination argument that proves the decidability of the existential fragment of $\mathsf{FO}(\mathbb{Z};<,+, V_α, V_β)$.

</details>


### [4] [Comparison Invariants for Verifying Control Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24598)
*Promit Panja,André Platzer*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文在微分动态逻辑（dL）中建立控制不变性的公理与证明规则，并以比较原理和比较不变量为核心，统一验证控制屏障函数及多种安全验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制不变性是证明动态系统始终保持给定性质的基础，但现有控制屏障函数等方法通常侧重于安全控制器综合，缺乏系统化的形式化验证框架。因此，本文旨在为控制不变性提供可靠、可复用且具有潜在自动化能力的证明基础。

Method: 首先在dL中公理化标量和向量比较原理，主要借助微分幽灵将原系统与比较系统关联起来。随后定义比较不变量，并推导相应的可靠公理和证明规则，将控制不变性转化为适当函数沿李导数方向满足的函数不等式。本文进一步证明控制屏障函数是比较不变量的特例，并为其建立专门的验证规则；同时分析比较不变量与达布不变量、微分不变量等方法之间的统一关系。

Result: 研究给出了比较原理、比较不变量和控制屏障函数的形式化公理化框架。比较不变量能够将控制不变性验证化为函数不等式问题，恰当选择函数时还可归约到可判定的算术问题。所提出的规则支持对控制屏障函数进行形式化验证，并统一解释多种已有安全验证技术。

Conclusion: 比较不变量为控制不变性提供了通用且可靠的dL证明框架，兼具理论统一性和验证实用性。该框架不仅覆盖控制屏障函数，还连接达布不变量与微分不变量等方法，有望促进安全关键动态系统的自动化和可验证控制。

Abstract: Control invariance validates that dynamical systems have a control input that preserves a given property at all times. This paper introduces a set of sound axioms and proof rules in differential dynamic logic (dL) that enable verification of control invariance. First, the scalar and vector comparison principles, relating a system of differential equations to a comparison system such that invariance properties can be established more easily, are axiomatized in dL. This axiomatization primarily utilizes differential ghosts, which are proof-theoretic generalizations of comparison systems. Next, with the comparison principles serving as the basis, comparison invariants are introduced, and sound axioms and proof rules are derived. Comparison invariants reduce the question of control invariance to a functional inequality on its Lie derivative for a suitable class of functions, moreover, the right choice of function can result in decidable arithmetic. Furthermore, the perennially popular control barrier functions (CBFs) used in safety-critical control are shown to be a special instance of comparison invariants. This yields an axiomatization of CBFs that leads to a dedicated set of proof rules. The rules allow for the verification of CBFs, which are traditionally used for synthesizing safe controllers without verification. Lastly, comparison invariants are shown to unify several other safety verification techniques, including Darboux invariants and differential invariants, further cementing their versatility.

</details>


### [5] [Design and Empirical Characterization of a Hardware-Realized Turing Machine with Automated Card-Based Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24742)
*Agrima Regmi,Jenish Pant,Pratistha Sapkota,Sanskriti Khatiwada,Binod Sapkota*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可自主运行、支持光学重编程输入的硬件图灵机，通过Arduino Mega、步进电机、红外传感器和ESP32-CAM实现多步计算与状态表自动加载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理图灵机和机械逻辑演示通常依赖人工触发计算步骤或重新配置状态表，难以自主执行较长计算。本文旨在消除人工干预限制，并提升物理实现的可编程性与可验证性。

Method: 系统采用Arduino Mega执行状态转移逻辑，双NEMA 17步进电机驱动双向纸带，红外反射传感器识别符号，ESP32-CAM读取光学打孔卡并自动加载状态表。针对非均匀光照和微控制器资源限制，采用局部自适应阈值与广度优先搜索洪泛填充算法进行孔洞检测，并通过机械压平改善卡片读取效果。

Result: 在20张卡片测试中，打孔卡解码准确率由75%提升至90%，机械压平后达到100%。系统加工精度为±0.15 mm，齿条齿轮定位误差在50次试验中低于0.3 mm，满载时电源电压稳定性为±0.2 V。硬件运行结果与tlang软件模拟器在多个测试程序中完全一致。

Conclusion: 该系统实现了物理图灵机的自主多步执行、光学可重编程输入和端到端计算验证，较现有依赖人工操作的演示装置显著增强了自动化能力，并通过机械、光学和计算性能指标证明了系统的有效性。

Abstract: Physical implementations of Turing Machines remain rare, and existing electromechanical demonstrators and mechanical logic games typically require manual operator intervention, either to trigger each computational step or to reconfigure the state table, or both. This restricts prior physical models to short, operator-paced demonstrations and prevents autonomous execution of extended computations. This paper addresses that gap with a hardware Turing Machine that enables autonomous multi-step execution and reprogrammable optical input without manual intervention between programs. The system integrates an Arduino Mega for state-transition logic, dual NEMA 17 stepper motors for bidirectional tape actuation, infrared reflectance sensors for symbol detection, and an ESP32-CAM-based optical punched-card reader for automated state-table loading. Hole detection under non-uniform illumination used a Breadth-First Search flood-fill algorithm with local adaptive thresholding rather than fixed global thresholding, driven by the memory and library constraints of the ESP32-CAM's microcontroller environment; this improved card-decoding accuracy from 75% to 90% (100% with mechanical card flattening) on a 20-card test set. Mechanical evaluation showed fabrication accuracy of +/-0.15 mm, rack-and-pinion positional error below 0.3 mm across 50 trials, and voltage supply stability within +/-0.2 V under full system load. End-to-end computation was validated against a parallel software simulator (tlang), with all hardware outputs matching the simulated reference exactly across multiple test programs. The system advances prior physical Turing Machine demonstrations through autonomous execution, reprogrammable optical input, and quantitative evaluation of its mechanical, optical, and computational performance.

</details>


### [6] [Cycle time minimization for the simple assembly line balancing problem under peak power constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24779)
*Bao Gia Hoang,Tuyen Van Kieu,Khanh Van To*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 研究针对固定工作站数量和总瞬时功率上限下的简单装配线平衡问题类型2（SALBP-2），采用精确SAT方法最小化生产节拍，并与商业求解器进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 峰值功率约束会限制并行任务的执行，可能延长装配线节拍时间。现有研究较少关注同时考虑固定工作站数和瞬时功率上限的SALBP-2优化问题。

Method: 构建精确SAT求解方法，用于寻找可行调度、系统搜索更短节拍并在可能时证明最优性。基于标准SALBP测试库的72个案例，定义较宽松和较严格的两类可复现功率上限，并使用Gurobi和CPLEX进行混合整数规划及约束规划对比。

Result: 与标准SALBP-2最优结果相比，较宽松和较严格功率上限分别使平均最优节拍增加16.49%和61.02%。表现最佳的SAT配置为全部案例找到可行解，其达到最优的案例数超过各商业求解器；在双方均能证明最优的案例中，SAT在所报告设置下几乎总是更快。

Conclusion: 功率约束显著增加装配线节拍，且约束越严格，影响越大。SAT方法在可行性、最优性求解数量和求解速度方面展现出优于Gurobi与CPLEX的综合性能，适用于该类受功率限制的SALBP-2精确优化。

Abstract: Peak power limits restrict concurrent tasks and may increase assembly-line cycle time. To the best of our knowledge, this study is the first to minimize cycle time for the simple assembly line balancing problem type 2 (SALBP-2) with a fixed number of workstations and a fixed limit on total instantaneous power. An exact satisfiability (SAT) method finds feasible schedules, searches systematically for shorter cycles, and proves optimality when possible. Two reproducible formulas define looser and tighter power limits for 72 cases based on the standard SALBP library, with Gurobi and CPLEX providing commercial MIP and CP comparisons. Relative to standard SALBP-2 optima, the two limits increase the best-known cycle time by 16.49% and 61.02% on average. The best-performing SAT configurations find a feasible solution for every case, solve more cases to optimality than each commercial solver, and when both prove optimality, are almost always faster under the reported settings.

</details>
