### [7] [Decomposing Browser Pipeline Architectures for DOM-Sourced Particle Effects: Worker Offload, WebGL, and WebAssembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23609)
*Hossein Asadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了线程位置、渲染器和模拟后端对浏览器粒子管线性能的影响，发现局部优化未必能转化为端到端帧率提升，性能结论高度依赖浏览器、GPU和主机配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web Workers、WebGL和WebAssembly常被笼统视为通用加速手段，但它们作用于不同架构层。本文旨在识别主导瓶颈，并验证某一层的性能改进能否传递到用户可感知的交互帧率。

Method: 以DOM驱动的粒子工作负载为对象，构建五种粒子管线P1–P5及一个CSS层基线P0，控制线程位置、渲染器和模拟后端。通过可复现实验框架测量交互帧率、高负载端到端性能和仅模拟微基准，并在不同浏览器、GPU类别及Google Colab Tesla T4环境中进行复现。

Result: 在主要Chrome环境中，Worker卸载使交互帧率约从52 FPS提升至144 FPS。AssemblyScript使Chrome模拟内核速度提升约1.5–1.6倍，在Colab T4上提升约1.85倍。然而，在约25万个WebGL粒子负载下，Chrome中WASM的模拟加速未稳定转化为更高的产品帧率；P5不优于或约等于P4，且线程内计时显示模拟阶段仍占主要已计量开销。渲染器排名和绝对性能差距会随浏览器、GPU及主机配置变化。

Conclusion: 浏览器性能优化应采用面向瓶颈的分层评估，而不能依据单项微基准推断端到端收益。只有当优化层正好对应主导瓶颈，并能在完整管线中传递其收益时，局部加速才可能转化为用户可见的帧率提升。

Abstract: Local optimization does not necessarily yield end-to-end optimization in layered browser architectures. Teams often treat Web Workers, WebGL, and WebAssembly as interchangeable ways to "make it faster," yet each lever targets a different layer. We present a controlled architectural decomposition--using DOM-sourced particle pipelines as a concrete workload--of five particle pipelines (P1-P5), with an additional non-particle CSS-layer baseline (P0), that isolates thread placement, renderer choice, and simulation backend. Using a reproducible harness we measure interactive pacing, high-load end-to-end stress, and a simulation-only microbenchmark, plus same-host cross-browser / dual-GPU-class replication (Firefox 153+Intel UHD; Chrome 138+NVIDIA NVK) and an independent second-host slice on Google Colab (Chrome 150, Tesla T4, n=5). Four results stand out. (1) Worker offload improves interactive pacing on paper-primary Chrome (approx. 144 vs approx. 52 FPS; Cliff's delta=1, n=10). (2) AssemblyScript speeds the Chrome update kernel by about 1.5-1.6x on the primary host and approx. 1.85x on Colab T4 (sim-only). (3) Under approx. 250k WebGL particles the Chrome sim-only WASM win need not raise product FPS (P5<=P4 on primary/Firefox; P5 approx. P4 on Colab T4); in-worker CPU phase timers show simulate still dominates the accounted budget, so the non-translation is not a simple "draw dominates CPU" story. (4) Renderer ranking and absolute margins vary across browser/GPU/host configurations. The contribution is bottleneck-aware architectural measurement: identify the dominant layer and test whether a layer win propagates to user-visible FPS.

</details>


### [8] [DeepRepoQA: Code Repository Question Answering with Deep Agent Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24221)
*Weihan Peng,Yuling Shi,Yingwei Ma,Longfei Yun,Beijun Shen,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeepRepoQA提出一种基于智能体和蒙特卡洛树搜索的仓库级代码问答框架，通过系统化探索代码仓库实现跨文件、多跳依赖推理，并在SWE-QA基准上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库理解方法主要依赖表层代码检索，难以处理多文件交互、复杂软件架构以及远距离代码依赖，导致其在回答需要深层推理的软件仓库问题时能力不足。

Method: DeepRepoQA采用智能体框架，让大语言模型智能体围绕仓库结构进行系统化树搜索。框架引入蒙特卡洛树搜索机制，使智能体能够动态搜索、导航和检查代码，从而支持针对远距离代码依赖的多跳推理。

Result: 在SWE-QA基准上的综合实验表明，DeepRepoQA相较于多个强基线取得了显著的性能提升，验证了蒙特卡洛树搜索引导的系统化仓库探索策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将智能体推理与蒙特卡洛树搜索相结合，DeepRepoQA增强了仓库级代码理解和问答能力，为复杂软件结构中的多跳依赖分析提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Answering developer questions about a software repository is a critical yet under-explored problem in software engineering. While existing repository understanding methods have advanced the field, they predominantly rely on surface-level code retrieval and lack the ability for deep reasoning over multiple files, complex software architectures, and grounding answers in long-range code dependencies. To address these limitations, we propose DeepRepoQA, a novel question answering (QA) framework for repository-level code understanding. DeepRepoQA builds on an agentic framework where LLM agents find answers through a systematic tree search over the repository structure. A Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) mechanism is employed to empower agents to dynamically search, navigate, and inspect code, enabling effective multi-hop reasoning over long-range code dependencies. Comprehensive experiments on the SWE-QA benchmark demonstrate substantial performance gains over strong baselines, validating the effectiveness of systematic MCTS-guided exploration for multi-hop repository reasoning.

</details>


### [9] [From Traceability to Justifiability: Accountability Structures in Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23610)
*Rashid Azarang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究发现，现有AI交付与服务平台的默认记录普遍无法对模型版本、指令、工具、检索及运行时配置组成的完整行为实体进行内容寻址，也无法充分证明部署转换获得了相应证据授权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估AI系统交付记录是否能够表达“被评估对象即被部署对象”的可验证主张，并检验其声明的保障深度是否在实际发布中得到实现。

Method: 研究分为两部分：第一部分对47个交付平台开展双重盲评文档调查，采用固定的三标签协议，并对来源页面进行内容哈希和日期固定；第二部分开发保障深度测量工具，在30个公开仓库的冻结分层样本上，对照流水线公开产物与其声明进行双重盲评。

Result: 47个平台均未在默认记录中输出完整行为实体的内容寻址身份，盲评中该项为0/47；27个智能体平台中16个仅采用可能指向可变对象的整数版本号。30个仓库中，15个采用证明工具的仓库有7个只发布源代码，导致其声明的绑定关系无法核验。在可核验案例中，7个有5个实现了端到端绑定，两个不足均源于身份绑定。

Conclusion: 当前记录体系在结构上仍不足以支撑可辩护的部署转换，主要缺口是对实际行为实体的身份绑定，而非单纯的版本编号或工具采用。研究同时给出了样本冻结、盲评和失效条件，以支持后续复核；调查结论也具有明确的时效性。

Abstract: A pipeline promoting an AI system publishes records claiming the thing evaluated is the thing deployed and that the evidence licensed the transition. We measure, from public material only, whether those records can express that claim and whether it holds where declared. First, a two-class documentation survey of 47 delivery platforms (20 CI/CD, 27 model-serving/agent) under one fixed three-label protocol, graded twice (second pass blind), every consulted page pinned by content hash and date. Across 188 double-graded cells we found no platform whose default record emits a content-addressed identity of the behavioral tuple (model version, instructions, tool definitions, retrieval and runtime configuration); the blind pass grades that column default on zero of 47. Immutable nominal versioning is meanwhile arriving as the agent platforms' default answer (16 of 27): version integers behind mutable pointers, a layer the artifact supply chain already found insufficient. Second, an instrument computes realized assurance depth from a pipeline's published exhaust alone and compares it with the declared depth. Applied to a frozen two-stratum frame of 30 public repositories graded twice from a hashed archive (second pass blind; cell-level agreement 23 and 19 of 30, both passes independently finding the same five full realizations), the sharpest result is a verifiability hole: seven of the 15 repositories chosen for adopting attestation tooling publish source-only releases, so the binding their workflows declare cannot be checked where declared. Where checkable it mostly checks out: five of seven measurable adopters realize the binding end to end; both shortfalls fall at identity binding. Together the results locate the field's records structurally short of justifiability, the one rung that can refuse a transition. The survey carries an expiry clock; we state what would falsify each finding.

</details>


### [10] [A Literate Programming Environment for Human and Machine Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24644)
*Adam T. Burke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个支持语言智能代理协作构建文学化程序的开发环境，将代码、自然语言说明和结构化数据组织在统一上下文中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型编程工具对代码符号、使用关系及相关自然语言上下文的利用不足，限制了代理的理解、生成和维护能力。

Method: 系统设计了可执行程序文章语法、将名称视为一等对象的解析器、连接文本与名称及可执行工件的内部名称图，以及面向既有编程语言和测试工具集的绑定机制。

Result: 研究实现了一个可运行原型，支持三种成熟编程语言，并通过多个示例程序验证其可行性，为代理提供了类似集成开发环境中符号搜索和引用信息的工具能力。

Conclusion: 该环境能够将程序及其最相关的说明和数据共同置于模型上下文中，从而提升语言智能代理进行代码理解、编写和协作开发的潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces an environment for constructing literate programs in concert with language-aware machine agents. This environment includes a grammar for executable program essays, a parser that treats names as first-class objects, an internal name-graph which relates prose, names and executable artifacts, and a binding mechanism for existing languages and testing toolsets. This supports co-location of code with its most relevant natural language and structured data context, making better use of Large Language Model (LLM) context windows. It also provides LLM coding agents with a toolset more analogous to the symbol-aware search and usage information available in human programmer-facing Integrated Development Environments (IDEs). We describe a working implementation with bindings to three established programming languages, and several example programs.

</details>


### [11] [REFINE: A Multi-Agent LLM Approach for Evidence-Guided Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23611)
*Muhammad Waseem,Aakash Ahmad,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: REFINE是一种面向Java文件级重构的工具无关、证据感知型多智能体方法，通过静态分析、规划、代码变换、自动复检和保持性检查生成重构候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能够自动生成重构代码，但需要在减少目标代码异味的同时避免引入新问题或改变与行为相关的代码结构，因此需要更可靠的生成与验证流程。

Method: REFINE结合静态分析驱动的异味识别、基于异味的重构规划、LLM代码变换、自动重新分析、保持性检查和结构化报告。研究在15个开源系统的450个Java文件上进行评估，使用三种模型生成共1350个模型输出，并与150个文件上的直接提示基线进行匹配比较。

Result: 三种模型配置下检测到的代码异味分别减少68.26%、72.79%和68.49%，其中主要异味的改善最明显。与直接提示基线相比，REFINE取得了更高的代码异味减少中位数，编辑规模更小，公共方法删除也更少。但整体质量提升并不稳定，保持性检查仍发现断言或失败调用变化、公共方法删除等风险。

Conclusion: REFINE能够有效提升LLM生成Java重构候选的异味消除效果和编辑可控性，但不能替代完整的软件工程验证。其输出在实际采用前仍需经过编译、测试、依赖分析和人工审查。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new opportunities for automated code refactoring. However, generated changes must reduce targeted quality problems without introducing new issues or altering behaviour-relevant code structures. We introduce REFINE (Refactoring with Evidence-aware Flow for Integrated ageNtic Execution), a tool-agnostic, evidence-aware multi-agent approach for generating Java file-level refactoring candidates. REFINE combines static-analysis-guided smell identification, smell-informed planning, LLM-based transformation, automated re-analysis, preservation checks, and structured reporting.
  We evaluate REFINE on 450 Java files from 15 open-source systems, producing 1,350 model-pass outputs using OpenAI GPT-5.5, Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview, and Anthropic Claude Opus 4.8. REFINE reduces detected code smells by 68.26%, 72.79%, and 68.49% across the three configurations, respectively, with the strongest reductions observed for major smells. A matched 150-file direct-prompt baseline shows that REFINE achieves a higher median code-smell reduction with smaller edits and fewer public-method removals. However, broader quality improvements are inconsistent, and preservation checks reveal residual risks, including assert/fail-call changes and public-method removal. Therefore, REFINE outputs should be treated as refactoring candidates requiring compilation, testing, dependency analysis, and human review before adoption in repository- or system-level settings.

</details>


### [12] [SDR Driver for Precise Timing Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23614)
*Fabrizio Pollastri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文开发了适用于低成本HackRF One软件定义无线电的精确定时驱动，实现同步采样、时间尺度控制和时钟频率调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HackRF One原有固件缺乏满足精确定时应用所需的同步采样与时钟控制功能，因此需要开发兼容性更好、可维护性更强的软件驱动。

Method: 首先基于Python封装实现定时功能，随后重构为兼容SoapySDR的驱动。开发过程中利用AI辅助流程，根据现有HackRF One和SoapySDR代码库生成新驱动，并进行有限调试。

Result: 新驱动成功运行，实现了同步采样、时间尺度控制和时钟频率调节。与传统开发方式相比，开发工作量减少了一个数量级以上，同时提升了软件可维护性。

Conclusion: 基于AI辅助开发和现有开源代码库，可以高效构建具备精确定时能力的HackRF One驱动，为低成本软件定义无线电在精密时序应用中的使用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper describes the development of software drivers that enable precise timing applications with the low-cost HackRF One software-defined radio (SDR). The original firmware was extended with timing functions for synchronized sampling, time-scale control, and clock-frequency adjustment. An initial implementation based on a Python wrapper was later redesigned as a SoapySDR-compatible driver. Using an AI-assisted development workflow, the new driver was generated from the existing HackRF One and SoapySDR codebases and became operational after only limited debugging. The approach reduced development effort by more than an order of magnitude while improving software maintainability.

</details>


### [13] [Rebuild Dossier: Mechanically-Enforced Specs for Agentic App Rebuilds, and What Model-Tier Failures Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23616)
*Parker Fawcett*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 rebuild-dossier，通过先锁定应用的精确输入输出接口，并以自动化检查强制逐测试构建，改进 AI 代理的软件重建流程。结果表明，该方法能发现测试作弊、日志错误和流程失效，但整体优势依赖检查机制的可靠运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究发现，当模型能力足够强时，多代理重建流程可能不如直接提供源代码和单条指令的方法。论文旨在提高重建过程的可验证性，避免仅凭代理报告或通过测试套件就误判实现正确性。

Method: 开发开源工具 rebuild-dossier，在编程前固定应用的真实输入输出接口，并通过自动化机制实施一次一个测试的构建流程。评估包含规则遵循与违规代理对比、与直接源代码加单指令基线对比，以及代理报告、自动化日志和实际产出文件的三级交叉验证；同时在不同模型和工具链上重复实验。

Result: 小规模比较显示，遵守流程的代理未通过一个保留测试，而违规代理全部通过，说明测试可能被规避。与基线相比，该方法在小型应用上持平，在大型应用上因自动检查未运行而明显失败，表明检查机制比接口锁定更关键。三级验证发现了真实实现错误及工具自身的日志缺陷；更强模型连续三次遵循流程，而较弱模型未能做到。

Conclusion: rebuild-dossier提升了软件重建的可审计性和错误发现能力，接口锁定本身具有一定稳定性，但方法成效高度依赖自动化检查的实际执行。研究支持采用多层证据验证，而不能仅依赖测试结果或代理自述；工具已开源并采用 MIT 许可证。

Abstract: An AI agent's rebuild is only as good as the process that produced it. Prior work found that once a model is strong enough, a multi-agent rebuild pipeline loses to the simplest approach: giving the model the original code and one instruction (AgentModernize). We present rebuild-dossier, an open-source tool that locks an application's real interface - its exact inputs and outputs - before any code is written, then enforces one-test-at-a-time building through automated checks, not written instructions alone.
  Three results shape this evaluation, with differing amounts of evidence. First, in a small comparison, the compliant agent failed a held-back test while the rule-breaking agent passed everything - proof that a passing suite doesn't certify correctness when tests can be gamed. Second, we tested whether this beats simply giving the weaker model the source and one instruction: tied on a small app, but lost outright on a larger one where the automated check wasn't even running - pointing to the check mechanism, not interface-locking, which held up separately. Third, every claim here is checked at three levels - the agent's own report, an automated log, and the actual files produced - catching real errors, including a bug in our own logging code, that a single level would have missed.
  These risks reproduce on a different model and toolchain: a stronger model followed our process three times running, something the weaker model never managed. The tool is public, MIT licensed, and reproduces end to end against our own applications.

</details>


### [14] [Identifying Latent Declarative Representations of Code for Assisting Repository Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23619)
*Shraddha Surana,Ashwin Srinivasan,Michael Bain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ADFD-Migrate通过显式构建带注释的数据流图，将遗留程序的隐含声明式语义用于大规模Fortran到Python的迁移，在行为一致性、迁移覆盖率和综合迁移效果方面均优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留软件仓库包含大量未文档化的领域知识，直接理解、维护和现代化迁移较为困难。现有代码翻译方法容易遗漏行为和跨模块依赖，因此需要一种可检查、可迭代的中间语义表示来提升迁移的完整性与可靠性。

Method: ADFD-Migrate使用大语言模型在有限仓库上下文中推断源程序的带注释数据流图，表示过程、数据存储、外部实体、数据流及行为契约，并通过静态分析覆盖率检查进行约束。随后采用依赖感知的分块策略生成目标语言代码，再通过比较源数据流图与静态恢复的目标数据流图定位差异并指导再生成。研究在包含50个不同规模和复杂度Fortran仓库的f2x50基准上进行评估。

Result: 在382个经过整理的Fortran行为探针中，生成的Python程序通过327个，行为一致率为85.6%，其中40个仓库通过全部尝试的探针。方法覆盖全部382项计划行为，而直接翻译、仓库上下文翻译、静态画像消融和依赖分块消融分别覆盖99、98、69和30项。平均综合迁移结果指数达到93.1%，并在47个仓库上较直接翻译高出17至59个百分点。

Conclusion: 将可检查的语义瓶颈引入仓库级程序迁移，能够改善行为覆盖、模块集成和迁移效果，并支持对多个仓库进行成本较低的代码生成。结果表明，显式的数据流与行为表示是提升大规模遗留软件现代化质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Legacy software repositories embed decades of domain knowledge in undocumented code, making understanding and modernization difficult. We treat a program as the implementation of an unobserved, declarative description of its computation and investigate whether making this latent declarative representation explicit improves repository-scale porting. ADFD-Migrate approximates the latent representation with an annotated data-flow diagram (ADFD) of processes, data stores, external entities, flows, and behavioral contracts. An LLM infers the source ADFD from bounded repository context, guided by static-analysis coverage checks. Dependency-aware chunking orders bounded process groups for target-language generation. Differences between the source ADFD and a statically recovered target ADFD then guide regeneration. We evaluate ADFD-Migrate on f2x50, a new benchmark of 50~Fortran repositories spanning 1.5k--1.6M lines of code and three complexity tiers, and assess the resulting ports along two dimensions: porting soundness, measured by source-oracle behavioral agreement, and porting completeness, measured by a composite migration outcome index. Against 382 curated Fortran-oracle probes, the generated Python passes 327 (85.6\%), with 40 repositories passing every attempted probe. ADFD-Migrate exposes all 382 planned behaviors as runnable targets, compared with 99 and 98 for direct and repository-context translation and 69 and 30 for the static-profile and dependency-chunking ablations. It also achieves a 93.1\% mean migration outcome index and a 17--59 percentage-point outcome-index advantage over direct translation on 47 repositories. These results suggest that an inspectable semantic bottleneck can improve the coverage and integration of repository-scale migration while enabling lower-cost generation for many repositories.

</details>


### [15] [When May an Agent Stop? Evidence-Carrying Termination for Tool-Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23623)
*Jason Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出证据携带终止机制（ECT），要求智能体仅在所有答案声明均绑定到有效且范围内的轨迹证据，并可通过确定性重放重构所声明值时返回COMPLETE。实验显示，ECT显著降低了无证据提前终止，同时保持支持性完成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用智能体通常能够判断终端成功、验证执行轨迹或实施运行时策略，但尚未系统检验一种在COMPLETE边界上结合收据、范围约束与闭环重放的终止设计。研究旨在降低智能体在证据不足时错误结束的风险。

Method: 研究实例化ECT，并在六类工具使用任务上的48个全合成任务中进行受控评估，覆盖正常执行和八类故障。随后开展预先设定且冻结的576条轨迹研究，将ECT与终止评论器核心、其忠实控制器及完整轨迹语言模型评论器进行比较。ECT通过类型化证书绑定答案声明与轨迹证据，并使用确定性重放验证声明值。

Result: 在288次初始评估中，ECT产生0次不安全完成，而对照终止评论器核心产生252次。主要留出任务簇中，ECT的提前且无支持终止为0/66，控制器为40/66，差异为-60.61个百分点；支持性完成分别为97/132和92/132，差异为3.79个百分点，并满足预设的10个百分点非劣效界值。ECT在66条轨迹中成功恢复18次，其中17次随后获得支持性完成，三个闭环门控指标均通过。

Conclusion: ECT能有效抑制工具使用智能体的无证据提前终止，并在支持性完成率方面不劣于基线。其保证范围限于：在明确假设下，记录轨迹中的证据支持及可重放性；这并不等同于外部事实真实性、安全性或整体对齐性。

Abstract: Tool-using agents must decide when to stop. Existing systems already gate terminal success, certify execution traces, or enforce runtime polici es, but do not test this particular receipt-, scope-, and closed-replay design at the COMPLETE boundary across controlled termination faults. W e instantiate and evaluate Evidence-Carrying Termination (ECT): an agent may return COMPLETE only when a typed certificate binds every required answer claim to valid, in-scope trace evidence and a deterministic replay reconstructs the claimed value. A locked static study crosses 48 ful ly synthetic tasks in six tool-use families with clean execution and eight faults. ECT produced 0/288 unsafe completions versus 252/288 for the inspected termination-critic core (difference -87.50 pp, 95% task-cluster interval [-87.50, -87.50] pp). A fresh, prespecified and frozen 576- trajectory study then compares ECT with the critic core, its faithful controller, and a full-trace LLM critic. On 22 primary held-out task clus ters, ECT produced 0/66 premature unsupported terminations versus 40/66 for the controller (difference -60.61 pp, 95% interval [-78.79, -40.91] pp), while supported completion was 97/132 versus 92/132 (difference 3.79 pp, interval [0.00, 9.09] pp), satisfying a -10-point noninferiority margin. ECT executed successful recovery in 18/66 trajectories, of which 17 subsequently completed with support; all three closed-loop gates p assed. ECT certifies support in a recorded trace under declared assumptions, not external truth, safety, or alignment.

</details>


### [16] [Callability Is Not Operability: Controlled Interface Interventions for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23628)
*Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究工具接口的可操作性，强调工具不仅要可调用，还应向智能体提供足以支持安全后续决策的状态与语义信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当外部操作已经提交但响应丢失时，智能体可能无法区分操作已提交与未提交两种状态，进而采取错误的后续行动。因此，需要评估工具接口在运行不确定性下是否支持可靠决策。

Method: 提出智能体优先工具设计（AFT），涵盖能力选择性发现、执行生命周期与恢复、外部效果语义、机器可读结果以及后置条件验证等机制；同时构建AFT-Bench，在固定任务、后端、初始状态、故障注入方式、智能体和语言模型的条件下，仅改变工具接口进行对照评估。

Result: 摘要主要介绍了可操作性概念、AFT设计框架和AFT-Bench评测方法，尚未报告具体实验结果或定量性能。

Conclusion: 工具接口的质量不能仅以是否能够成功调用衡量，还应考察其能否暴露与行动相关的状态和语义，使自主智能体在响应丢失等不确定情况下安全、正确地继续执行。

Abstract: A tool call can be perfectly valid yet still leave an autonomous agent unable to determine what to do next. For example, if an external effect commits but its response is lost, committed and uncommitted states may become indistinguishable to the agent even though they require different continuation actions. We study this gap between callability and operability: whether a tool interface exposes the action-relevant state and semantics needed for an agent to continue safely under operational uncertainty. We operationalize tool operability through Agent-First Tooling (AFT), a set of interface mechanisms spanning selective capability discovery, execution lifecycle and recovery, explicit external-effect semantics, machine-readable results, and postcondition verification. We introduce AFT-Bench, a controlled interface-intervention framework that holds the task, backend, initial state, injected failure, agent, and language model fixed while varying the interface exposed to the agent.

</details>


### [17] [FPGAgent: An LLM-Assisted Framework for Autonomous HLS Code Generation and Verification in FPGA Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23630)
*Tianyu Wang,Wenjie Wang,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FPGAgent是面向真实FPGA环境的多智能体HLS代码生成框架，通过进化搜索、功能验证、缺陷诊断与定向修复，实现从自然语言任务描述到可执行硬件设计的端到端生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM辅助HLS方法通常仅验证仿真或综合结果，未充分考虑时序、布局布线及真实硬件执行约束，导致生成代码可能无法部署运行。同时，缺乏公开基准限制了方法评估的规模与可信度。

Method: FPGAgent首先注入HLS领域知识，并采用进化搜索迭代生成可靠的HLS内核实现；随后自动生成C++验证程序进行功能正确性检查，诊断缺陷并指导定向修复；最后生成主机端代码，在真实FPGA平台上完成编译与板级执行验证。研究在包含78个多领域任务的HLS-Eval基准上，使用5种主流LLM进行评估。

Result: 相较于现有基线，FPGAgent平均将可综合率提升16.9%，可执行率提升26.7%，功能正确性提升30.6%，并在真实FPGA平台上验证了板级可执行性。

Conclusion: 端到端执行验证能够显著提升LLM生成HLS代码的实际可用性。FPGAgent在综合、执行和功能正确性方面均取得明显改进，证明了多智能体协作、迭代搜索与真实硬件验证相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown substantial promise for high-level synthesis (HLS) code generation, but most existing approaches validate only simulation or synthesis results. Because of timing and place-and-route constraints, \emph{HLS code that passes simulation and synthesis may still fail to produce deployable, runnable designs on real FPGA platforms}. Moreover, the lack of public benchmarks has limited many evaluations to small, self-curated test suites. We propose FPGAgent, a multi-agent framework tailored to real FPGA environments for autonomous HLS coding with end-to-end executability validation. To the best of our knowledge, FPGAgent is \emph{the first task-specification-to-executable HLS generation framework experimentally validated on a well-established benchmark}. Given a natural-language task specification, FPGAgent injects HLS-specific knowledge and employs evolutionary search to iteratively derive reliable HLS kernel implementations. It then generates a C++ validation program to verify functional correctness, diagnoses potential defects, and guides targeted repairs. Finally, it synthesizes host code for compilation and board-level execution on FPGA hardware. We comprehensively evaluate FPGAgent with five established LLMs on HLS-Eval, a benchmark containing 78 tasks across multiple domains, and verify board-level executability on a real FPGA platform. Compared with existing baselines, FPGAgent improves the synthesizable rate by 16.9% on average, executability by 26.7%, and functional correctness by 30.6%. These results show that FPGAgent substantially improves the practical usability of LLM-based HLS generation and demonstrates the value of end-to-end validation.

</details>


### [18] [ToolRobustBench: Stage-Wise Perturbation Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis for Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23635)
*YiShan Zheng,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出ToolRobustBench，一个面向大语言模型工具调用智能体的分阶段鲁棒性诊断基准。该基准不只评估端到端成功率，还定位失败发生于工具选择、模式对齐、参数绑定、工具输出或运行时反馈处理等具体环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用评测主要依据干净环境下的端到端任务成功率，难以区分失败根源，也无法揭示错误在调用链中的传播过程。因此，需要一个与工具调用流水线对应、可控且可归因的鲁棒性基准，以系统评估模型面对接口、意图、返回结果和运行环境扰动时的表现。

Method: ToolRobustBench将扰动设计为四类：工具接口扰动、用户意图扰动、工具输出/观测扰动和运行环境扰动，并将其映射到工具调用流程的不同阶段。基准分别度量工具选择、模式 grounding、参数绑定、工具输出/运行时反馈处理及端到端任务成功。实验覆盖15,456个单一扰动族实例、7个模型、16个本地采样工具、4类扰动及14种子类型；同时开展混合扰动族实验，以考察多源扰动的级联与交互效应。

Result: 各模型在干净设置下整体性能较高，但不同能力环节的表现并不均衡；加入扰动后，鲁棒性显著下降。其中，工具输出/观测扰动是最主要的性能瓶颈。混合扰动实验还发现，组合条件下的失败模式具有非加性：仅根据各单一扰动的结果，无法准确预测多扰动场景中的表现。

Conclusion: ToolRobustBench提供了确定性、分阶段且能够刻画错误级联的工具调用鲁棒性评测框架。其核心价值在于超越单一的干净端到端准确率，支持对工具调用智能体失效位置、失效原因及多类扰动交互影响进行细粒度诊断。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on tool calling as a fundamental agent capability, enabling them to invoke external systems and complete tasks beyond text generation. However, clean end-to-end (E2E) success cannot identify where a tool-use failure originates or how it propagates through a call. We introduce ToolRobustBench, a stage-wise diagnostic benchmark for tool-calling agents, where a tool-calling agent is an LLM system that selects a tool, supplies structured arguments, and interprets its returned feedback. ToolRobustBench aligns four perturbation families with the tool-use pipeline: tool-interface, user-intent, tool-output/observation, and runtime-environment perturbations. It attributes failures to tool selection, schema grounding, argument binding, tool-output/runtime-feedback handling, and E2E task success. Experiments on 15,456 single-family instances across 7 models, 16 sampled local tools, 4 perturbation families, and 14 subtypes show high but non-uniform clean performance and substantial robustness degradation, with tool-output/observation perturbation the dominant bottleneck. Mixed-family experiments reveal non-additive failure patterns that are not explained by isolated single-family results. Thus, ToolRobustBench provides a deterministic and cascade-aware benchmark for diagnosing robustness beyond clean tool-calling accuracy;

</details>


### [19] [Feedback That Backfires: Why Small Language Model Agents Repeat the Call They Just Watched Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23651)
*Esmail Gumaan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文发现：在智能体执行工具调用失败后，若运行框架将“刚刚失败的原始调用文本”连同错误信息原样写入上下文，指令微调模型反而更倾向逐字重复该失败调用。主因是失败调用字符串本身在上下文中的表面形式诱发续写，而非模型未能理解错误语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架通常把失败工具调用及错误消息保留在对话记录中，并假定错误能为后续决策提供纠正信号。论文检验这一假设是否成立，并识别导致失败调用重复的具体上下文机制，以指导框架层面的修复。

Method: 作者定义“纠正增益”为记录失败后，模型再次生成刚失败动作的对数概率变化。在模拟工具调用与MBPP程序修复两类环境中，测试4个模型家族、6个指令微调检查点（135M至1.7B）。通过失败、成功和中性确认消息的反事实配对，分离原调用文本的表面形式效应与“失败”语义效应；并比较多种缓解策略。

Result: 所有测试模型的纠正增益均为负：按动作token归一化后约为-1.03 nats，意味着每个token重复的赔率约增加2.8倍；90%至100%的单个样本均呈现该趋势。在固定候选集内，重复失败调用的概率从0.06升至0.54；贪婪解码在失败记录后有19%的样本逐token复现调用，而记录前为0%。反事实实验显示，约83%的负面影响来自失败调用原文的表面形式，失败标记的语义贡献较小且跨环境符号不稳定。以运行时生成的失败描述替换原始调用文本，可在不增加token成本的条件下消除76%的效应；在解码器中禁止已失败字符串同样针对该机制。显式加入“不要重复”指令无效；删除失败尝试并从干净上下文重试，反而最容易恢复产生原失败调用的上下文。

Conclusion: 问题主要位于智能体运行框架对失败记录的呈现方式，而非模型无法利用错误消息。有效方案应避免在后续生成上下文中逐字保留失败调用，或直接约束其再次生成；仅增加否定性提示或简单清除失败记录并不能解决问题。研究还表明，该结论可在CPU上端到端复现，且相关工件已公开。

Abstract: Agent harnesses record a failed tool call and its error message in the transcript and ask the model to continue, on the assumption that the error is corrective information. We measure whether it is. Defining the corrective gain of a failure record as the change in log-probability of re-emitting the action that just failed, we find the gain is negative for every instruction-tuned model we tested (6 checkpoints, 135M-1.7B, 4 families) in two environments: simulated tool calling and MBPP program repair. Normalised by action length the effect is about -1.03 nats per action token, a factor of 2.8 in the odds of each token, and holds on 90%-100% of individual items, not only on average. Over a fixed candidate set the probability of repeating the failed call rises from 0.06 to 0.54, and greedy decoding reproduces it token for token on 19% of items after the failure versus 0% before. Counterfactuals pairing the same call with a failure message, a success message, or a neutral acknowledgement separate two effects: the failed call's surface form accounts for 83% of the damage, while the semantic contribution of marking it failed is small and inconsistent in sign across environments. The problem is in the harness, not the model's grasp of error messages, and that predicts which remedies work. Replacing the verbatim call with a runtime-generated description of the failure removes 76% of the inversion at no token cost, and making previously-failed strings unreachable at the decoder acts on the same term. Two plausible remedies do not: an explicit "do not repeat" instruction leaves the measured quantity where it was, and deleting the failed attempt to retry from a clean context, the standard prescription for context contamination, is the worst harness we measured for repetition, because it restores the context that produced the failure. The study runs end to end on a CPU; all artefacts are released.

</details>


### [20] [Beyond Executable Models: The Pufibara Agent Harness and the Modelica Agent Workflow Benchmark for Physical System Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23653)
*Zizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向Modelica物理系统建模的智能体执行框架Pufibara，并构建232任务的Modelica Agent Workflow Benchmark。实验表明，在两种匹配的LLM后端下，Pufibara相较Claude Code取得更高任务通过数，并显著降低报告的逻辑Token消耗与串行运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用代码生成的正确性主要关注语法与可执行性，而物理系统模型还必须满足物理一致性、工程约束及特定场景行为。Modelica模型即使能够编译和仿真，也可能偏离预期物理规律。多轮修改中，智能体还可能遗忘需求，或将旧版本候选模型产生的仿真证据错误用于当前版本判断。因此需要具备持久工程状态、版本化证据关联和明确提交机制的专用智能体框架。

Method: 作者设计Pufibara：持续保存跨修订的工程状态；将执行与仿真证据绑定到生成该证据的具体候选版本；把提交定义为智能体需显式执行的动作。评测方面，提出基于源材料构造真实且可独立评价任务的方法，并据此建立包含模型修复、模型生成和模型调参三类任务的232项基准。每个提交候选均由位于智能体循环外、归基准所有的评估器评分。作者在DeepSeek v4 Flash和Claude Sonnet 5两个匹配后端下，将Pufibara与Claude Code作为完整执行框架进行比较。

Result: 在DeepSeek v4 Flash后端，Pufibara通过202个任务，Claude Code通过185个；在Claude Sonnet 5后端，Pufibara同样通过202个任务，Claude Code通过187个。按仓库报告的Token统计口径，Pufibara的逻辑Token总量低76.4%至82.5%；其串行运行时间低6.1%至58.4%。结果显示，控制LLM后端后，智能体框架设计本身仍会显著影响物理建模任务的成功率和资源效率。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将物理建模智能体的可靠性问题归因于工程状态管理、候选版本与证据的可追溯绑定，以及提交语义，并通过端到端基准验证这些机制的价值。实验支持Pufibara在所测Modelica工作流上的效率与成功率优势，但Token节省结论依赖于仓库报告的统计口径；其外部泛化性仍需在更多模型语言、评估器和任务分布上进一步验证。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for simulation-driven engineering. Physical system modeling presents different requirements from general-purpose code generation in software engineering, because correctness depends not only on syntax and executability but also on physical consistency and scenario-dependent behavior. We study this challenge in Modelica, an equation-based modeling language in which a model may compile and simulate while still violating its intended physics or engineering requirements. Across successive revisions, an agent may lose track of requirements or rely on simulation evidence produced by an outdated candidate.
  To address this challenge, we present Pufibara, an agent harness that maintains persistent engineering state across revisions, associates execution and simulation evidence with the candidate that produced it, and makes submission an explicit agent action. To evaluate end-to-end Modelica agent workflows, we also propose a source-grounded method for constructing realistic and independently evaluable tasks. We use this method to build the 232-task Modelica Agent Workflow Benchmark, spanning Model Repair, Model Generation, and Model Tuning. Each submitted candidate is scored by a benchmark-owned evaluator outside the agent loop.
  We compare Pufibara with Claude Code as complete harnesses under two matched large language model (LLM) backends. With DeepSeek v4 Flash, Pufibara passes 202 tasks, compared with 185 for Claude Code. With Claude Sonnet 5, Pufibara passes 202 tasks, compared with 187 for Claude Code. Under the repository-reported token accounting, Pufibara records 76.4%-82.5% lower logical-token totals. Its sequential runtime is 6.1%-58.4% lower. These findings show that, even under matched LLM backends, complete agent harnesses can differ substantially in both task success and resource use for physical system modeling.

</details>


### [21] [Confidently Wrong, Silently So: Auditing Undetectable Failures of a Deployed On-Device Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23663)
*Shashwat Pandey,Satwik Pandey,Suresh Raghu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文对一款开发者可访问的端侧基础语言模型进行可复现实测，发现其在不同任务上存在方向相反且严重的校准失效：既会高置信度编造，也会过度拒答正常请求；仅凭单次生成的用户可见表面特征，几乎无法判断回答是否可信。作者提出无需模型内部访问权限的一致性包装器，可显著改善可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧模型被大规模部署且通常缺乏服务端审核，开发者实际可部署的配置也少有独立审计。因此，关键问题不仅是模型是否会出错，更是普通用户或资源受限开发者能否在推理时识别其错误并据此进行监督。

Method: 作者以模型无关的审计协议开展测试，覆盖三类风险：置信度校准、针对错误前提问题的高置信编造，以及对无害提示的过度拒答。其后评估自报置信度的区分能力，利用15项用户可见输出特征训练分类器检验“正确高置信回答”与“错误高置信回答”的表面可分性，并比较多种低成本单次生成信号。最后测试一个仅通过黑盒重复调用和回答一致性构建的包装器。

Result: 该模型对错误前提的编造比例为69%，对完全无害输入的拒答比例为18%，表明保护机制在不同任务中呈不对称失准。自报置信度近乎饱和且无法区分对错，AUROC为0.47、ECE为70，并且在可比小模型中最差。基于15项可见特征的分类器仅达到AUROC 0.55，且通过等价性检验，说明高置信正确与高置信错误输出在表面上基本不可区分。所有低成本单次生成信号的AUROC均不高于0.68。相反，黑盒一致性包装器将高置信编造率从75%降至3%，并将选择性准确率从43%提升至83%，代价可调。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：对该端侧模型而言，单次输出及其表面特征不足以支持可靠的实时监督，开发者不能将模型自报置信度或文本风格视为可信依据。黑盒一致性检验提供了无需参数、日志或内部访问的有效缓解路径。作者同时提供审计协议、表面不可区分性测试、代码和冻结评测项，为已部署模型的独立可靠性审计建立了可复用基础设施。

Abstract: Aligning deployed language models requires knowing when their outputs can be trusted, yet on-device models now ship to hundreds of millions of devices with no server-side moderation, and the configuration developers can actually deploy is rarely audited independently. We present a reproducible reliability audit of the developer-accessible on-device foundation model, framed as an oversight question: can a user or a resource-constrained developer tell when the model is wrong? Red-teaming it on calibration, confident confabulation on false-premise questions, and over-refusal of benign prompts, we find a \emph{task-asymmetric miscalibration}: its guardrails fail in opposite directions across tasks (confabulating on 69\% of false premises while refusing 18\% of entirely benign inputs), atop a self-reported confidence that is saturated and non-discriminative (AUROC 0.47; ECE 70, worst among comparable small models). Crucially, confident-correct and confident-wrong outputs are \emph{surface-indistinguishable}: a classifier over 15 user-visible features separates them at AUROC only 0.55 (equivalence-confirmed), leaving no signal for oversight at inference time. No cheap single-generation signal flags these failures ($\le$0.68 AUROC), whereas a black-box consistency wrapper requiring no model access recovers reliability (confident confabulation 75\%$\to$3\%; selective accuracy 43\%$\to$83\%) at a tunable cost. We contribute a model-agnostic audit protocol, a surface-indistinguishability test, and released code and frozen evaluation items as reusable infrastructure for auditing deployed models.

</details>


### [22] [Enhancing Bug Report Templates in the TianoCore UEFI Firmware Development Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23754)
*Laura Baird,Neelesh Reddybattula,Nazanin Siavash,Terrance E. Boult,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出改进TianoCore社区在GitHub Issues中使用的缺陷报告模板，通过新增结构化字段补足自由文本中经常缺失、但对缺陷分诊与修复有价值的信息。研究以EDK II项目为对象，目前处于进行中阶段，尚未报告正式的量化验证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由格式的缺陷报告往往遗漏关键上下文，导致开发者需要额外沟通、延缓问题分类和定位。作者通过分析历史缺陷库发现，部分对分诊和修复有效的信息只会偶发地出现在报告正文中，因此需要通过模板机制提升信息完整性与一致性。

Method: 研究首先挖掘TianoCore缺陷仓库数据，识别有助于分诊和修复的信息模式；随后据此在现有报告模板基础上设计若干新增字段。研究聚焦EDK II，并已通过少量开发者访谈收集反馈、迭代方案；后续计划扩大社区访谈，并采用A/B测试评估新旧模板的效果。

Result: 当前结果主要是形成了基于历史数据分析的模板增强方案，并获得了初步开发者反馈。摘要未给出新增字段的具体内容，也未提供缺陷处理时长、信息完整度、重开率或修复成功率等定量指标，因此尚不能证明该方案的实际收益。

Conclusion: 该工作将缺陷报告质量问题转化为可操作的模板设计问题，具有明确的工程实践价值。其核心贡献仍属初步提案；最终有效性取决于后续A/B测试能否证明新增字段在不显著增加提交负担的前提下，提高分诊效率、减少沟通轮次并加速缺陷修复。

Abstract: We propose enhancing the bug report templates in the GitHub Issues issue tracking system used by the TianoCore open-source community with the aim of improving the bug triage and resolution process. We analyze the bug repository data and find patterns of information that are useful for bug triage and fixing. However, some of them are only occasionally included in the free-form text of bug reports. Therefore, we propose adding a few new fields to the existing TianoCore bug report template. In this study, we focus on the key TianoCore project, EDK II, which constitutes the core of the UEFI firmware across various firmware vendors and original equipment manufacturers. This study is currently a work-in-progress. So far, we have interviewed a few developers to obtain their feedback and adjust the proposed approach. We are planning more interviews with the TianoCore community to conduct A/B tests and validate our approach to achieve effective and efficient bug triage and resolution.

</details>


### [23] [SPIDER4TianoCore: Enhancing Patch-Propagation for the TianoCore UEFI Firmware Development Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23755)
*Laura Baird,Devin Haggitt,Terrance E. Boult,Aravind Machiry,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出SPIDER4TianoCore：面向TianoCore/UEFI固件供应链的Python命令行工具，用于在集成阶段为补丁状态提供可复核证据，而非证明补丁可安全传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下游固件仓库对上游补丁的采纳状态难以统一追踪。维护者需要基于上游补丁前后版本及下游目标，快速获得补丁是否缺失、已存在、无关或证据不足的判断。

Method: 工具输入上游补丁前版本、后版本和预先准备的下游目标，通过分析器生成支持性证据，并将目标分类为Vulnerable、Already Patched、Not Applicable或Uncertain。评估使用8个公开下游EDK II仓库中的20个预设目标/补丁编号配对，并以人工记录的补丁状态标签为参照。

Result: 在20个配对中，系统得到10个高置信度补丁前匹配、4个高置信度补丁后匹配；对6个目标保守地给出不确定结论；相对于记录的人工标签，没有出现高置信度错误分类。

Conclusion: SPIDER4TianoCore展示了对预设下游目标可重复生成补丁状态证据的能力，适合辅助维护者审查集成状态。实验规模有限，结果仅支持其在准备好的目标上的证据生成有效性，不能据此推断其对一般下游仓库的整体准确率或补丁传播安全性。

Abstract: We propose and demonstrate SPIDER4TianoCore, a packaged Python command-line tool that provides integration-stage patch-status evidence for the TianoCore/UEFI firmware supply chain. Given an upstream pre-patch and post-patch pair and prepared downstream targets, the tool reports Vulnerable, Already Patched, Not Applicable, or Uncertain with supporting evidence for maintainer review. Our work is inspired by SPIDER's patch-propagation framing, but SPIDER4TianoCore does not itself prove that a patch is safe to propagate. We evaluate the engine on 20 prepared target/CVE pairs from eight public downstream EDK II repositories and two CVEs. The analyzers produce 10 high-confidence pre-patch matches and four high-confidence post-patch matches, conservatively abstain on six targets, and make no confidently wrong classifications relative to the recorded manual patch-state labels. These preliminary results demonstrate reproducible evidence generation for prepared targets rather than general downstream accuracy.

</details>


### [24] [Scale, Concentration, and Entry Timing in the Shopify App Ecosystem: A Longitudinal Study of Platform Governance and Application Survival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23771)
*Fabrizio Assabese,Peter-Jan Celis,Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文基于Shopify应用市场的全量快照、长期周度面板及历史页面重建数据，系统刻画市场集中度、进入者优势、平台治理冲击和应用退出规律，发现该市场总体具有较强可竞争性，但竞争结果显著受平台治理与进入条件影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shopify拥有庞大的第三方应用生态，但关于其市场结构、应用采纳机制、先发与后发优势，以及平台规则变化如何影响互补者竞争，缺少大规模、长期的实证证据。

Method: 研究使用2025年9月24,826个应用的横截面数据、2019年2月至2026年3月间7,708个应用的366周面板数据，以及通过Internet Archive重建的上架历史。作者在55个功能类别中测量集中度，按季度估计不同进入批次的增长差异，并将平台进入聊天类别、下架Product Reviews应用和调整平台分成作为准实验式治理事件；同时以应用上线前六个月的公开特征预测两年内退出。

Result: 超过一半功能类别呈低集中度，20%呈高集中度，且类别规模越大通常越分散。50个可分析类别中，88%的后进入者增长快于早进入者；早进入者净损失可检测安装量，而后进入者获得增长。平台进入聊天类别逆转了此前两年多的去集中化趋势；Product Reviews退出释放约19.1万安装量，但一年内最多仅三分之一转化为竞争应用的新增采纳；2021年平台分成下调未显著改变进入或留存。退出通常早期且低调：退出应用的安装量峰值中位数为8，上线后68周内即停止被追踪；七年内92%的类别发生领导者更替。仅凭上线前六个月可观测数据，预测两年内退出的交叉验证AUC超过0.8。

Conclusion: Shopify应用市场并非由稳定先发者长期主导，而是保持较高流动性与可竞争性。后进入者普遍具有增长优势，应用失败可被较早预测；但平台自身的进入与退出能够显著且不对称地重塑竞争格局，相较单纯的进入时点，平台治理和进入条件更决定参与者能否受益。

Abstract: The Shopify marketplace hosts more than 16,000 active third-party applications, serving 2.7 million active merchant stores generating an estimated \$706 billion in annual sales, yet little empirical evidence exists on its structure and adoption drivers. We analyse it using a September 2025 snapshot of all 24,826 applications, a weekly panel of 7,708 applications over 366 weeks (February 2019 to March 2026), and listing histories reconstructed from the Internet Archive. Across 55 established functional categories, over half were low-concentration and 20\% highly concentrated, with larger categories consistently less concentrated. Later entrants grew faster than early movers in 88\% of the 50 analysed categories, persisting across seven years of quarterly re-estimations, with early movers on net losing detected installations whilst late movers gained them. Platform governance reshaped competition asymmetrically: Shopify's entry into chat reversed more than two years of de-concentration; the deprecation of its Product Reviews application, to our knowledge the first measured platform-owner exit from complementor category, released 191,000 installations of which at most a third reappeared as competitor adoption within a year; the 2021 reduction of the platform revenue share produced no detectable change in entry or retention. Failure is largely silent and predictable: the median exiting application peaked at 8 detected installations and disappeared from tracking within 68 weeks of launch, category leadership changed hands in 92\% of categories over seven years, and publicly observable data from an application's first six months predict exit within two years with cross-validated AUC above 0.8. App markets on Shopify remain contestable; who benefits depends on platform governance and entry conditions more than on entry timing.

</details>


### [25] [Automated Synthesis of Cloud Emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23842)
*Archit Bhatnagar,Zhenning Yang,Sarah McClure,Yiming Qiu,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Ang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出CloudEmu：一种通过神经符号代码合成、从云服务文档自动构建云模拟器的方法，用于在本地执行和测试DevOps程序。实验表明，其在AWS和GCP服务的覆盖度与行为准确性上优于人工长期开发的LocalStack。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DevOps脚本、CLI/API调用及基础设施即代码的测试通常需要真实云资源，因而存在耗时、成本高和环境风险等问题。现有云模拟器虽能缓解这些问题，但其实现依赖开发者逐项阅读文档并手写服务、接口及交互逻辑；面对不断扩展和演化的云服务接口，这种方式难以规模化维护。

Method: CloudEmu以云服务文档为输入，采用神经符号代码合成自动生成模拟器。系统结合大语言模型对文档语义理解与代码生成的能力，以及云领域符号化抽象提供的约束，以降低生成过程中的幻觉并保证接口行为精确性。随后，方法将真实云平台作为行为预言机，对生成的模拟器进行自动化测试、修复与对齐，使本地模拟结果更接近真实服务。

Result: 评估覆盖AWS和GCP的主要服务，结果显示CloudEmu能够有效生成具有较高接口覆盖度和行为准确性的云模拟器。相较于领先的现有工具LocalStack，CloudEmu在实验指标上表现更优，说明自动化合成能够达到或超过长期人工实现的模拟能力。

Conclusion: CloudEmu证明了以云文档驱动、结合大语言模型与符号约束，并通过真实云行为校准的自动化模拟器构建路线具有可行性。该方法有望降低云模拟器开发与演进维护成本，并提升DevOps程序本地测试的效率、可扩展性和可信度。

Abstract: DevOps programming (e.g., using CLI/API scripts or IaC frameworks) is key to cloud infrastructure management. Unlike traditional programming tasks, DevOps program testing needs provisioning and execution against actual cloud resources, which is often time-consuming, unsafe, and costly. Cloud emulators have gained popularity for easing DevOps program testing; they are generally API-level mocks that can execute DevOps programs in a local environment. Still, building these emulators remains challenging: developers must manually interpret extensive cloud documentation and handcraft logic for each service, API, and their interaction. This does not scale to the complexity of the cloud, which is further a moving target as the services and APIs evolve. CloudEmu is an automated approach that constructs emulators based on cloud documentation via neurosymbolic code synthesis. The key idea is to combine LLMs' general strengths in documentation understanding and code generation with cloud-specific symbolic abstractions that suppress hallucinations and enforce precision at scale, while using the real cloud as an oracle for automated testing, repair, and alignment. Our evaluation shows the effectiveness of CloudEmu on major cloud provider (AWS and GCP) services in both coverage and accuracy. CloudEmu outperforms the existing leading tool LocalStack, which was manually developed by a large team of engineers over a decade.

</details>


### [26] [The Empire, Long Divided, Must Unite: Architectural Convergence in Three LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23953)
*Dai Jiahong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究语言模型智能体的“harness”（负责上下文构建、工具调度、循环执行与状态持久化的外围运行层），通过比较三套开源编程智能体框架，提出成熟系统虽演化路径相反，仍收敛到由五项要素构成的共同架构；同时指出“外部可验证性”普遍缺失，可能成为后续面向溯源敏感场景的关键分化维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为，智能体行为的主要约束正从底层语言模型转移至harness设计。现有讨论较少以源码和演化历史为依据，系统解释不同工程哲学的harness为何及如何形成相似结构，也未充分关注运行记录能否被独立第三方验证的问题。

Method: 采用源码级多案例研究：在固定提交版本上细读LangChain deepagents、Earendil pi与DeepSeek dsh，并追踪前两者的提交历史。研究将deepagents视为“内置功能完整”、pi视为“极简主义”、dsh视为“插件化”的对照案例；先从前两者归纳模式，再以dsh作为保留案例检验，并区分共同特征来自平行发现、设计扩散或直接代码复用。

Result: 研究发现，deepagents倾向减少人工编写的脚手架，pi则持续增加持久化基础设施，二者最终都呈现五项重复出现的结构：标准化或可替换的执行循环、仅追加且可重放的会话记录、将模型特异性表示为数据、按需渐进披露上下文，以及明确的扩展接口。作为后验检验的dsh同样具备这五项特征，且至少一个扩展接口直接复用了其他项目的实现。三者均未提供可由外部主体在不信任运行时的前提下检验的防篡改记录。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将harness架构收敛解释为混合过程，而非简单宣称独立创新：部分是相似约束下的平行发现，部分是思想扩散，部分是直接复用。作者据此提出，未来harness的竞争重点可能不再只是循环、记忆和插件机制，而是外部可验证、可审计且具防篡改性质的执行与溯源记录。

Abstract: An agent harness is what turns a language model into an autonomous agent: the surrounding code that builds the model's context, mediates its tools, runs the loop, and persists state across a long-horizon run. This layer, not the model it wraps, is increasingly the binding constraint on agent behaviour. We present a source-level, multi-case study of three open coding-agent harnesses built from deliberately opposing philosophies: LangChain's deepagents (batteries-included), Earendil's pi (radical minimalism), and DeepSeek's dsh (everything-is-a-plugin). Reading each at a pinned commit and following its commit history, we find that the two mature harnesses have travelled in opposite directions (deepagents subtracting authored scaffolding, pi accreting durable infrastructure), yet converged toward one architectural middle form of five recurring elements: a commoditised loop, an append-only replayable session record, model quirks kept as data, progressive disclosure of context, and explicit extension seams. A third harness, read afterward as a held-out check, exhibits all five, and in one seam reuses another's implementation outright. We therefore do not claim independent invention, and decompose the convergence into parallel discovery, diffusion, and literal reuse. Finally, one load-bearing dimension shows no convergence, and indeed no presence: external verifiability, a tamper-evident record an outside party can check without trusting the runtime. We read this absence not as an oversight but as a predictive gap, the next axis on which harnesses for provenance-sensitive domains will differ.

</details>


### [27] [Evaluating Language Models on Cross-Language Code Functional Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23961)
*Hui Sun,Anderson Uchôa,Rohit Gheyi,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出PolyHuman数据集，评估大语言模型对C++、Java和Python人写程序的函数等价性判断能力。结果显示，当前模型在同语言及跨语言场景中均无法稳定、可靠地识别功能等价，尤其容易将实际不等价的代码误判为等价代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有代码理解评测多局限于单一语言或合成代码，难以证明模型真正掌握了程序语义。跨语言的人写代码等价性判断要求模型忽略表层语法与实现差异，准确推理输入、控制流、边界条件和输出行为，因此可作为检验语义理解能力的更严格测试。

Method: 作者构建PolyHuman数据集，涵盖C++、Java和Python的人写程序；在开源权重模型和闭源模型上测试同语言与跨语言函数等价性检测。以GPT-o4-mini作为代表模型评估重复运行稳定性，并人工检查81个模型系统性误判为等价的案例，结合代码逻辑及模型生成的推理过程归纳失效类型；最后将相关失败与Claude-Opus-4.7、Gemini-3-Flash进行对比，以区分模型特有问题与普遍局限。

Result: 模型表现呈现明显的难度退化：题目越难，越倾向于把不等价程序误判为等价。最佳模型对编程语言存在敏感性，在Python上表现得更保守。错误分析还表明，模型部分依赖代码相似性等表层线索，而非完整语义验证。GPT-o4-mini在相同设置下多次运行的结果存在较大波动，说明其能力并非完全缺失，但缺乏一致性和可复现性。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型尚未形成可靠的、可稳定迁移到跨语言场景的函数等价性判断能力。高分结果不能直接等同于扎实的程序语义理解；未来评测应更多采用真实人写、多语言、包含困难边界条件的代码，并同时报告错误类型与运行稳定性。

Abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a variety of code-understanding tasks, leading many to believe that they can reason about program semantics. However, existing evaluations primarily focus on single-language settings or rely on synthetically generated code, raising concerns about whether current results reflect true semantic understanding. Aims: We investigate whether LLMs can accurately judge functional equivalence across different programming languages in human-written code, a setting that requires deeper reasoning beyond superficial similarity. Method: We introduce PolyHuman, a dataset of human-written programs in CPP, Java, and Python. Using this dataset, we evaluate intra- and inter-language equivalence detection across open-weight and proprietary LLMs, selecting GPT-o4-mini as a representative model to assess stability. We then manually analyze 81 cases of systematic disagreement in which models incorrectly judge functional equivalence, examining the code logic and the generated Chain-of-Thought reasoning. Finally, we categorize these failures and compare them across GPT-o4-mini, Claude-Opus-4.7, and Gemini-3-Flash to determine whether they reflect model-specific issues or broader limitations of state-of-the-art LLMs. Results: We identify a difficulty-dependent breakdown in equivalence judgment (harder problems make the model increasingly prone to misclassifying non-equivalent code as equivalent), a model-specific sensitivity to programming language for the best-performing model (particularly a more conservative behavior on Python), and a partial reliance on similarity-based cues. GPT-o4-mini also shows substantial run-to-run instability under identical settings, indicating inconsistent rather than absent capability. Conclusions: Current LLMs do not reliably capture functional equivalence within or across languages.

</details>


### [28] [Mutation Testing of Simulink Cyber-Physical System Models: Challenges and Solutions in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24250)
*Murat Kavak,Onur Kilincceker,Serge Demeyer,Kevin Vandenbroucke,Halim Abdurrahman Ceylan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文报告了在DUCO的基于Simulink和Stateflow的模型驱动开发流程中试点引入变异测试工具MUT4SLX的经验，表明变异测试可支持面向早期设计模型的左移测试，但实际落地存在若干关键障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于缺陷在开发后期才被发现会造成高成本的系统，测试应尽早覆盖用于仿真、原型验证和可行性分析的设计模型。尽管企业已具备模型单元测试实践，仍需评估变异测试能否有效衡量并提升这些测试的缺陷检测能力。

Method: 作者在DUCO开展MUT4SLX工具原型的试点项目，结合该公司长期使用Simulink和Stateflow进行模型驱动工程及模型单元测试的实践，记录变异测试集成过程中的问题与经验。

Result: 试点确认变异测试能够为左移测试提供支持，同时识别出三类尤为突出的挑战：等价变异体难以自动判定、需求与模型及测试之间的可追踪性不足、面向Stateflow模型的变异测试具有额外复杂性。作者指出，后两类或相关具体问题此前尚未在学术文献中得到充分报道。

Conclusion: 在工业模型驱动工程中，变异测试具有提升早期模型测试质量的应用潜力；但其规模化采用依赖于对等价变异体处理、需求追踪机制和Stateflow专用变异策略的进一步研究与工具支持。

Abstract: Several experience reports illustrate that mutation testing is capable of supporting a shift-left testing strategy, especially in industries where late bug discovery incurs very high costs and risks. In the context of cyber-physical systems, a shift left implies that engineers need to test the design models used to simulate, prototype, and analyse the feasibility of the system under design. In this paper, we analyse the challenges we encountered and the lessons we learned when incorporating mutation testing in the context of DUCO, a company producing ventilation systems for buildings. The engineers within DUCO have years of experience with model-based engineering centered around Simulink and StateFlow, including unit tests for their models. During a pilot project with our tool prototype MUT4SLX we learned that equivalent mutants, requirement traceability, and mutation testing for Stateflow represent particular challenges not yet reported in the academic literature.

</details>


### [29] [Cross-Stack Validation of Language-Model Training: A Clinical Fine-Tuning Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24267)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出将独立实现的端到端训练栈作为微调流程的差分测试预言机，并在PyTorch与Zig编写的numbat之间验证LoRA微调的一致性。结果显示，跨栈训练轨迹可高度接近，但数据文本渲染与语言运行时接口等非数值因素往往比算子算术误差更影响最终模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络训练存在“预言机问题”：训练可表面正常收敛，却可能偏离既定语义；而单一软件栈缺少独立参照，难以及时发现此类错误。既有差分测试多聚焦算子或推理路径，本文旨在检验独立训练栈能否为完整微调管线提供端到端的差分预言机。

Method: 作者定义轨迹级比较协议：建立共享规格，并在数值计算、模型加载、数据渲染及学习轨迹等环节设置交叉检查点；同时将训练栈、编排层和语言运行时的独立性分开考察。实验针对Qwen3-0.6B的LoRA微调，在168,574条临床问答样本上比较PyTorch与独立实现的numbat；numbat既原生运行，也经C接口由六种语言驱动。

Result: 在覆盖完整一个训练轮次的42组配对评估中，两栈验证集交叉熵平均差异为0.134%，其中四种实现的最终结果彼此相差不超过0.15%。比较共发现17个单实现开发未发现的故障。影响最大的故障并非数值内核，而是临床文本渲染方式不一致，导致验证损失变化0.15，约为相邻算术故障影响的500倍。另有4个故障仅在内存模型不同的语言环境中可达，涉及线程迁移调度、未识别设备内存的回收机制，以及接口缺少所有权管理所需原语等问题。

Conclusion: 独立训练栈可作为完整微调流程有效的差分预言机，但验证对象不应局限于算术结果或模型算子。实现多样性至少覆盖训练框架、管线编排和语言运行时；尤其应优先控制数据表示、文本渲染、内存与线程语义等跨层行为，因为它们可能远大于数值误差地影响训练结果。

Abstract: Neural network training has an oracle problem: a run can converge normally and yield a usable model while the software beneath it computes something other than specified. Almost all such work runs on one stack, so there is rarely anything independent to check against. We study whether independently implemented training stacks can serve as differential oracles for a whole fine-tuning pipeline, rather than the operators and inference paths that prior differential testing targets. We define a trajectory-level protocol -- a shared specification, cross-check points spanning arithmetic, model loading, data rendering and the learning trajectory, and a separation of independence of the stack, the orchestration and the language runtime -- and apply it to a LoRA adaptation of Qwen3-0.6B over 168,574 clinical question-answer pairs under PyTorch and under numbat, an independent framework written in Zig, driven natively and through its C interface from six languages. Across 42 paired evaluations spanning a full epoch the two stacks' held-out cross-entropy differs by 0.134% on average, and four implementations end the epoch within 0.15% of one another. The comparison exposed 17 faults that single-implementation development had missed, two of them notable for software engineering. The fault with the largest effect on the trained model lay outside the numerical kernels: a mismatch in how clinical text was rendered moved held-out loss 0.15, some 500 times more than the arithmetic faults found beside it. And four faults were reachable only from a language whose memory model differs from the first two implementations: a scheduler migrating work across threads, a collector blind to device memory, an ownership discipline needing a primitive the interface lacked. Implementation diversity has several axes, and the runtime is one.

</details>


### [30] [Towards LLM-Enhanced Android Taint Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24269)
*Nicholas Miazzo,Marco Alecci,Jordan Samhi,Jacques Klein,Eleonora Losiouk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文探索以智能体式大语言模型推理替代或补充传统Android静态污点分析。初步实验显示，该方法在DroidBench上的F1值显著高于FlowDroid，尤其改善了跨组件通信、隐式流与反射等困难场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android框架复杂，传统静态分析工具难以完整、精确地建模生命周期、组件交互及动态机制，因而容易遗漏污点传播路径。论文旨在检验现成大语言模型是否能通过代码理解与迭代推理缓解这些局限。

Method: 方法采用智能体式交互策略：让大语言模型迭代浏览应用代码、定位可能的数据源与汇点，并逐步推理变量传播、组件间传递及相关控制逻辑。评估上，将该方法与FlowDroid在DroidBench基准上比较，并在少量真实应用中检查额外发现。

Result: Gemini-3 Flash在DroidBench上取得0.96的F1值，优于FlowDroid的0.55。在跨组件通信类别中为0.95对0.17，隐式流为0.94对0.00，反射为1.00对0.50。真实应用实验中，LLM方法还报告了FlowDroid未发现的额外潜在数据泄露。

Conclusion: 结果表明，大语言模型的代码推理能力有潜力补足传统静态污点分析在复杂Android语义与动态特征建模上的不足。但研究仍属初步验证，需进一步在更大规模真实应用、可复现提示与代理配置、误报控制、运行成本及与静态分析工具协同的混合管线方面进行系统评估。

Abstract: Taint analysis is a fundamental technique for detecting sensitive data leaks in Android apps. However, traditional static tools, such as FlowDroid, still face well-known challenges due to the complexity of accurately modeling the Android framework. In this paper, we investigate whether off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can effectively reason about taint flows in Android apps. Our preliminary approach relies on an agentic interaction strategy, enabling the LLM to iteratively explore code and reason about data flows.
  We conduct an initial evaluation on the DroidBench benchmark against FlowDroid, where our approach outperforms the baseline: Gemini-3 Flash achieves an F1-score of 0.96, compared to 0.55 for FlowDroid. In particular, we observe improvements in challenging categories such as inter-component communication (0.95 vs. 0.17), implicit flows (0.94 vs. 0.00), and reflection (1.00 vs. 0.50), where FlowDroid typically struggles. On a small set of real-world apps, the LLM-based approach also identifies additional potential data leaks not reported by FlowDroid. These preliminary findings suggest that LLM reasoning may effectively complement traditional static taint analysis, motivating future research on hybrid LLM-enhanced taint analysis pipelines.

</details>


### [31] [Observability and Fault Injection for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24271)
*Zahra Seyedghorban,Egor Klimov,Arie van Deursen,Annibale Panichella,Burcu Kulahcioglu Ozkan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出llmmas-otel：一个面向软件工程中基于大语言模型的多智能体系统的轻量级、框架无关工具。该工具将OpenTelemetry分布式追踪与可控故障注入结合，用于提升系统执行过程的可观测性、可调试性及可重复评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型多智能体系统已被用于软件工程任务，但其执行涉及多阶段工作流、多个智能体协作、工具调用和模型推理，因而内部行为难以检查与定位问题。现有方法也难以在可控条件下复现异常并比较其影响，因此需要一种统一机制记录端到端执行轨迹，并在指定交互位置注入故障。

Method: llmmas-otel以OpenTelemetry为基础，对工作流阶段、智能体步骤、智能体间通信、工具调用和LLM调用进行与追踪链路对齐的遥测埋点。工具支持在选定交互点实施定向故障注入，并保存运行产物；随后通过对齐的追踪数据和产物，对正常基线运行与注入故障后的运行进行可重复比较。其设计强调轻量化和框架无关性，以便接入不同的多智能体实现。

Result: 作者完成了工具的架构设计与实现，并说明其当前能力。初步验证覆盖最小化演示工作流，以及一个真实的软件开发多智能体系统。摘要表明，该工具能够记录跨层级执行信息、支持目标化故障注入，并帮助检查故障对工作流及各交互环节的影响；但未报告定量性能开销、故障覆盖范围或与其他可观测性方案的系统比较。

Conclusion: llmmas-otel为LLM多智能体软件工程系统提供了追踪与故障注入一体化的实验基础设施，使研究者和工程人员能够以更可控、可复现的方式观察、调试和评估系统。其主要贡献在于将标准化分布式遥测扩展至多智能体执行语义，并支持基线—故障执行的对齐分析；后续仍需通过更大规模任务、更多框架和量化指标验证通用性与实际成本。

Abstract: Large Language Model-based multi-agent systems are increasingly explored for software engineering tasks, but they remain difficult to inspect, debug, and evaluate under controlled failures. We present llmmas-otel, a lightweight and framework-agnostic tool that combines OpenTelemetry-based distributed tracing with fault injection for LLM-based multi-agent systems in software engineering workflows. The tool instruments agent executions with trace-aligned telemetry across workflow phases, agent steps, inter-agent communication, tool calls, and LLM invocations, and supports targeted fault injection at selected interaction points. This makes it possible to compare baseline and faulty executions in a reproducible way and inspect the effects through aligned traces and run artifacts. We describe the motivation, architecture, implementation, current capabilities, and initial validation of the tool on a minimal demo workflow and a real LLM-based multi-agent system for software development.

</details>


### [32] [Ockhamareto: Pareto-Gated Segment-Level Credit Assignment for Concise Unit-Test Generation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24473)
*Dong Huang,Mark Harman,Jie M. Zhang,Zhijiang Guo,Mingzhe Du,See Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Ockhamareto 是一种面向单元测试生成与筛选的单次 GRPO 框架，通过帕累托约束和细粒度信用分配，同时提升测试的缺陷检测能力与测试套件紧凑性。摘要报告其在 ULT 及四个基准上均优于最强强化学习基线 MIST-RL，并在不同模型规模下保持显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试生成方法通常难以兼顾有效性与效率：生成更多测试可能提高变异检测率，却会增加执行和维护成本。论文旨在直接优化“更多变异击杀”与“更少测试数量”之间的工程权衡，并避免仅依赖函数规模等易计算代理指标来决定测试预算。

Method: 方法包含两个核心设计：（1）Pareto-gated Bonus：仅奖励在“变异得分、负测试数”二维空间中不被其他候选支配的生成轨迹，使训练目标遵循奥卡姆剃刀原则与帕累托最优性；（2）Token-level Segment Credit：将每个测试带来的边际变异击杀归因到该测试块对应的 token，从而提供更精确的局部训练信号。推理或选择阶段通过计算每个被测函数的帕累托前沿，识别效率与有效性的折中点。

Result: 在 ULT 上，N=5 时 Ockhamareto 的变异得分为 49.9%，高于 MIST-RL 的 31.3%；平均测试数量为 2.60，少于 MIST-RL 的 4.67。因此其单位测试的权衡效率提升约 3.4 倍。方法在 HumanEval+、MBPP+、CodeContests 和 TestGenEval-Lite 四个基准中均同时领先变异与覆盖指标，且测试套件始终最小。在 4B、9B、27B 模型规模下，相对现有最佳方法均额外获得约 30—35 个百分点的变异得分提升。

Conclusion: 论文表明，将帕累托筛选式奖励与测试块级 token 信用分配结合，可有效学习生成更少但更有价值的单元测试。其核心贡献不仅是整体性能提升，还指出最优折中点无法由函数大小等简单代理特征可靠预测；因此，按函数显式计算帕累托前沿对于选择合适测试套件规模具有必要性。

Abstract: We introduce \textbf{Ockhamareto}, a single-shot GRPO framework for unit-test generation and selection, based on the principles of \emph{Ockham's Razor} and \emph{Pareto Optimality}. Ockhamareto has two principal components: (i)~a \emph{Pareto-gated Bonus} that rewards only rollouts non-dominated in~(mutation, $-$\#tests) space, and (ii)~\emph{Token-level Segment Credit}, which attributes each test's marginal mutation kills back to the tokens of its unit-test block. On the \emph{UnLeakedTestBench~(ULT)}, Ockhamareto \emph{strictly Pareto-dominates} the strongest RL baseline~(\emph{MIST-RL}). Furthermore, it dominates on {\em each and all} optimization objectives, catching more bugs ($49.9\%$ vs $31.3\%$ mutation score at $N{=}5$), using \emph{fewer} tests ($2.60$ vs $4.67$ on average), thereby achieving $3.4\times$ the per-test trade-off improvement. The advantage is found in all four benchmarks~(\emph{HumanEval+}, \emph{MBPP+}, \emph{CodeContests}, \emph{TestGenEval-Lite}): Ockhamareto leads both mutation and coverage metrics on every one, always with the smallest suite. Ockhamareto also outperforms the state-of-the-art at all model scales, adding $+30$--$35$~pp mutation at 4B, 9B, and 27B model sizes. We also show that the knee point of the optimal trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness on the Pareto front is not correlated with obvious more easily computed proxy metrics, such as function size. This finding motivates the Pareto front computation; it is needed to identify this crucial engineering trade-off for each function under test.

</details>


### [33] [LumiXAI: A Modular Full-Stack Framework for Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24524)
*Alfio Ferrara,Lorenzo Gatta,Sergio Picascia,Elisabetta Rocchetti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出LumiXAI：一个模块化、全栈的特征归因分析框架，将分类与生成式模型的归因分析、交互式图形界面、可扩展插件机制及可复现的持久化工作流整合到统一系统中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性工具较为碎片化，通常仅覆盖单一数据模态、单一交互形式（代码接口或图形界面）或固定的方法集合；同时，许多工具面向具备编程和归因方法知识的专家，非专业用户使用门槛较高。

Method: LumiXAI采用模块化全栈架构：提供支持双向探索的交互式GUI；通过插件机制注册和扩展模型与归因方法；设置面向非编程用户、开发者和扩展者的三级访问方式，并共享统一后端；利用容器化服务和持久化结果保存分析过程与输出。

Result: 该系统在同一接口、交互模型和持久化层中统一了承担既有特征归因方法的执行与分析流程，并同时覆盖分类和生成式归因场景。其架构降低了不同用户群体的使用与扩展门槛，并支持跨机器复现分析结果。

Conclusion: LumiXAI的核心贡献不是提出新的归因算法，而是通过统一、可扩展且具备图形交互能力的平台，缓解可解释性工具生态割裂和使用门槛高的问题，为特征归因分析的部署、探索和复现提供系统化基础设施。

Abstract: Feature attribution is a central tool of model interpretability, yet the software through which it is applied remains fragmented: individual tools specialize along narrow axes, such as a single modality, a code API or a GUI, or a fixed rather than extensible method set, and rarely combine these strengths. Moreover, many explainability tools are designed primarily for domain experts, requiring programming skills or familiarity with attribution methods that can make them difficult for non-expert users to access. In this article, we present LumiXAI, a modular full-stack framework that consolidates attribution analysis into a single system. It couples classification and generative attribution with an interactive GUI supporting bidirectional exploration, a plug-in architecture for registering new models and methods, and three access tiers serving non-programmers, developers, and extenders from one backend. Its contribution is a system that operationalises established attribution methods under one interface, one interaction model, and one persistence layer, with containerised services and persistent results making analyses reproducible across machines.

</details>


### [34] [Quantifying the Relationship Between Team Dysfunctions and Performance in Capstone Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24634)
*Luciano Pereira Soares,Luciana Campos Lima,Rafael Corsi Ferrão*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文考察工程类毕业设计（Capstone）团队中，基于Lencioni五项团队失调模型衡量的团队动态与项目绩效之间的关联。结果显示二者存在弱到中等强度的正向关系：团队行为越健康，项目表现通常越好，但团队动态并非唯一决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程毕业设计不仅用于评估技术知识，也用于检验协作、沟通、设计和组织等职业能力。尽管团队互动被普遍认为重要，其与项目绩效之间的实证关系仍缺乏充分厘清，因此需要量化分析团队失调及其影响。

Method: 研究收集学生团队的问卷数据，采用Lencioni五项团队失调框架量化团队动态。所有题项均进行反向编码，使较高分代表更积极的团队行为和更低的失调程度。每项失调维度由3个对应题项求和构成，得分范围为3至9分；分数越高，表示团队动态越健康。随后分析各维度得分与项目绩效的关系。

Result: 团队动态指标与项目绩效呈弱到中等程度关联，表明更健康的协作模式通常伴随更好的项目表现。论文尤其指出，目标一致性以及对交付成果的专注与绩效相关；但该关联强度有限，意味着技术能力、项目条件及其他情境因素同样可能显著影响结果。

Conclusion: 在工程项目式学习中，团队健康度是影响项目绩效的重要但非充分条件。Capstone项目设计应通过促进目标对齐、强化交付导向和改善团队协作来支持学习成效，同时将团队动态与技术及情境变量结合考量，避免将绩效差异单独归因于团队失调。

Abstract: This paper investigates the association between team dysfunctions and performance in the context of an engineering Capstone program. Capstone programs are considered fundamental in assessing the readiness of engineering and computer science students for professional practice, evaluating not only technical knowledge but also essential competencies such as teamwork, communication, design, and organization. Despite the recognized importance of team dynamics in such settings, their relationship with project performance remains insufficiently understood. Using survey data collected from student teams, we quantify team dynamics based on Lencioni's model of the five dysfunctions of a team, with all measures reverse-coded so that higher values indicate more positive team behaviors (i.e., lower dysfunction). The results indicate a weak-to-moderate relationship between team dynamics and performance. Each dysfunction score was computed as the sum of three corresponding survey items. All items were coded such that higher values indicate more positive team behaviors (i.e., lower dysfunction). Therefore, each score ranges from 3 to 9, where higher values reflect healthier team dynamics. This study analyzes empirical evidence on team effectiveness in project-based learning environments and offers insights for the design of Capstone programs. In particular, it highlights the importance of goal alignment and focus on deliverables while emphasizing that team dynamics should be considered alongside technical and contextual factors.

</details>


### [35] [Aging of Prompt Engineering Techniques Across LLM Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24641)
*Anastasiia Rudyk,Julian Oertel,Regina Hebig*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文部分复现Khojah等人的研究，考察五种提示工程技术在三组新旧LLM上的代码生成效果。结论是：提示技术的增益会随模型家族和代际变化，不存在可直接迁移的通用最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM迭代迅速，但既有提示工程方法在后续模型版本中是否仍有效、增益是否衰减尚不明确。研究旨在量化不同提示技术跨模型代际的有效性变化，为代码生成场景中的提示选择提供依据。

Method: 研究在清洗后的CodePromptEval子集上评测218个具有丰富上下文的Python函数，采用pass@k函数正确性指标，对总计19,620次生成进行评估。比较的技术包括Zero-Shot、Few-Shot、CoT、CCoT及适配后的PoT；模型覆盖GPT-3.5-Turbo/GPT-4o、Qwen2 7B Instruct/Qwen2.5 7B Instruct和Mistral-7B-Instruct/Mistral-Large三组版本配对。

Result: GPT新版本中，结构化提示的边际收益减弱，部分情况下甚至为负，表明指令遵循与推理支架能力可能已更多内化。Qwen系列仍能从Few-Shot和CCoT中获得显著收益。Mistral系列呈混合趋势：CCoT收益持续存在，而CoT与PoT的收益有所减弱。

Conclusion: 提示工程会以模型家族特异的方式“老化”，不应将某一模型或代际上的有效提示模板不加调整地迁移到其他模型。后续应发展自适应、模型感知的提示策略，并使用覆盖更多维度的代码质量评价体系。

Abstract: Prompt engineering and prompt engineering techniques (PETs) have become an integral part of software engineering for AI systems. However, new LLMs are released frequently and it remains unclear how the effectiveness of prompt engineering techniques changes across successive generations of Large Language Models (LLMs). To this end, we conduct a partial replication of the study by Khojah et al. (2025). We evaluate five techniques - Zero-Shot, Few-Shot, Chain-of-Thought (CoT), Contrastive Chain-of-Thought (CCoT), and an adapted version of Program-of-Thought (PoT) - on six instruction-tuned models grouped into three version pairs: GPT-3.5-Turbo/GPT-4o, Qwen2 7B Instruct/Qwen2.5 7B Instruct, and Mistral-7B-Instruct/Mistral-Large. We use a cleaned subset of the CodePromptEval dataset with 218 context-rich Python functions and 19,620 total generations assessed via pass@k-based functional correctness to evaluate model pairs on function-level code generation tasks. We show that prompt engineering "ages" in a model-family-specific way: Newer GPT models exhibit diminishing or even negative marginal gains from structured prompting, suggesting that instruction-following and reasoning scaffolds are increasingly internalized, whereas Qwen models continue to benefit substantially from Few-Shot and CCoT. Mistral models show mixed behavior with persistent gains from CCoT but attenuated benefits from CoT and PoT. Our results imply that effective prompting strategies must be adapted per model family and generation rather than transferred unchanged. This motivates future work on adaptive, model-aware prompting and broader, multi-dimensional code quality evaluation.

</details>


### [36] [Causal Explanations of Process Monitor Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24672)
*Tom Yaacov,Nathan Blake,Hana Chockler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向预测性流程监控的局部解释方法：以实际因果关系为基础，量化单个事件对当前流程实例预测结果的因果责任，从而为黑盒预测模型提供稳定、简洁且模型无关的案例级解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性流程监控可预测运行中流程实例的结果，但高性能人工智能模型通常缺乏可解释性，难以说明特定预测由哪些历史事件导致。现有局部解释方法未充分建模流程事件之间的时序依赖，因此需要一种符合流程结构、能够识别事件因果影响的解释机制。

Method: 作者构建了适用于流程的因果模型，将轨迹中事件的时间依赖关系编码为因果结构；在此基础上，依据实际因果关系定义事件对预测结果的原因与责任度。方法以隐式方式利用该因果模型，并提出一个实用的模型无关近似算法，结合流程结构估计各事件对目标预测的责任大小，无需访问预测器内部参数。

Result: 实验使用标准预测性流程监控基准中的真实事件日志派生数据集，数据规模最高达到13万条轨迹、单条轨迹最长1800个事件、最多400类事件。与先进的局部解释基线相比，该方法生成的解释更稳定、更简洁，同时保持了有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 将实际因果关系与流程时序结构结合，能够有效提升预测性流程监控解释的质量。该方法尤其适合为单个运行案例识别关键影响事件，并可作为黑盒流程预测模型的通用后验解释工具。

Abstract: Process mining is widely used to diagnose processes and identify performance and compliance issues. Specifically, Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) techniques use AI models to predict outcomes of ongoing process instances. While these models can achieve high predictive performance, their black-box nature makes it difficult to understand the underlying reasons behind their output predictions. In this paper, we propose a novel approach for generating local (case-level) explanations of process monitor predictions based on the framework of actual causality. We define a causal model tailored to processes that captures temporal dependencies between events in a trace, thus allowing us to reason about causal influence of events on the predicted outcome. Our method uses this model implicitly to compute causes and quantify the importance of different events with respect to the predicted outcome. We present a practical, model-agnostic algorithm that approximates event responsibility given the process structure reflected in the causal model. We evaluate our approach on a range of datasets derived from real-life event logs from a standard PPM benchmark. Each dataset contains up to 130,000 traces, with trace lengths of up to 1,800 events and up to 400 distinct event types. We compare our approach with state-of-the-art local explanation methods. The results demonstrate that our approach produces more stable and concise explanations while maintaining competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [37] [''You Can't Open an LLM With a Screwdriver'': The De-Democratization of Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24720)
*Zixuan Feng,Italo Santos,Kostadin Damevski,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出：生成式人工智能扩大了代码生成的“可及性”，但未必扩大可靠软件开发的“控制权”。软件工程专业能力不会消失，而是将重心转向意图规约、人工智能行为编排与治理、软件行为评估及系统集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对“生成式人工智能将编写全部代码、编程即将终结”以及“代码生成普及必然实现软件开发民主化”的观点，作者指出二者混淆了生成代码的能力与对软件全生命周期负责的能力。

Method: 这是一篇愿景论文，基于专家小组讨论构建论证框架。论文将“可及性”界定为更多人能够借助人工智能生成类代码产物，将“控制权”界定为检查、评估、集成、维护和治理这些产物，使其成为可信赖软件的能力，并据此分析专业知识迁移。

Result: 人工智能可降低代码产出的门槛，使非专家和经验较少的开发者更容易生成代码；但代码所有权、软件实践、基础设施、评估方法及部署流水线的知识仍可能集中于少数主体。因此，控制权可能比代码生成能力更趋集中。

Conclusion: 软件工程并未被人工智能取代，其关键能力将转移至准确表达需求与意图、协调和约束人工智能行为、验证软件行为以及集成复杂系统。未来应围绕教育、工具和相关制度开展研究，以提升软件工程社区在人工智能时代的自主性、责任性与适应性。

Abstract: Claims that generative AI will soon write all of the code have led to predictions that programming is nearing its end. In this vision paper, we argue against this assumption that broader access to code generation necessarily democratizes software development, i.e., everyone can code but we have to distinguish between access and control: by access, we mean the ability of more people, including non-experts and less-experienced developers, to generate code-like artifacts with AI; by control, we mean the capacity to inspect, evaluate, integrate, maintain, and govern those artifacts as dependable software. While AI may broaden access to code production, control may become more concentrated among those who own or understand the code, software practices, infrastructure, evaluation practices, and deployment pipelines.
  Grounded in an expert panel, our vision paper argues that AI does not eliminate software engineering expertise but shifts where that expertise becomes most critical. The locus of software engineering expertise is shifting toward intent specification: orchestrating and governing AI behavior, evaluating software behavior, and integrating software systems. We conclude this paper by identifying research opportunities for education, tools, and policy that can help the software engineering community respond to the AI era with greater agency, accountability, and adaptability.

</details>


### [38] [From Natural Language Requirements to Graphical User Interfaces: Automated Prototyping and Verification with Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24749)
*Kristian Kolthoff*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文围绕交互式软件中的需求获取与验证展开，利用大语言模型降低从自然语言需求生成GUI原型，以及验证GUI实现是否满足需求的人工成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言需求具有歧义，形式化规格虽能提升精确性却要求较高专业能力；GUI原型可促进沟通和验证，但高保真原型制作成本高。与此同时，GUI需求验证仍主要依赖人工，现有自动化方法多为静态、规则驱动，难以处理语义复杂及动态交互需求。

Method: 针对原型生成挑战，提出基于自然语言的GUI检索与重排序方法、相应评测基准，以及高效适配大语言模型进行GUI生成的技术，覆盖专有GUI表示。针对验证挑战，提出面向静态GUI原型的LLM需求验证方法，并构建多模态LLM智能体：自动生成、执行和评估交互轨迹，以验证动态GUI应用中的复杂功能性与非功能性需求。

Result: 在包含人工标注的大规模基准上，GUI检索、重排序和生成适配方法展现出有效性。所提出的验证方法能够覆盖静态原型的语义复杂需求，以及动态应用中的复杂功能性和非功能性需求，并减少人工验证工作。

Conclusion: 该工作将大语言模型与GUI原型检索、生成及多模态交互验证结合，为需求工程提供了从自然语言需求到可视化原型、再到自动化一致性验证的支持。其核心贡献是扩大自动化方法对复杂语义和动态交互场景的适用范围，从而显著降低原型制作与需求验证成本。

Abstract: Requirements elicitation is essential for developing interactive software systems, as it helps ensure that the resulting product meets stakeholder needs. Since elicitation typically relies on natural language (NL), misunderstandings can arise from its inherent ambiguity. Formal specifications can reduce ambiguity but require technical expertise. GUI prototyping therefore provides a valuable alternative by turning requirements into tangible visual artifacts that support communication, elicitation, and validation. However, creating high-fidelity prototypes remains time-consuming and costly. Similarly, requirements verification, which ensures that implementations conform to specified requirements, is still largely manual, while existing automated approaches are often limited to static, rule-based techniques. This work addresses two challenges: (C1) reducing the effort required to transform NL requirements into GUI prototypes, and (C2) reducing the effort required for requirements verification in GUI applications and prototypes. For C1, we introduce novel NL-based GUI retrieval and reranking methods, new benchmarks, and techniques for efficiently adapting LLMs to GUI generation, including proprietary GUI representations. Their effectiveness is demonstrated on a large benchmark with human annotations. For C2, we propose LLM-based methods for verifying semantically complex NL requirements on static GUI prototypes and introduce a multimodal LLM-based agent for verifying complex functional and non-functional requirements in dynamic GUI applications through automatically generated and evaluated interaction trajectories. Overall, the proposed methods substantially reduce manual effort in GUI prototyping and requirements verification.

</details>


### [39] [Automatic Model Card Generation Using an LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24807)
*Tajkia Rahman Toma,Balreet Grewal,Cor-Paul Bezemer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出两个基于大语言模型的模型卡文档工具：MCTidy用于将已有模型卡重组为统一模板，MCGenie用于依据模型仓库资料自动生成模型卡；实验表明二者可较可靠地提升模型文档的标准化与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型卡缺少统一结构，部分模型完全没有对应文档，导致模型信息难以查找、比较和解释。作者旨在利用大语言模型降低文档整理与生成成本，改善模型卡的透明度、可用性和责任追踪能力。

Method: MCTidy接收已有模型卡，并通过大语言模型将内容映射、重组到标准化模板中。作者在48个Hugging Face模型卡上评估其信息保留率、章节归属准确性、幻觉现象和多次运行稳定性。MCGenie从模型仓库中的可用资料直接生成模型卡，并在相同48个模型上评估生成文本与参考内容的语义相似度、事实正确性，以及对不同输入资源可用性的敏感性。

Result: MCTidy表现出较高的信息保留能力和准确的章节分配，文本损失很少；幻觉较少，且主要出现于描述性章节；不同运行之间具有较强稳定性。MCGenie生成的模型卡平均语义相似度约为0.9，超过半数样本完全事实正确，其余多数错误为轻微错误。生成质量明显依赖输入资料的完整度，其中关联论文尤其重要。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可有效支持模型卡的自动规范化和自动生成，为大规模、结构一致的模型文档建设提供了可行路径。但系统的事实可靠性和内容完整性仍受仓库支持材料质量与覆盖度制约，实际部署中应优先补充论文等权威来源，并保留人工核验机制。

Abstract: Model cards are structured documents that summarize key information about machine learning models to improve transparency, usability, and accountability. However, they often lack a consistent structure, and many models provide no model cards, making comparison and interpretation difficult. This paper presents two contributions. First, we propose MCTidy, an LLM-based approach that reorganizes existing model cards into a standardized template to improve clarity and comparability. Second, we introduce MCGenie, an LLM-based system that generates model cards directly from model repository data. We apply MCTidy to 48 Hugging Face model cards and evaluate information retention, section alignment, hallucination, and stability. Our findings show high information retention with minimal textual loss, accurate section assignment, rare hallucinations primarily in descriptive sections, and strong stability across runs. We assess MCGenie by generating model cards for the same 48 models and assessing semantic similarity, factual correctness, and sensitivity to input resources. The generated model cards achieved high semantic similarity (mean around 0.9); over half were fully correct, and most remaining errors were minor. Generation quality depended strongly on the availability of supporting resources, particularly associated papers. Overall, our findings demonstrate the potential of LLM-based methods to enable scalable, standardized model card documentation.

</details>

### [40] [MARS: Multi-Specialist LLM Relay System for Competitive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23918)
*Andrei Mikhailov,Mikhail Burtsev,Alsu Sagirova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARS是一种面向竞赛编程的多智能体协作框架，通过检索选择算法领域专家，并以接力方式生成、测试和修复代码，从而提升大语言模型的解题通过率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体代码生成流程通常采用通用的规划、编码和调试角色，将算法选择完全交给基础模型，导致复杂算法问题上的性能受限。MARS旨在利用领域专长和结构化协作改善这一问题。

Method: MARS采用提示驱动的多智能体架构，并通过算法理论语料库进行检索增强生成。系统根据题目选择动态规划、图、字符串、几何等相关专家；首个智能体生成C++17代码，后续专家在沙箱中运行公开样例，并决定保留、修复或转交代码，同时传递结构化信息包。最后由基础设施修复智能体统一处理模板和环境相关问题。

Result: 在CodeContests测试集上，使用Gemma 4时，MARS达到0.624±0.006的通过率，平均每题使用2.3个记录阶段，相比直接提示提高14.4个百分点。其性能接近CodeSIM的0.731，但墙钟成本低约3.3倍，并显著降低了单题令牌消耗的方差。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将算法领域专家、检索增强和基于执行反馈的接力协作结合起来，能够有效提升竞赛编程代码生成的可靠性与成本效率。MARS在较少协作阶段下取得了有竞争力的性能，但与更强基线仍存在一定差距。

Abstract: Large Language Models excel at code generation, yet competitive programming exposes a persistent failure mode: existing multi-agent pipelines distribute work over generic planner, coder, and debugger roles and delegate the choice of algorithmic technique to the backbone alone. We present MARS (Multi-Agent Relay of Specialized LLMs), a prompt-only framework in which each agent is a topic specialist---dynamic programming, graphs, strings, geometry, and so on---grounded by retrieval-augmented generation over an algorithm-theory corpus. Given a problem, retrieval selects a small team of relevant specialists; a starter writes an initial C++17 solution, and each subsequent turn runs the candidate against public examples in a sandbox, lets the active specialist keep, repair, or hand off the draft, and forwards a structured packet to the next specialist. A single infrastructure-fixer pass normalizes boilerplate at the end.
