A 3-level evaluation pyramid assesses execution robustness, reasoning dynamics, and diagnostic outcomes. Experiments show that EviDx improves diagnostic performance and process stability while revealing model-dependent capability boundaries.

</details>


### [139] [PhysMLLMs: Spatial Priors for Unified Referring Segmentation and Grounded Reasoning of Images and Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24574)
*Siyao Yan,Bo Han,Jisheng Dang,Bimei Wang,Shude Wang,Hong Peng,Yulan Guo,Jianhuang Lai,Bin Hu,Tat-SengChua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PhysMLLMs，一种在训练阶段向视频多模态大语言模型注入物理启发空间连续性先验的方法。其核心REPA-Global通过蒸馏冻结DINOv2教师的全局视觉表征，提升语言引导视频分割的跨帧稳定性，且不改变推理流程或增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频多模态大语言模型在语言引导视频分割中易出现掩码抖动、目标漂移和身份切换等时空不一致问题，尤其在目标尺度较小、快速运动、部分遮挡或相邻存在相似干扰物时更明显。作者认为，根源之一是训练过程缺少显式空间先验，导致模型难以持续保持对象的空间身份与形状表征。

Method: PhysMLLMs采用训练期先验注入架构，以对象中心的稳定表征为目标。核心模块REPA-Global使用离线构建的DINOv2视觉嵌入缓存，将学生模型的全局视觉表征与冻结教师模型的表征进行对齐；同时通过分阶段的蒸馏调度控制该监督信号在训练过程中的作用。由于教师模型和蒸馏过程仅用于训练，部署时无需额外模块与计算。

Result: 在多个视频基准上，该方法提高了视频分割掩码质量及跨帧一致性；对于小目标、快速运动、遮挡、干扰目标和需要推理的查询，增益更显著。在单帧指代表达图像分割及代表性通用视觉语言模型评测中，性能与基线相当，表明方法未明显损害图像级定位能力和通用多模态能力。摘要未提供具体数据集名称、评价指标及数值提升幅度，因此尚无法独立判断收益的统计显著性和不同设置下的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，可通过训练阶段的全局视觉表征蒸馏，为视频多模态模型引入空间连续性归纳偏置，从而改善长期视频分割的时序稳定性，并保持原有推理效率与通用能力。其主要价值在于将一致性改进限制在训练端，具有较好的部署兼容性；后续仍需结合完整实验验证其对不同模型规模、长视频场景及真实复杂数据的泛化效果。

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models support language guided video segmentation, but they often show spatio temporal inconsistencies, e.g., jitter, drift, and identity switches. These failures are more common when targets are partly hidden or when similar objects appear nearby.One likely reason is that current training lacks explicit spatial priors, which makes it difficult to maintain stable spatial identity and shape over time. We present PhysMLLMs, a training-stage prior injection architecture that injects physics-inspired spatial continuity priors into Video MLLMs. PhysMLLMs is designed to encourage more stable object-centered representations by aligning the student global visual representation with a frozen teacher model during training. Our core mechanism, Global Representation Prior Alignment (REPA-Global), distills global visual representations from a frozen DINOv2 teacher using an offline embedding cache and a scheduled distillation plan. This design keeps inference unchanged and does not add inference time cost. Across multiple video benchmarks, PhysMLLMs improves video segmentation mask quality and cross-frame consistency, with larger gains on challenging cases involving small targets, fast motion, occlusion, distractors, and reasoning queries. On single-frame referring image segmentation and representative general VLM benchmarks, PhysMLLMs maintains comparable performance, demonstrating that the injected spatial prior improves video consistency without compromising image-level grounding or general multimodal capability. These results suggest that physics-inspired spatial prior injection can improve temporal stability while preserving general capability. The code is available at https://github.com/tusu-code/20260121-icml2026-2.git.

</details>


### [140] [Pivot-and-Station Multi-Agent Path Finding: Solvability, Complexity, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24585)
*Andrea Di Nezza,Mihir Patel,Fabio Fagnani,Sara Bernardini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出枢纽—站点多智能体路径规划（PS-MAPF）问题：部分执行任务的智能体需先经过可互换的枢纽资源，随后全体智能体停靠至匿名站点，并研究其可解性、计算复杂度与求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高密度仓储、自动化停车和生产物流等场景中，智能体既要访问关键作业资源，又必须最终停靠且不妨碍后续运行。传统多智能体路径规划通常未同时刻画“先访问可替代资源”和“终点为匿名停靠位”这两个核心约束。

Method: 作者形式化定义PS-MAPF；从图结构角度给出完备可解性刻画：在双边连通图上任意实例均可解，而在一般连通图上，利用相对于空闲顶点数量的结构性有效距离给出充要条件。随后证明以停靠完成时间为目标的最小化问题，以及以停靠总流时间为目标的最小化问题，即使仅有一个枢纽也为NP难。算法方面提出完备基线方法、基于SAT的最优求解器，以及启发式的枢纽优先规划（PPP）。

Result: 理论上，论文获得了PS-MAPF实例可解性的完整判定条件，并确立两类主要优化目标的NP难性。实验上，PPP可求解74%至89%的基准实例，其完成时间和流时间相较完备基线低多个数量级。

Conclusion: PS-MAPF有效扩展了传统多智能体路径规划，使其适配具有共享作业点和匿名停靠需求的高密度自动化系统。其主要贡献是可解性的精确结构理论、优化问题的复杂度下界，以及在大规模实例上表现显著更优的实用规划算法。

Abstract: Automated high-density storage systems (warehouses, robotic parking, plant logistics, etc.) require fleets of agents to move through scarce task-critical resources and then park without obstructing future operations. We introduce Pivot-and-Station Multi-Agent Path Finding (PS-MAPF), a MAPF variant in which a subset of tasked agents must each visit one of a set of interchangeable pivots (e.g., workstations) before the entire fleet terminates at anonymous stations, one agent per station. We characterize solvability completely: every instance on a 2-edge-connected graph is solvable, and, on arbitrary connected graphs, a structural effective-distance measure relative to the number of unoccupied vertices gives a necessary and sufficient condition. We prove that minimizing station-makespan or station-flowtime is NP-hard already with a single pivot. We present three algorithms, a complete baseline, a SAT-based optimal solver, and Pivot-Prioritized Planning (PPP), the last solving 74-89% of benchmark instances with makespan and flowtime orders of magnitude below the baseline.

</details>


### [141] [Causal Modelling of Support Interventions for Student Competency Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24632)
*Francesca Mangili,Alessandro Antonucci,Rafael Cabañas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张将结构因果模型引入教育测评，以补足项目反应理论等关联性模型无法直接支持的干预与反事实推理能力，并以算法技能测评数据展示其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统心理测量模型主要刻画学生能力与作答表现之间的统计关联，适合能力估计，但难以回答“给予提示会如何影响表现”或“若未给予提示结果会怎样”等干预性问题。教育者需要这类信息来识别个体需求、设计针对性支持并评估教学策略。

Method: 作者提出结构因果模型的构建协议：基于领域专家知识定义变量、因果结构及结构方程，将学生能力、复杂任务表现和提示等教育测评要素显式纳入模型。该方法强调方程所需知识主要是逻辑性的机制描述，而非依赖难以验证的概率假设；随后使用测量义务教育阶段学生算法技能的复杂任务数据进行说明。

Result: 摘要表明，该框架能够表达标准关联模型不可获得的推理形式，尤其包括对提示等外部干预的显式建模，以及围绕不同干预条件的反事实情景分析。论文未在摘要中报告具体量化结果或与基线模型的性能比较。

Conclusion: 结构因果模型可将教育测评从单纯的概率信念更新扩展为机制驱动的决策支持工具，使测评结果能够服务于干预设计与效果评估。其关键限制在于因果结构和结构方程依赖专家引出，因此模型有效性取决于专家知识的完整性、变量定义及因果假设的合理性。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of student competencies is essential for enabling educators to identify individual needs, design targeted interventions, and evaluate the effectiveness of educational strategies. Empirical assessment procedures are typically grounded in psychometric models, such as item response theory, which relate student competence levels to performance on assessment tasks. In this paper, we advocate adopting a structural causal modelling approach to educational assessment, moving beyond probabilistic belief updating toward a framework that explicitly supports interventional and counterfactual reasoning. We propose a corresponding protocol for its construction and analyse the practical relevance of forms of reasoning that remain inaccessible to standard associative models, including the explicit modelling of interventions such as hints and the related counterfactual scenario analysis. Although our protocol requires the structural equations to be elicited from experts, the necessary information is purely logical and does not rely on probabilistic, less tenable assumptions. We illustrate the approach using data from an assessment that employs complex tasks designed to measure compulsory school student algorithmic skills.

</details>


### [142] [Parason: Revealing Subtask and Trial Parallelism in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24658)
*Zhengyang Zhang,Zijian Zhang,Jiaxuan Gao,Shusheng Xu,Yi Wu,Song Han,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Parason框架，通过同时挖掘子任务并行与试探并行，将大模型测试时的串行推理轨迹重构为可并行执行的结构，在保持竞争性准确率的同时实现约1.7倍平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归推理需按令牌和步骤串行生成，复杂任务的推理延迟极高。既有并行推理主要依赖任务分解，即将问题拆成可独立求解的子任务；但推理中大量计算实际来自多个候选思路的并行探索、验证与聚合。论文旨在系统刻画并利用这种试探并行，以降低端到端推理时延。

Method: 作者将推理并行性划分为子任务并行和试探并行两类，并分析其在推理步骤中的占比。Parason利用上下文无关文法把原本串行的推理轨迹转换为带有并行分支的结构化轨迹；随后采用并行性感知的组相对策略优化（PA-GRPO）进行训练，其奖励函数联合考虑答案正确性、延迟以及两类并行比例。推理阶段通过工具调用实际调度和执行已学习的并行结构。

Result: 在HLE上的分析显示，试探并行占DeepSeek-V4推理步骤中可并行计算的65.5%，且题目越困难，其主导性越强。在AIME24和AIME25等数学推理基准上，Parason在维持竞争性准确率的前提下取得约1.7倍的平均加速，说明结构化并行轨迹能够转化为实际墙钟时间收益。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将测试时推理并行从单一的任务分解扩展为“子任务并行+试探并行”的统一框架，并以训练目标和执行机制共同优化准确率与时延。结果表明，试探并行是高难度推理中重要且常被忽视的加速来源；不过摘要未提供不同硬件、工具调度开销、规模扩展规律及各并行组件消融结果，实际泛化性仍需结合全文实验进一步判断。

Abstract: Scaling test-time reasoning has substantially improved the problem-solving ability of large language models (LLMs), but standard autoregressive decoding still executes long reasoning traces sequentially, creating severe latency for difficult tasks (up to days and weeks). Parallel reasoning offers a natural remedy. However, prior systems primarily focus on Subtask Parallelism, where the model learns to decompose a high-level task into smaller chunks that can be solved independently. This approach overlooks another pervasive form of parallelism: Trial Parallelism, where multiple speculative attempts explore, verify, and aggregate competing hypotheses in parallel. In this paper, we introduce Parason, which reveals and learns both forms of parallelism in LLM reasoning. Our analysis identifies Trial Parallelism as the majority of parallelizable reasoning computation (65.5% in DeepSeek-V4's reasoning steps in HLE), and it becomes increasingly dominant on hard problems. Guided by this taxonomy, Parason converts sequential reasoning traces into structured parallel trajectories with a context-free grammar, then trains models with Parallelism-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (PA-GRPO), whose reward jointly balances accuracy, latency, and the two parallelism ratios. At inference time, Parason executes the learned parallel structure through tool calls, translating theoretical savings to real-world wall-clock acceleration. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks including AIME24 and AIME25 show that Parason achieves an average acceleration about 1.7$\times$ while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [143] [The Invisible Editorial Layer: Formalizing Undisclosed Inference-Time Steering, Probability Placement, and the Attribution Problem in Deployed Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24662)
*Augusto Camargo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are commonly evaluated under the assumption that their observable behavior is primarily determined by model weights, training data, alignment procedures, and user prompts. This view is incomplete. Modern inference pipelines may systematically modify the probability distribution produced by a model immediately before token selection, creating an additional layer of control between frozen weights and observed text.
  While controlled generation (e.g., PPLM, GeDi, DExperts, FUDGE) and text-watermarking systems (e.g., SynthID-Text) demonstrate the technical maturity of decoding- and logit-level interventions, the governance, security, and economic implications of an undisclosed inference policy remain comparatively underexplored. This paper examines the emergence of inference-time framing bias: the systematic modification of generated language toward political, ideological, institutional, or commercial frames via interventions applied after model inference but before token sampling.
  We formalize the operational reality Model != Deployed System and introduce three concepts: (1) the Inference Attribution Problem, characterizing why observed behavioral bias cannot generally be causally attributed to model weights alone under limited observability; (2) Probability Placement, defining a hypothetical advertising primitive in which commercial influence is implemented through systematic shifts in generation probabilities rather than explicit product insertions; and (3) Inference Policy Transparency, a governance principle for making deployment-layer interventions auditable. We examine these concepts in relation to Article 5 of the EU AI Act, the EU Digital Services Act, and FTC doctrines.

</details>


### [144] [Confident at the moment of action: belief miscalibration in LLM play under hidden information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24691)
*Bhushan Kashinath Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现：在隐藏信息棋类任务中，模型行动时陈述的高置信度并不代表更高正确率；尤其是对对手隐藏关键棋子位置的高置信度判断，实际准确性极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多智能体系统以模型自报置信度作为执行、拦截或升级决策的依据，但这一做法隐含“行动当下的置信度与正确性一致”的假设。论文旨在直接检验该假设，并考察传统性能指标能否反映信念质量。

Method: 作者构建可秘密且反复转移关键棋子身份的隐藏信息棋类变体。模型每回合需独立于落子选择，报告对对手隐藏关键棋子位置的概率分布；比赛结束后依据可恢复的真实状态对概率预测进行评分。研究包含两个独立批次、一次复现，以及覆盖另一提供方的四种额外模型配置；另比较合法性、成本、延迟和完成率等常规指标与信念质量的关系。

Result: 在两个独立批次中，模型对隐藏位置自报高置信度（至少0.5）后作出的捕获仅62次中正确1次。校准误差几乎完全由这类高置信度事件贡献：原始批次占99.3%，复现批次占98.7%。该趋势在其他配置中以较弱形式保持一致，但多数两两差异受样本量限制而未达统计可区分。固定外部排行榜分数时，仅改变推理预算即可使指标变化接近较大跨模型差距，说明不能将结果解读为能力决定校准。更重要的是，常规指标可与信念质量完全脱钩：在所有常规轴上最佳的配置，反而具有测试中最差的信念质量。

Conclusion: 模型可能在任务结果上获胜，却对其决策所依赖的隐藏状态持有严重失准、且危险地过度自信的信念。因此，仅以最终胜负、合法性、速度、成本或完成率评估智能体不足以发现该问题；部署中若使用自报置信度作为行动门控信号，应单独进行与决策时刻对应的概率校准和高置信度错误审计。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly gate actions on a model's own stated confidence, which assumes confidence tracks correctness at the moment of acting. We test this in a hidden-information chess variant where royal status can be secretly, repeatedly relocated between pieces, and where an agent's stated probability distribution over the opponent's hidden royal piece -- elicited every turn, separately from the move it chooses -- is scored against ground truth recoverable after the game. Across two independent batches, captures made at high stated confidence ($\geq 0.5$) about the hidden piece's location were correct in 1 of 62 cases. The calibration deficit is concentrated almost entirely in these events: 99.3% of it in the original batch, 98.7% in the replication. The same pattern, in weaker form, orders consistently (point estimates only; most pairwise gaps are not statistically distinguishable at this sample size) across four further model configurations spanning a second provider -- reported as scope for the finding, not as evidence that capability predicts calibration: a same-model comparison at a fixed external leaderboard score shows a deliberation-budget change alone moves the metric by nearly as much as a large cross-model gap. In a separate seat, conventional evaluation axes -- legality, cost, latency, completion rate -- can dissociate entirely from belief quality, with the configuration winning on every conventional axis producing the worst belief quality tested. A model exhibiting this pattern can still win the game its belief was about, which is why outcome-only evaluation would not detect it.

</details>


### [145] [Lifted Model Construction under Approximate Commutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24713)
*Malte Luttermann,Jan Speller,Tanya Braun,Marcel Gehrke,Ralf Möller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ε-可交换性，用于处理学习参数中的微小偏差导致因子仅近似满足置换不变性的问题；该方法可构建近似提升模型，并在降低推理运行时间的同时保持较高查询精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升推理依赖对象在概率分布中的不可区分性，以及势函数分解中因子的严格可交换性。实际数据学习会引入参数扰动，使理论上相同的对象对应因子不再完全可交换，从而限制传统提升模型构建与推理方法的适用性。

Method: 作者将可交换性放宽为ε-可交换性：对于指定输入子集的任意置换，因子输出的差异被ε所界定。在此定义基础上，研究如何识别和利用近似可交换因子构建提升表示，并将其用于后续概率推理；同时对由近似合并或提升操作引入的推理误差给出严格上界。

Result: 理论分析表明，ε-可交换性能够支持近似提升模型构建，且其造成的误差可被严格控制。实验结果验证：相较于更精细但计算成本更高的处理方式，该方法可获得相近的查询准确率，并实现更低运行时间。

Conclusion: ε-可交换性为含有学习噪声和参数非完全一致性的概率模型提供了可控的近似提升推理机制。其核心价值在于以可证明的误差界换取计算效率，使提升模型构建在真实数据驱动场景中更具实用性。

Abstract: Lifted inference algorithms enable scalable probabilistic inference even for large object domains by leveraging the indistinguishability of objects in a probability distribution. An essential prerequisite for constructing a lifted representation is to identify commutative factors, i.e., functions whose output values are invariant under permutations of a subset of their input values, in a potential-based factorisation. In practice, however, parameters learned from data inevitably deviate even if associated objects are indistinguishable, causing their corresponding factors to be only approximately commutative instead of being exactly commutative. We address this problem by introducing the concept of ε-commutativity, a relaxation of commutativity where output values are only approximately invariant under permutations of input values. Specifically, we show how ε-commutativity can be exploited for lifted model construction, downstream probabilistic inference, and prove strict bounds on the induced approximation error, thereby ensuring the practical applicability of lifted model construction while maintaining highly accurate query results. These theoretical guarantees are confirmed empirically, demonstrating comparable query accuracy at lower runtime.

</details>


### [146] [Meta$^n$: Recursive Self-Improvement through Emergent Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24735)
*Zae Myung Kim,Young-Jun Lee,Seungyeon Jwa,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Meta^n通过固定元操作Ω、递归扩展其输入并逐层生成策略预处理器与可调用工具，实现了可持续加深的自我改进；在两种基础模型和八类基准上均优于既有方法，并在ARC-AGI-2上取得唯一非零成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进LLM主要优化答案而非产生答案的过程；元层通常被固定，或必须保留部分编辑机制以维持稳定性，因此元推理深度通常受限于约两层。该工作旨在实现稳定且可扩展的多层元推理。

Method: 提出Meta^n，保持元操作Ω不变，不断递归处理自身生成的结果。每一层读取下层求解器堆栈的执行轨迹及生成代码，并将下一层写成战略性预处理流程和可调用辅助函数。由于Ω固定，系统稳定性不依赖持续修改自身；由于输入严格增长，各层可在更高层次的信息视角上推理。递归深度由收敛情况决定，并通过进化式档案搜索层级链。

Result: 在两种基础模型、八类基准任务上，Meta^n整体优于既有自我改进智能体。在刻意抑制技能记忆的ARC-AGI-2上，Meta^n是唯一取得非零成绩的方法。消融实验表明，递归收益主要来自各层向下一层传递的条件信息；随着深度增加，还会自发形成不同的层级角色，且无需预先设定提示。

Conclusion: 将固定的元操作作用于不断扩展的递归输入，是实现稳定深层自我改进的一种有效路径。实验结果支持：层间条件传递和自发角色分化是性能提升的关键，而递归深度可通过收敛自适应确定。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents refine answers, not the process that produces those answers. Systems that add a meta-level hold that level fixed, and those that edit themselves must leave part of their own editing machinery untouched to stay stable, capping the meta-depth they realize at roughly two. We present Meta$^n$, which keeps the meta-operation fixed and recurses on its input instead. That operation, $Ω$, is applied repeatedly to its own products, reading the traces of the solver stack below together with the code that produced them, then writing the next layer as a strategic pre-process and a library of callable helpers. Because $Ω$ never changes, it cannot destabilize the system, and because its input strictly grows, each layer reasons from a higher vantage than the last. Depth is set by convergence rather than fixed in advance, and an evolutionary archive searches over layer chains. Across two backbones, Meta$^n$ outperforms prior self-improving agents on all eight benchmark families. The sharpest case is ARC-AGI-2, built to resist skill memorization, where it alone scores above zero. Ablations indicate that most of the gain from recursion comes from the conditioning each layer passes to the next, and distinct layer roles emerge with depth although no prompt prescribes them. Code available at https://github.com/minnesotanlp/meta-n

</details>


### [147] [RACE: Scalable Statistical Estimation of Functional Consistency in LLM Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24758)
*Runyu Wang,Bo Liu,Xiaxin Zhang,Yu Han,Jiawei Cao,Xiaoye Zhang,Zhe Zhang,Yifan Yang,Peng Ping*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RACE（Residual Alignment for Consistency Estimation），一种仅依赖前向传播的统计框架，用于评估Transformer神经元在整个目标领域内的功能一致性；其在领域特异性、与目标领域的关联验证及计算效率上优于梯度式点估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性需要识别跨整个数据领域都具有稳定行为的神经元。现有方法多采用样本级点估计，难以刻画群体分布及跨样本稳定性；或依赖高成本计算，因而不适合大规模、全领域分析。

Method: RACE通过残差对齐来构造神经元功能一致性的统计估计，基于领域内样本的整体行为而非单个实例评分进行判断。该方法只需前向传播，并结合扰动实验评估所选神经元的领域特异性，再以词元分布层面的统计结果检验神经元选择与目标领域之间的关联。

Result: 摘要声称：相较梯度式点估计，RACE在扰动实验中表现出更强的领域特异性；词元分布分析支持所选神经元与目标领域存在关联；其计算开销比梯度方法低两个数量级。摘要未给出具体数据集、评价指标、绝对性能数值、显著性检验或不同模型规模下的结果。

Conclusion: RACE将神经元分析从实例级归因扩展到领域级一致性评估，并以低成本前向统计提高可扩展性。其核心贡献在于提供一种更适合领域范围机制分析的神经元筛选准则；但其泛化性、统计稳健性及与其他基线方法的全面比较仍需依赖正文实验确认。

Abstract: Discovering stable neuron behavior across entire domains remains a challenge in mechanistic interpretability. Existing methods often rely on instance-level point estimates or computationally expensive procedures, which either obscure population-level variability or limit scalable domain-wide analysis. We present RACE (Residual Alignment for Consistency Estimation), a forward-pass statistical framework that evaluates the domain-wide functional consistency of Transformer neurons. Perturbation experiments demonstrate that RACE achieves superior domain specificity compared to gradient-based point estimates. Meanwhile, token-distribution-level results verify the association between the selected neurons and the target domain. Furthermore, its computational overhead is two orders of magnitude lower than that of gradient-based methods.

</details>


### [148] [Evidence Blindness in Direct Corpus Interaction: Persistent Navigation with AtlasNav](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24764)
*Hongyu Guo,Zhiyu Zheng,Zhao Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AtlasNav：一种面向大规模智能体检索的持久化多视图语料导航框架。其核心是预先将语料组织为可复用的“Corpus Atlas”，使智能体在有限交互预算内进行自适应导航，而非针对每个查询在线重建检索空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接语料交互虽允许访问完整语料，但在有限步骤、调用或阅读预算下，证据可能未被检出、已检出文档未被打开，或打开后关键片段未被暴露。论文将这一逐阶段发生的证据失效称为“Evidence Blindness（证据盲区）”。作者认为，现有方法的主要瓶颈不只是证据可访问性，还在于缺乏可复用的语料组织结构，导致大量结构发现工作被重复地在线执行。

Method: 作者首先以阶段化证据实现率量化证据盲区，覆盖证据被发现、文档被打开和决定性片段被暴露等环节。随后提出AtlasNav，在保留直接语料交互能力的同时，对语料进行一次性、持久化的多视图组织，形成Corpus Atlas。查询时，智能体基于该图谱式/多视图结构进行预算受限的自适应导航，以更早定位并串联所需证据，而不是从查询和轨迹中临时构建动态工作空间。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus上，AtlasNav达到92.05%的严格准确率，相比此前最强动态工作空间方法，记录的在线推理成本降低30.21%。在相同预算下，该方法能更早实现完整所需证据，并更快接近向同一模型直接提供证据时的经验性能参考。该表示原则还在PhantomWiki的不同语料组织形式及10K至1M规模控制实验中保持有效，并能竞争性迁移至异构企业知识场景。

Conclusion: 论文表明，大规模智能体搜索的性能关键不仅是能否访问证据，更是语料能否被表示为适合有限交互预算导航的持久结构。通过离线构建可复用的多视图语料地图，AtlasNav减少了在线重复建构成本，缓解了阶段性证据盲区，并提升了检索效率与问答准确性。

Abstract: Large language model agents are moving beyond conventional retrieval-augmented generation toward direct interaction with external corpora. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) keeps the full corpus accessible, yet reachable evidence can remain unusable under finite interaction budgets. Required evidence may fail to surface, a surfaced supporting document may remain unopened, or an opened document may fail to expose its decisive fragment. We call this progressive silent loss Evidence Blindness and quantify it through stage-wise evidence realization. Within the DCI paradigm, raw interaction adds little reusable corpus organization, while dynamic-workspace methods reconstruct a query-conditioned interaction space from each query and trajectory. In both cases, useful structure is recovered largely online. We instead formulate large-scale agentic search as finite-budget navigation over reusable corpus structure. We introduce AtlasNav, a persistent multi-view corpus-navigation framework that retains direct corpus interaction but organizes the corpus once into a Corpus Atlas, allowing each query to navigate adaptively rather than reconstruct shared structure. On BrowseComp-Plus, AtlasNav achieves 92.05% strict accuracy while reducing recorded online inference cost by 30.21% relative to the prior dynamic-workspace state of the art. Under matched budgets, it realizes the complete required evidence earlier and approaches the same model's evidence-supplied empirical reference more rapidly. The same representation principle remains effective under PhantomWiki's distinct corpus organization and controlled 10K-1M scaling, and transfers competitively to heterogeneous enterprise knowledge. These results show that agentic search depends not only on accessible evidence, but also on how the corpus is represented so that limited interaction becomes effective navigation.

</details>


### [149] [Right Diagnoses, Decorative Reasoning:A Perturbation Audit of Medical Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24790)
*Mengzhu Xu,Jifan Gao,Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Xi Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向医疗问答大模型的“医疗扰动审计”框架，用于检验可见思维链是否真实参与答案生成。结果显示，多数模型的思维链与最终答案显著脱钩，更像事后文档而非实际推理依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用思维链忠实性测试未充分考虑医疗场景中证据变更的临床重要性；而医疗大模型评测通常只检查答案正确性，将推理文本视为不可验证的黑箱。因此，作者希望建立能直接检验医疗思维链因果作用与失效模式的评估标准。

Method: 作者构建含30类操作的扰动集，同时修改问题和思维链，操作包括病情严重度反转、否定词翻转、人口学信息替换及证据删减等。通过联合考察“思维链是否随编辑更新”与“答案是否翻转”，对模型失效模式分类；并定义链脱钩率CDR，即面对具有临床意义且应破坏原结论的编辑时，思维链未反映变化且答案未改变的比例。实验覆盖14个大模型、4个医疗问答基准，并辅以思维链破坏实验、去除思维链提示实验及两名资深临床专家对197个扰动问题的复核。

Result: 在临床意义明确的破坏性编辑下，全体模型的平均CDR为72.9%，表明大量模型既未在推理文本中登记关键信息变化，也未相应修正答案。进一步地，直接破坏思维链文本不会改变准确率，取消思维链提示也不会降低准确率。专家复核显示，197个扰动题中98.5%的金标准答案仍可成立，支持扰动设计的有效性。该模式在不同规模、医疗微调和推理微调模型中均存在；对于无法取得推理文本的闭源模型，答案侧信号也与脱钩现象一致。

Conclusion: 论文认为，当前医疗大模型的可见思维链通常缺乏对最终决策的可验证因果贡献，不能被直接视为可靠的临床推理证据。所提出的医疗扰动审计与CDR指标可作为可复用工具，用于区分忠实推理与事后生成的解释性文档，并推动医疗模型评估从答案正确性扩展到推理过程的忠实性。

Abstract: Clinicians read chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales as evidence of medical reasoning, but whether the visible chain plays that role is rarely tested. General-domain CoT-faithfulness probes ignore clinical cost, and medical LLM evaluations treat the chain as a black box. We close this gap with a medical perturbation audit: a 30-operator battery edits both the chain and the question with clinically motivated operators (severity reversal, negation flip, demographic swap, evidence ablation), paired with a chain-update times answer-flip joint analysis that classifies each model by its failure mode. Applied to 14 LLMs on four medical QA benchmarks, three independent tests converge: the Chain-Decoupling Rate (CDR; chain does not register the edit and the answer does not flip) is 72.9% panel-wide on clinically meaningful destructive edits, chain corruption leaves accuracy unchanged, and removing CoT prompting does not reduce accuracy. Two board-certified clinicians re-annotate N=197 perturbed questions; 98.5% leave the gold defensible. The pattern holds across medical and reasoning fine-tuning and scale; on the closed-source tier, where the chain text is unavailable, the answer-side signals are consistent with the same decoupling. Our framework and CDR provide a reusable yardstick for auditing whether medical CoT is faithful or merely documentation.

</details>


### [150] [CAFE: Self-Improving Search Agents Need Co-Evolving Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24794)
*Boyang Liu,Senjie Jin,Peixin Wang,Zhangyue Yin,Yibo Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Xinbing Liang,Shizheng Zhu,Yuhui Wang,Jingqi Tong,Zhiheng Xi,Jiazheng Zhang,Clive Bai,Clarenceai,Blaze Chen,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CAFE（Coupled Agent--Feedback Evolution）框架，使检索搜索智能体与过程纠错器共享参数并交替优化。该方法将反馈视为轨迹内可学习干预：智能体学习何时请求和利用反馈，纠错器则从成败轨迹中学习生成有效修正。实验显示，CAFE在7个智能体搜索基准上平均优于所比较的基于强化学习的搜索智能体，并在6个域外基准上保持增益，同时降低答案级幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖终局奖励的结果监督无法定位中间步骤的错误，也无法在错误累积前改变当前轨迹。另一方面，纠错反馈的学习受结果混杂影响：失败原因不易从最终成败中分离，且随着智能体能力提升，错误分布会持续变化。因此，作者关注如何让“请求反馈的策略”与“生成反馈的能力”同步适应并共同提升。

Method: CAFE采用共享参数模型，在搜索智能体与批评/反馈角色之间交替训练。初始化阶段，围绕基础智能体自身失败轨迹构建反馈条件化恢复能力。在线强化学习阶段，使用提示级的“调用反馈—跳过反馈”成功率差构造比较式反馈估计，用于塑造请求反馈的回报；同时通过反馈感知优势塑形，对反馈前后token级优势进行重加权。离线阶段，从匹配的成功与失败轨迹中提取偏好对，采用基于偏好的优化来学习更有效的反馈。

Result: 在7个智能体搜索基准上，CAFE的平均表现超过文中评估的强化学习搜索智能体；在全部6个域外基准上仍保留性能增益，表明具有一定泛化性。结果还显示其可减少答案级幻觉。消融实验表明，仅升级搜索智能体或仅升级纠错器都会逐渐进入性能平台期，而交替更新两者能持续带来改进。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：对于可自我改进的搜索智能体，反馈不应被视为固定外部信号，而应与受其指导的策略共同演化。CAFE通过在线的请求决策与优势塑形、离线的轨迹偏好学习，以及智能体—纠错器交替优化，缓解了终局奖励稀疏和反馈分布漂移的问题。摘要未提供具体数值、计算开销及不同任务类型下的失效边界，仍需结合正文进一步评估其统计显著性和成本效益。

Abstract: Outcome-supervised search agents learn when and how to retrieve evidence, but terminal rewards neither localize intermediate errors nor redirect an ongoing trajectory before those errors compound. Treating corrective feedback as a learned in-trajectory intervention couples the two roles: the agent must decide when to request and use feedback, while the critic must infer useful corrections from outcome-confounded rollouts whose failure patterns shift as the agent improves. We introduce CAFE (Coupled Agent--Feedback Evolution), a framework in which a shared-parameter model alternates between search-agent and critic roles. CAFE initializes feedback-conditioned recovery from trajectories built around the base agent's own failures, then couples online and offline optimization. During online RL, a comparative feedback estimate uses a prompt-level call--skip success gap to shape request returns, while feedback-aware advantage shaping reweights token advantages before and after feedback. Offline, rollout-derived preference optimization learns feedback from matched successful and unsuccessful trajectories. On seven agentic search benchmarks, CAFE outperforms the evaluated RL-based search agents on average, retains its gains across all six out-of-domain benchmarks, and reduces answer-level hallucinations. One-sided ablations show that improving only the agent or only the critic eventually plateaus, whereas alternating the two updates continues to improve performance. These findings suggest that a self-improving search agent needs feedback that co-evolves with the policy it guides.

</details>


### [151] [Strictly Causal Streaming Video Anomaly Detection with a Theoretically-Grounded State-Space Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24810)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出严格因果的视频异常检测流式模型：每帧以O(1)时间和内存更新固定大小状态，不使用未来帧或片段缓存。模型在端侧设备上实现了低时延和高吞吐，但当前准确率低于已有非因果状态空间模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mamba风格状态空间模型的视频异常检测方法通常仍依赖内部片段或时间窗口缓存，难以满足真正在线部署需求；同时，时间记忆与检测延迟之间缺乏理论解释，效率评测也多局限于GPU吞吐，未直接反映目标端侧硬件性能。

Method: 方法采用对角线性状态空间递推作为时间核心，并引入依赖输入和当前状态的衰减门控机制。视觉特征由冻结的视觉骨干网络提取，模型通过因果的下一嵌入预测进行自监督训练。作者推导了递推衰减谱与检测延迟、可可靠捕捉的最短异常持续时间之间的闭式关系，并对衰减率、状态维度和门控模块进行消融分析。

Result: 在UCSD Ped2和CUHK Avenue上，基于学习到的基础衰减得到的稳态延迟理论界为57至59帧，但实测检测延迟仅为1.6帧和18.4帧，表明事件边界处的门控比基础衰减更主导响应速度。在Apple M3 Pro端侧硬件上，端到端单帧时延为0.74毫秒和0.77毫秒，吞吐超过1300 FPS。未经充分调参时，Ped2与Avenue的帧级AUC分别为67.9%和70.2%，落后于已有非因果状态空间模型基线。门控在较小训练集Ped2上降低准确率，但在较大训练集Avenue上带来改善。

Conclusion: 该工作的重要贡献是构建了无需缓冲的严格因果流式检测框架，给出时间状态衰减与检测特性的理论联系，并以真实端侧硬件测量验证了高效性。其主要局限是检测精度尚未达到非因果方法水平，且评测规模有限；后续需优化模型配置、缩小精度差距，并扩展至更大的第三个基准数据集。

Abstract: Recent work has applied Mamba style state space models (SSMs) to video anomaly detection, yet existing approaches still rely on buffering clips or windows internally, lack a theoretical account of how temporal memory relates to detection latency, and benchmark efficiency only through GPU throughput rather than the edge hardware these methods are intended to target. We introduce a strictly causal streaming anomaly detector whose fixed size state is updated in O(1) time and memory per incoming frame, with no lookahead and no clip buffering. Its temporal core is a diagonal linear state space recurrence with an input and state dependent decay gate, trained self supervised through causal next embedding prediction on a frozen visual backbone. We derive a closed form relationship between the recurrence decay spectrum and both detection delay and the shortest anomaly it can reliably capture, then validate empirically on UCSD Ped2 and CUHK Avenue. The settling delay bound predicted from the learned base decay (57 to 59 frames) sits far above the measured detection delay (1.6 and 18.4 frames), showing that the event boundary gate, not the base decay, governs responsiveness. We further report end to end latency and throughput measured directly on Apple M3 Pro hardware, 0.74 ms and 0.77 ms per frame (over 1300 FPS), rather than simulated GPU numbers. With an untuned initial configuration the method reaches 67.9 percent and 70.2 percent frame level AUC on Ped2 and Avenue, trailing prior non causal SSM baselines in accuracy. Ablations over decay rate, state size, and gating reveal that the gate contribution is dataset size dependent, hurting accuracy on the smaller Ped2 training set but helping on the larger Avenue one. Closing this accuracy gap and extending evaluation to a third, larger benchmark are immediate next steps.

</details>


### [152] [Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge for LLM-Based Knowledge Graph QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24824)
*Emanuel Kitzelmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CES-PK（部分知识下的受约束实体选择）框架：先由基于大语言模型的系统生成KGQA候选答案，再利用从问题中提取的轻量级符号约束进行验证、过滤与重排序。核心贡献是在不构造可执行逻辑形式的前提下，提高答案与知识图谱的可验证一致性，并通过三值语义适应知识图谱不完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动KGQA主要存在两类局限：端到端语义解析依赖SPARQL等可执行查询，面对复杂模式和不完整知识图谱时较脆弱；基于LLM推理与生成的方法虽更灵活，却缺少形式化的图谱落地保证。论文旨在引入一种互补的后验证机制，识别并剔除明确不合法的候选实体，同时为保留答案提供可解释的符号证据。

Method: 方法将任务定义为CES-PK：输入为LLM系统产生的候选实体及由问题导出的约束，输出为经验证和排序后的候选集合。约束包括类型约束、关系约束和排除约束。针对开放世界下图谱事实缺失的问题，论文采用“满足、违反、未知”的三值语义：仅当候选明确违反约束时才被过滤；无法在图谱中确认的情况标为未知并予以保留；被满足的约束则作为正向符号证据，用于提升候选排序。实验基于Hetionet生物医学知识图谱开展。

Result: 实验表明，约束过滤能够去除明确无效的候选答案，从而提升精确率；由于未知状态不会被误判为违反，候选不会仅因知识图谱缺失而被剔除，因此召回率得以保持。对于未被过滤的候选，已满足约束还能提供额外的可解释支持信号，并改善其排序。摘要未给出具体数值、基线模型、统计显著性或不同约束组合的定量增益。

Conclusion: CES-PK将LLM候选生成与轻量符号验证结合，避免了完整语义解析的脆弱性，也弥补了纯生成式KGQA缺乏可验证支撑的问题。其关键设计是面向不完整知识图谱的三值约束语义：将“未知”与“违反”区分开，以在提升精确率的同时减少开放世界假设下的错误拒绝。该框架尤其适合作为现有LLM-KGQA系统的后处理验证层。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for knowledge graph question answering (KGQA), but can fail to correctly ground answers in the underlying graph. Current approaches to LLM-based KGQA either rely on full semantic parsing into executable queries such as SPARQL, which is brittle in practice due to complex schemas or incompleteness of real-world KGs, or on LLM-reasoning and answer generation over KGs, which can be more robust but lacks formal guarantees. In this work, we study a complementary setting in which \emph{candidate} answers are generated by an LLM-based system and subsequently verified using lightweight symbolic constraints derived from the question. We introduce \emph{Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge (CES-PK)}, a problem formulation that focuses on eliminating invalid answers and providing symbolic support for valid ones without requiring construction of executable logical forms. To account for incomplete KGs, we employ a three-valued constraint semantics (\emph{satisfied, violated, unknown}) that avoids incorrect rejections under open-world assumptions. To demonstrate the effects of our method, we instantiate this framework over the Hetionet biomedical knowledge graph and evaluate the impact of type, relation, and exclusion constraints. Experiments show that precision improves by filtering invalid candidates, while recall is preserved due to retaining candidates whose constraints are not explicitly violated. Satisfied constraints provide additional positive symbolic evidence to rank remaining candidates.

</details>


### [153] [A Dual-Dimensional LLM Framework for Automated Item Incidental Content Similarity Analysis in Large-Scale Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24825)
*Jing Huang,Jihong Zhang,Hua-Hua Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出由大语言模型驱动的自动试题相似性分析（AISA）框架，以“结构化分解”和“语义关联”两个维度识别试题间由措辞或情境重复引发的非构念相关冗余；验证与仿真表明，该框架优于BLEU和余弦相似度等传统文本指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模测评与自动试题生成扩大了题库规模，也增加了试题在表层表述、情境框架等非构念相关内容上意外重复的风险。这类冗余可能导致局部依赖、影响题目参数解释，并削弱计算机化自适应测验的估计质量。传统指标难以同时刻画试题的结构层次与细粒度语义关系，因此需要更贴近心理测量目标的相似性评估方法。

Method: 研究构建双维AISA框架：其一，利用大语言模型进行结构化分解，将试题拆解为可比较的内容组成；其二，评估这些组成之间的语义关联，以形成相似性指标。随后通过心理测量验证，比较该指标与传统文本相似度指标在识别非构念相关局部依赖、形成一致题目参数分组方面的表现；并在计算机化自适应测验模拟中，将不同相似性约束纳入选题策略，考察能力估计稳定性、偏差与测验效率。

Result: 大语言模型生成的相似性度量与非构念相关局部依赖指标的一致性更高，并能得到更连贯的题目参数分组。在自适应测验模拟中，基于该框架施加相似性约束可提升估计稳定性、降低估计偏差，同时仅带来较小的效率损失；总体效果优于采用BLEU、余弦相似度等常规指标的约束策略。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，基于大语言模型、兼顾结构与语义的试题相似性分析可成为题库治理和自适应测验内容控制的有效工具。其价值在于更准确地识别构念无关的内容重复，从而支持可扩展的题库筛选、内容感知的测验组卷以及更注重受测体验与测量质量的自适应测验。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale assessments and the growing adoption of automatic item generation have intensified concerns about incidental content redundancy, where construct-irrelevant elements such as wording or contextual framing become unintentionally repetitive across items. Traditional similarity metrics like BLEU or cosine similarity, often fail to capture the nuanced structural and semantic layers that drive perceived redundancy simultaneously. This study proposes a dual-dimensional framework for Automated Item Similarity Analysis (AISA) powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), operationalizing similarity through Structured Decomposition and Semantic Relatedness. Psychometric validation indicates that LLM-derived metrics align more closely with indicators of construct-irrelevant local dependence and yield more coherent item parameter groupings than traditional text-based measures. The framework is further evaluated through its application in Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT). Simulations reveal that incorporating LLM-based similarity constraints into item selection improves estimation stability and reduces bias with minimal efficiency trade-offs, outperforming constraints based on conventional metrics. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-powered AISA to support scalable bank curation, content-aware test assembly, and experience-sensitive adaptive testing across diverse assessment contexts.

</details>


### [154] [FedV-KGQA: Multi-Hop Question Answering over Vertically Partitioned Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24846)
*Md Saikat Islam Khan Bappy,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出FedV-KGQA，用于关系被纵向划分于多个机构的知识图谱上的多跳问答。该框架在不共享原始三元组、关系参数且不进行运行时跨机构通信的条件下，实现接近集中式系统的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实知识图谱数据受治理与数据主权约束而分散保存。现有集中式方法依赖统一图访问，难以处理答案所需事实分布在不同关系数据孤岛中的多跳问题；因此需要兼顾隐私边界、跨关系推理能力与部署可行性的方案。

Method: FedV-KGQA面向机构间实体重叠但关系集合互不相交的纵向划分场景。方法结合本地图谱增强与知识图谱嵌入，使原始三元组和关系参数始终保留在各自数据孤岛内，形成结构化数据边界；同时设计主题实体锚定机制，将问题定位到正确的图局部邻域，从而避免推理阶段的跨机构通信。

Result: 作者在三个基准上评估了12种模型配置。结果表明，FedV-KGQA具有较强问答性能，与集中式方法的表现差距较小；其能力可泛化至三跳推理，并且面对嵌入扰动时保持较好的鲁棒性。摘要未提供具体指标、数据集名称及各配置之间的统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，在关系纵向分区的知识图谱环境中，可通过本地增强、嵌入式协同表示及主题实体锚定，在严格限制数据外流和在线交互的前提下完成有效的多跳问答。其核心贡献是将隐私边界与多跳推理相结合；后续仍需依据完整论文检验通信与训练成本、不同划分比例下的性能，以及更复杂开放环境中的适用性。

Abstract: Real-world data for knowledge graph question answering is often distributed across different organizations due to governance and data sovereignty constraints. While centralized systems exist, they cannot answer multi-hop questions when the required facts are split across vertically partitioned silos. In this paper, we propose FedV-KGQA, a framework for multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs in which organizations share entities but own disjoint sets of relations. Our approach combines local graph enrichment and knowledge graph embeddings to ensure raw triples and relation parameters never leave each silo, establishing a structural data boundary without requiring centralized graph access. We further introduce a topic entity anchoring mechanism that grounds questions in the correct graph neighborhood without any runtime inter-silo communication. We evaluate 12 model configurations across three benchmarks and show that FedV-KGQA performs strongly, remains close to centralized performance, generalizes to 3-hop reasoning, and is robust to embedding perturbations.

</details>


### [155] [SPO++: Stream-Aligned Policy Optimization for Asynchronous Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24870)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Qianshan Wei,Ziqi Zhou,Zihe Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SPO++，用于改进单流策略优化（SPO）在长且时长不一的工具调用轨迹上的在线强化学习效率。核心改动是按动作词元测度对终局回报优势进行标准化，并按策略生成事件而非学习器接收顺序组织同一提示的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对强化学习需要等待同一提示的多个并行轨迹，面对长短不一的工具调用过程成本较高。SPO虽以持久化的提示级价值估计消除了该等待依赖，但其“逐轨迹白化一个优势值”与“按词元均值计算演员损失”的组合存在测度不一致：轨迹层面的中心化通常不能保证演员实际使用的词元加权优势量被中心化。

Method: 作者首先指出SPO的归一化对象与演员损失的加权方式不匹配；随后将终局结果产生的优势值依据动作词元测度进行标准化，使优势统计与词元级演员目标一致。其次，针对同一提示的历史证据，按产生该数据的策略事件进行排序和管理，取代按学习器接收数据的先后顺序组织。通过配对消融实验区分各组件贡献。

Result: 在ALFWorld的两种模型规模以及Math-TIR上的匹配实验中，SPO++均比SPO具有更高的在线学习效率。配对消融结果表明，动作词元测度下的优势归一化是所测试改动中贡献最显著的组件。

Conclusion: SPO的关键问题并非仅是优势估计本身，而是优势标准化的统计测度与词元级优化目标不一致。将终局优势按动作词元分布标准化，并以策略事件顺序维护提示证据，可在不依赖同提示兄弟轨迹的前提下稳定提升单流在线强化学习效率。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning waits for sibling rollouts of the same prompt, which is costly for long and variable tool-use trajectories. Single-stream Policy Optimization (SPO) removes this dependency with a persistent prompt-level value estimate, but its recipe whitens one advantage per trajectory before optimizing a token-mean actor loss. We show that trajectory centering generally does not center the token-weighted quantity consumed by the actor, and fix the mismatch by standardizing terminal-outcome advantages under the action-token measure. We additionally organize prompt evidence by the policy event that generated it rather than learner receipt order. Across matched runs on ALFWorld at two model scales and on Math-TIR, SPO++ improves online learning efficiency over SPO. A paired ablation identifies action-token-measure normalization as the strongest tested component.

</details>


### [156] [Recursive Experiential-Working Memory Evolution for Long-Horizon Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24876)
*Zhaochen Yu,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Kaiyuan Chen,Zhe Zhao,Mengdi Wang,Shuicheng Yan,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Recuris，一种递归式经验—工作记忆架构，用于提升智能体在长时程任务中的状态跟踪、技能调用与持续改进能力。该方法在37个已完成的模型—基准组合中有35个提升，并在更长交互任务上呈现更大的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体通常依赖不断增长的完整历史进行决策，容易造成任务状态模糊、技能调用与当前需求错位，进而使错误难以定位和修复。现有递归自我改进机制也缺乏可控、可验证的局部更新路径。

Method: Recuris将记忆划分为工作记忆与经验记忆：工作记忆持续表示当前任务进展，并据此从经验记忆中选择技能；执行过程被组织为可诊断证据，用于将失败归因到具体记忆组件。固定的元智能体依据这些证据，对技能记忆执行局部且经验证门控的更新；更新后的技能影响后续执行并产生新证据，构成有界的递归记忆演化闭环。

Result: 在4个长时程基准和10个模型上，Recuris在37个完成的模型—基准配对中提升了35个。摘要报告：tau-bench上，GPT-5.6 Sol提升17.8个百分点，Claude Opus 5提升15.6个百分点并达到87.9%；SkillFlow上，Qwen3.6-27B和35B分别提升16.6和13.5个百分点。最长任务中的增益达到32.2个百分点，若干常见长时程失败模式最多下降80%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将当前任务状态驱动的技能检索、执行证据归因和验证约束下的局部记忆更新结合起来，可显著增强长时程智能体表现。Recuris为递归自我改进提供了一种更可扩展且相对可控的记忆演化范式，但其泛化性、更新成本及在开放环境中的稳定性仍需结合完整论文实验进一步验证。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) remains hard in long-horizon tasks, where growing histories obscure the task state and misalign skill invocation. We introduce Recuris, a recursive Experiential-Working Memory architecture for long-horizon agent harnesses, in which Working Memory tracks task progress and guides skill selection from Experiential Memory, grounding skill use in current needs rather than the full history. This coupling also turns execution into structured evidence that localizes failures to specific memory components. Across tasks, a fixed Meta-Agent turns that evidence into localized, validation-gated updates to Skill Memory that reshape execution and yield new evidence, forming a bounded recursive memory-evolution loop. Across four long-horizon benchmarks and ten models, Recuris improves task success in 35 of the 37 completed model-benchmark pairs, carrying frontier models to SOTA-level task success: on tau-bench it adds +17.8 points to GPT-5.6 Sol and +15.6 to Claude Opus 5, taking Opus 5 to 87.9%, and +16.6/+13.5 points on Qwen3.6-27B/35B on SkillFlow. The advantage widens as the interaction horizon grows, to +32.2 points on the longest tasks, and common long-horizon failures fall by up to 80%. These results position recursively evolving memory as a scalable foundation for RSI, enabling agents to continuously transform accumulated experience into increasingly effective long-horizon behavior. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/Recuris

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Effective Pivot Attack Detection via System and Network Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23731)
*Ava Powelson,Carson Kuzniar,Hyojoon Kim,Israat Haque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出主机侧实时检测系统Stitch，通过可编程内核、流量观测与进程追踪识别经被攻陷主机转发的通信，从而检测横向跳转行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统边界设备难以发现外观合法的转发流量；现有检测方案还存在处理延迟高、准确率不足或依赖全网部署等问题，因此需要轻量、独立且适用于单主机的检测机制。

Method: Stitch在主机上观测穿越该主机的网络流，并利用进程追踪关联入站与出站通信，将系统层进程信息与网络层流信息结合，提取两类通信间的转发特征并进行实时判定。

Result: 在两项真实环境部署中，Stitch相较现有先进方案将检测准确率提升31%，最高误报率仅为0.006%。

Conclusion: Stitch以较低开销提供了高准确度的主机级转发检测能力，不依赖网络范围协同部署，可弥补现有检测方案在易受影响主机覆盖方面的不足。

Abstract: Perimeter-based security appliances, such as firewalls or Intrusion Detection Systems, are ineffective against modern attacks that use pivoting, wherein attackers "pivot" traffic through compromised hosts to gain access to additional targets that would otherwise be inaccessible. Due to the legitimate appearance of the relayed traffic, pivoting is extremely difficult to detect. Although the consequences of these attacks are known to be severe, existing defenses suffer from drawbacks such as high processing delays, low accuracy, or reliance on network-wide participation, making them inconvenient or even ineffective. This work presents Stitch, a host-based system that uses the programmable kernel to detect pivoting in real time. By observing host-traversing flows, Stitch uses process tracing to effectively combine system and network-level information, connecting incoming and outgoing communications and identifying pivoting characteristics between them. Showing 31% gains in accuracy over state-of-the-art pivoting defenses and a maximum false positive rate of 0.006% over two separate real-world deployments, Stitch covers the gap in current pivot detection solutions by providing accurate, lightweight, and independent coverage for vulnerable hosts in a network.

</details>


### [158] [TrustShiftProbe: Characterizing, Benchmarking, and Defending Staged Trust Attacks on MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23763)
*Mehrdad Rostamzadeh,Sidhant Narula,Mohammad Ghasemigol,Daniel Takabi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustShift攻击：恶意MCP服务器先以正常行为建立代理信任，再在达到特定交互阈值后切换为攻击模式，从而绕过部署前静态分析和部分运行时过滤。实验显示平均攻击成功率为69.5%，SHIELD防御后降至42.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全分析通常假设服务器行为在部署前后保持一致，难以识别具有时间延迟和状态依赖的服务器端攻击。TrustShift利用可信工具通道实施突变，区别于用户提示注入和传输层中间人攻击。

Method: 提出TrustShiftProbe评估与防御框架，包括状态化时序威胁模型、跨四个生产领域的语言无关攻击引擎，以及位于MCP传输边界的多层零预言机防御SHIELD。攻击覆盖结构违规、语义破坏和权限范围扩张三类机制，以及干扰、数据外泄和组合目标。SHIELD依据清洁信任窗口形成的行为基线审计服务器载荷。

Result: 在前沿闭源模型和开放权重模型上，TrustShift取得69.5%的平均攻击成功率；部署SHIELD后，成功率降至42.7%，表明其能够显著缓解但无法完全消除该类攻击。

Conclusion: MCP服务器端的时序性信任转移是独立且重要的安全威胁。静态部署前检测不足以应对服务器后续变节，需要结合生命周期建模、行为基线和传输边界运行时审计来提升代理系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has emerged as the standard layer connecting Large Language Model agents to external tool backends. This openness introduces a severe server-side threat we term TrustShift: a compromised MCP server behaves benignly during an initial conditioning phase, building operational reliance and suppressing agent skepticism, before switching to an adversarial payload once an interaction threshold is reached. The evasion is temporal, not syntactic: benign at deploy time, the server's defection is invisible to predeployment static analysis, which sees only the honest phase. Switched payloads range from overt structural violations to schema-valid manipulations, the latter preserving outer protocol compliance to evade runtime middleware filters. Crucially, TrustShift originates in the server-controlled tool channel, not user prompts (unlike indirect prompt injection) or the transport (unlike man-in-the-middle): the adversary is the trusted server endpoint itself. We introduce TrustShiftProbe, an evaluation and defense framework with four contributions: (1) a stateful temporal threat model of the agent-server lifecycle as a benign conditioning phase followed by an adversarial defection at a trust horizon; (2) a language-agnostic attack engine that instantiates each variant as a compromised MCP server across four production domains; (3) SHIELD, a multi-tier, zero-oracle runtime defense at the MCP transport boundary that audits server payloads against behavioral baselines learned during clean trust windows; and (4) a taxonomy of nine TrustShift variants spanning three execution mechanisms (structural violation, semantic corruption, scope expansion) and three adversarial objectives (disruption, exfiltration, and their combination). Across frontier proprietary and open-weight models, TrustShift attacks achieve a 69.5% mean attack success rate that SHIELD mitigates to 42.7%.

</details>


### [159] [ROBBIN: Rowhammer-Based Backdoor Injection during Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23774)
*Saion K. Roy,Yufei Wang,A. Adam Ding,Yunsi Fei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ROBВIN是一种硬件感知的Rowhammer推理时后门注入攻击，通过结合特定DRAM芯片的实际位翻转模式，提高攻击可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅在算法层面设计位翻转，未考虑硬件实际产生的附带翻转，导致攻击成功率和正常输入测试准确率下降，并且在不同DRAM设备上的表现差异显著。

Method: ROBВIN首先刻画目标DRAM的设备特定位翻转模式，然后迭代选择模型权重的DRAM页映射，使Rowhammer引发的全部位翻转共同服务于后门构造，在提升触发输入攻击成功率的同时保持正常输入测试准确率。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集的ResNet-20和VGG-16模型上，使用三种商用DDR4芯片进行评估，ROBВIN在不同设备上均实现接近90%的攻击成功率，并将测试准确率保持在83%以上。

Conclusion: 将硬件位翻转特性纳入后门设计能够显著提升Rowhammer后门攻击的跨设备稳定性和有效性，验证了硬件感知构造相较于硬件无关方法的优势。

Abstract: Existing Rowhammer-based inference-time backdoor attacks design their bit-flip strategies purely at the algorithmic level, without accounting for the bit-flips that the underlying DRAM hardware will actually produce. This disconnection between the algorithmic backdoor construction and its hardware realization leads to unreliable attack performance, as collateral bit-flips at unintended locations degrade both the attack success rate (ASR) on triggered inputs and the test accuracy (TA) for normal inputs. Consequently, the performance of such attacks varies significantly across different DRAM devices, as each device presents a unique set of exploitable bit-flip locations. This work presents ROBBIN, a hardware-aware Rowhammer-based backdoor injection attack that integrates the device-specific vulnerability into the backdoor construction process. ROBBIN first characterizes the bit-flip patterns of a target DRAM and uses this information to iteratively select DRAM \textit{page} mappings for the model weights that would maximize ASR while preserving TA under Rowhammering. By treating every hammering-induced bit-flip as an integral part of the attack design rather than first constructing a hardware-agnostic backdoor and dismissing collateral flips as side effects, ROBBIN produces backdoors that remain robust across devices. Evaluated on ResNet-20 and VGG-16 with CIFAR-10 across three commodity DDR4 chips, ROBBIN consistently achieves close to 90\% ASR while maintaining TA above 83\%, demonstrating reliable backdoor efficacy across diverse DRAM devices.

</details>


### [160] [A Scenario-Based Evaluation of CRQC+AI Vulnerability Spectrum for TLS 1.3 Cryptographic Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23785)
*Noel Grover,Mussie Haile,Brad Pedersen,Eric Uner,Bradley J Erickson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个证据分层、可复现的量子与人工智能加速风险评估模型，用于分析TLS 1.3密码依赖项及后量子密码迁移时间表。研究未发现ML-KEM、ML-DSA、SLH-DSA或AES-256已被攻破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算可能通过Shor算法威胁RSA和椭圆曲线密码，而后量子密码及对称密码的风险证据仍不充分。本文旨在以明确的情景假设、参数和更新规则，改进密码迁移规划并降低长期保密数据面临的“先窃取、后解密”风险。

Method: 研究采用四种能力情景模型，将风险区分为机制支持的RSA与ECC威胁、基于条件假设的格密码风险，以及仅有假设依据的哈希和对称密码风险。模型为每个参数指定名称、依据和敏感性分析，并提供伪代码、可重复运行机制及基于历史观测修订参数的证伪与更新规则。

Result: 截至2026年年中，模型未显示任何获NIST批准的算法已被攻破。在不同情景下，RSA风险预计于2030至2032年间超过50%；在维数坍缩假设成立的应急情景中，后量子密码风险可能于2032至2035年后出现非零水平。模型结果支持尽快推进后量子密码迁移，并将密码敏捷性和混合密码部署作为配套措施。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是一个可复现、可更新的密码风险情景估计工具，而非对现有后量子算法安全性的直接攻破。研究建议依据既定期限推进迁移，同时部署密码敏捷性和混合方案，以应对量子能力演进及模型假设变化。

Abstract: This paper evaluates quantum and AI-accelerated risks to TLS 1.3 cryptographic dependencies under an evidence-tiered model, distinguishing mechanism-backed threats (Shor algorithm against RSA and ECC) from contingency-backed risks to lattice-based post-quantum cryptography (PQC) and hypothesis-only risks to hash-based and symmetric primitives. We do not identify any known breaks of ML-KEM, ML-DSA, SLH-DSA, or AES-256. Instead, we use explicit scenario assumptions, organized as a four-scenario capability model with parameters and pseudocode for reproducibility, to stress-test migration timelines accompanied by parameter sensitivity analysis and explicit falsification analysis. The primary methodological contribution is a reproducible scenario-estimation instrument together with its explicit update mechanics: every parameter is a named, anchored quantity that can be varied and the model rerun; a stated protocol maps observed conformance to, or deviation from, the modeled curves onto revisions of specific parameters, so progressive refinements can be tested against accumulating historical data. The paper is a methodological companion to quantum resource-estimation studies and to expert-elicitation timeline surveys such as the Global Risk Institute quantum threat reports, with its revision rules stated explicitly. As of mid-2026, the model does not show any NIST-approved algorithms as broken. Instead, the vulnerability spectrum under different scenarios shows RSA risk crossing the 50% threshold between 2030-2032 and the PQC risk becoming a non-zero risk after 2032-2035 under contingency scenarios conditional on the unproven dimension-collapse. We urge PQC migration as mandatory per the 2030 and 2031 federal deadlines and by Mosca HNDL reasoning, and that crypto-agility and hybrid cryptographic deployment be considered necessary complements to any PQC migration efforts.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond the Mandate: A Systematic Security Analysis of the Agent Payments Protocol (AP2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23858)
*Avital Aviv,Parth A. Gandh,Ron Bitton,Asaf Shabtai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Google推出的Agent Payments Protocol（AP2）v0.2进行了系统安全分析，发现签名后的结账和支付授权仅能保护授权后的交易数据，无法保证授权前形成交易意图的上下文未被篡改。研究识别出48项威胁，其中8项在至少一种部署架构下达到高风险，并通过测试床、概念验证和扫描器验证了相关风险及缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AP2支持由大语言模型驱动的购物代理代表用户授权和执行支付。尽管v0.2修复了部分已知问题，但其新增能力、部署假设以及授权前的A2A消息和MCP工具调用仍缺乏完整保护，因此需要重新评估其安全性。

Method: 研究基于AP2的角色、交易生命周期、部署架构和信任边界开展系统分析，将生命周期划分为五个阶段，并识别五种部署架构。采用MAESTRO方法建模四类威胁主体、十一个攻击面、十八种对手能力和六类攻击目标，形成涵盖五个攻击家族的48项威胁目录。进一步使用AIVSS进行风险评分，构建覆盖全部架构的测试床，设计五个概念验证，并开发能够执行静态检查、跨角色一致性检查和对抗性检查的部署感知扫描器。

Result: 共有8项威胁在至少一种部署架构中达到高风险等级。五个概念验证覆盖了全部高风险威胁及其缓解措施，扫描器能够依据具体部署架构匹配适用威胁并开展自动化检查。结果表明，AP2的签名授权机制无法防止授权前上下文受到操纵。

Conclusion: 有效的授权签名并不等于交易体现了用户真实意图。AP2的安全性依赖于对授权前交互、外部输入、跨角色一致性和具体部署架构的整体保护；实际部署应结合威胁建模、针对性缓解措施和部署感知安全检测。

Abstract: The Agent Payments Protocol (AP2), introduced by Google, enables large language model (LLM)-driven shopping agents to authorize and execute payments on behalf of users. Its signed Checkout and Payment Mandates protect the integrity of transaction data after signing. Agent interactions and external inputs that shape a transaction before authorization remain outside that protection, including Agent-to-Agent Protocol (A2A) messages and Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool calls. Prior work identified replay and prompt-injection attacks in AP2 v0.1. AP2 v0.2 addresses some of these issues but adds capabilities and deployment assumptions that require renewed analysis. We present a systematic security analysis of AP2 v0.2 based on its roles, transaction lifecycle, deployment architectures, and trust boundaries. We divide the lifecycle into five phases and identify five deployment architectures. Using MAESTRO (Multi-Agent Environment, Security, Threat, Risk, Outcome), we model four threat actors, eleven attack surfaces, eighteen adversary capabilities, and six attacker goals. The resulting catalog contains 48 threats spanning five attack families. We score these threats with the Artificial Intelligence Vulnerability Scoring System (AIVSS), identifying eight that reach the High band in at least one architecture. Because no complete public AP2 deployment was available, we build a testbed spanning all five architectures and develop five proof-of-concept demonstrations covering all eight High-risk threats and their mitigations. We also develop a deployment-aware scanner that maps applicable threats to static, cross-role consistency, and adversarial checks. Our analysis shows that valid mandate signatures alone do not ensure that an agent-mediated transaction reflects the user's intent when its pre-authorization context is manipulated.

</details>


### [162] [SoK: ARCUS: On the Efficiency and Efficacy of Hardware Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23933)
*Alenkruth Krishnan Murali,Raghul Saravanan,Sai Manoj P D,Ashish Venkat*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了ISA、微架构和RTL三个抽象层次上的硬件模糊测试技术，比较其输入激励、变异策略、反馈机制、目标平台、参考模型和覆盖率等关键因素，并总结现有不足与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同硬件抽象层在测试目标、约束条件和设计权衡方面存在显著差异，现有研究缺乏统一的系统比较。本文旨在梳理各层次模糊测试实践，识别智能输入生成、可扩展参考模型、有效反馈机制及跨层协同等方面的关键需求。

Method: 围绕ISA、微架构和RTL三个层次，对硬件模糊测试方法进行综合分析，重点考察输入刺激质量、变异方法、反馈机制、目标平台、黄金参考模型和覆盖率等维度，并据此归纳不同层次的挑战、目标与设计取舍。

Result: 研究发现，各抽象层次的模糊测试在测试目标和技术权衡上差异明显。当前仍存在输入生成智能化程度不足、黄金参考模型难以可靠扩展、反馈通道表达能力有限以及跨层集成不足等问题。

Conclusion: 未来可重点发展混合模糊测试框架、人工智能辅助测试生成、可扩展参考模型、标准化评估指标与基准，以及人机协同的自动化探索和分析方法，从而提升硬件验证的效率、可靠性和覆盖全面性。

Abstract: This work presents a comprehensive analysis of contemporary hardware fuzzing techniques applied across three major abstraction layers: Instruction Set Architecture (ISA), microarchitecture, and Register-Transfer Level (RTL). Our study examines key factors including input stimulus quality, mutation strategies, feedback mechanisms, target platforms, reference models, and achieved coverage. We find challenges, goals, and design trade-offs vary significantly across abstraction layers. We further identify several unmet needs in current hardware fuzzing practices, such as intelligent input generation, reliable and scalable golden reference models, expressive feedback channels, and cross-layer integration. Building on these insights, we outline future research directions, including hybrid fuzzing frameworks, AI-assisted test generation, scalable reference models, standardized evaluation metrics and benchmarks, and human-in-the-loop automation for guided exploration and analysis. Together, they aim to unlock efficient, reliable, and comprehensive hardware verification solutions.

</details>


### [163] [STAIN-FL: Stealthy Targeted Attack Injection with Contextual Triggers in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23952)
*Ashlinder Kaur,Purnima Murali Mohan,Zengxiang Li,Tram Truong-Huu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出STAIN-FL，用于在联邦视频异常检测中实施隐蔽、具有情境触发特征的定向后门攻击，并在保持模型干净性能的同时提高触发条件下的误分类率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习服务器无法直接观察客户端原始视频和完整训练过程，恶意客户端可能通过更新注入后门。现有高强度攻击容易被检测，因此需要兼顾隐蔽性、持久性和攻击效果的方法。

Method: STAIN-FL利用低照度、室内场景和人群密度等自然监控条件作为上下文触发器，将异常类别操纵为良性类别，并对更新最少的参数坐标实施梯度掩蔽，以降低对正常样本性能的影响。实验采用UCF-Crime数据集的1024维I3D特征，在非独立同分布的四客户端环境下比较FedAvg和FedProx，并评估稀疏攻击与持续攻击。

Result: 稀疏攻击的平均干净准确率下降低于2%，但在峰值后门准确率下，FedAvg和FedProx分别可使超过一半的触发异常被误分类，后门准确率最高达到56.7%和54.2%。在FedAvg下，稀疏后门攻击在攻击结束后仍有较长持续性，超过25%后门准确率的平均持续时间为336轮。

Conclusion: STAIN-FL表明，结合自然监控条件、标签操纵和梯度掩蔽的稀疏后门攻击能够在低可检测性的同时实现显著且持久的模型操控效果。联邦视频异常检测系统需要加强客户端更新审计、异常触发检测和跨轮次后门持久性监测。

Abstract: Federated video anomaly detection trains model collaboratively without sharing raw surveillance footage, but limited server-side visibility lets compromised clients to inject backdoor via malicious updates. This paper introduces STAIN-FL, a stealthy targeted backdoor attack injection framework that uses naturally occurring surveillance conditions, including low-light scenes, indoor settings, and crowd density, as contextual triggers. STAIN-FL combines anomaly-to-benign label \textit{manipulation} with gradient masking over least-updated coordinates to preserve clean accuracy while inducing trigger-conditioned misclassification. We evaluate STAIN-FL on \texttt{UCF-Crime} using 1024-dimensional I3D features in a non-IID four-client multi-agency setting, comparing FedAvg and FedProx under sparse and continuous attacks. Results show that sparse attacks have low-detectability, operationally significant attacks rather than high-intensity attacks: they keep the mean clean-accuracy drop below $2\%$, yet still misclassify more than half of triggered anomalies at peak backdoor accuracy under FedAvg ($56.7\%$) and FedProx ($54.2\%$). Under FedAvg, the sparse backdoor remains above the $25\%$ backdoor-accuracy threshold for an average of $336$ post-attack rounds, highlighting the persistence risk of contextually triggered attacks in surveillance systems.

</details>


### [164] [NeuronGuard: Robust LLM Safety Alignment via Ablation-Aware Safety Signal Redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23959)
*Anjun Gao,Yueyang Quan,Yufei Xia,Zhuqing Liu,Minghong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NeuronGuard是一种训练阶段防御方法，通过分散安全信号、增强神经元冗余，同时抵御提示词越狱攻击与神经元级攻击，并尽量保持下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的安全信息集中于少量关键神经元，导致模型容易受到构造提示绕过和部署后神经元剪除等攻击。

Method: NeuronGuard利用定期更新的逐层线性分类器动态识别安全关键神经元；通过在神经元消融条件下强制模型拒答，促使安全信号扩散至更多神经元；结合KL散度正则保持输出分布一致，并采用随机梯度投影协调防御目标与下游任务目标之间的冲突。

Result: 理论分析表明，该方法能够严格降低攻击成功率上界。实验覆盖3种大语言模型、6种先进攻击策略及多模态场景，在白盒自适应攻击下仍实现接近零的攻击成功率，同时保持任务准确率。

Conclusion: NeuronGuard通过提升安全表示的分布性与冗余性，统一增强了模型对多类攻击的鲁棒性，并在安全性与任务实用性之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) remains brittle against a growing spectrum of attacks. Jailbreak attacks bypass safety mechanisms through crafted prompts, while neuron-level attacks directly prune safety-critical neurons post-deployment. Both exploit a common weakness: safety-relevant information concentrates in a sparse neuron subset. We present NeuronGuard, a fine-tuning-stage defense that simultaneously hardens LLMs against both attack classes by redistributing safety signals across a broader set of neurons. NeuronGuard dynamically identifies safety-critical neurons via periodically refreshed per-layer linear classifiers, forces refusal behavior under deliberate neuron ablation, and applies KL-divergence regularization for distributional consistency. A randomized gradient projection strategy preserves downstream task utility by resolving conflicts between the defense and task objectives. We provide a formal guarantee that NeuronGuard strictly reduces the attack success rate (ASR) upper bound, and experiments across three LLMs, six state-of-the-art attack strategies, and multimodal settings confirm near-zero ASR while maintaining task accuracy, including against white-box adaptive adversaries.

</details>


### [165] [RAGSentinel: Certifiable Geometric Consensus for Robust Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23965)
*Yueyang Quan,Anjun Gao,Yufei Xia,Minghong Fang,Zhuqing Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出RAGSentinel，一种面向黑盒检索增强生成系统的免训练、无标签文档投毒防御方法。其核心是在代理编码器的表征空间中检测检索文档对查询条件隐藏状态造成的异常偏移，并基于鲁棒多数共识过滤可疑文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG虽然可通过外部文档提升回答事实性，但攻击者能够向知识库注入对抗性文档，使其被检索后诱导模型输出指定错误答案。已有检索后防御通常依赖模型遵循指令、内部参数知识或文本一致性，因此可能被能够模仿或针对这些机制优化的自适应攻击绕过。

Method: RAGSentinel不依赖目标大模型的内部访问、额外训练数据或投毒标签。方法使用代理编码器衡量每篇检索文档相对于查询的隐藏表征变化；随后去除不同文档间共享的主题方向，以降低正常语义相关性带来的干扰；最后依据诚实文档多数形成的鲁棒几何共识，将表征偏移显著异常的文档识别并过滤。理论上，论文在诚实样本占多数且正常与投毒表征满足可分离条件时，证明该方法可精确恢复一个无投毒的多数规模上下文集合。

Result: 在三个问答数据集、三类大语言模型以及多种文档投毒攻击上的实验表明，该方法可持续降低攻击成功率，同时维持具有竞争力的问答准确率。即使攻击者了解完整防御流程并据此进行自适应优化，方法仍表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将RAG投毒检测从文本内容和目标模型行为层面转向查询条件下的表征几何异常检测，提供了适用于黑盒场景的轻量级检索后防御思路。其有效性依赖于诚实文档多数及投毒与正常文档在代理表征空间中的可分性；若这两个前提不成立，实际鲁棒性可能下降。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves the factuality of large language models by grounding responses in external documents, but it also exposes a critical security vulnerability: adversarial documents injected into the knowledge database can enter the context window and steer the model toward targeted incorrect answers. Existing post-retrieval defenses rely on instruction following, parametric knowledge, or text-level consistency, all of which can be imitated or optimized against by adaptive attackers. We propose RAGSentinel, a training-free, label-free defense for black-box RAG systems. RAGSentinel uses a surrogate encoder to measure query-conditioned hidden-state shifts induced by retrieved documents, removes shared topic directions, and filters poisoned documents as geometric outliers from a robust majority consensus. We prove that, under an honest-majority assumption and a representation-level separation condition, RAGSentinel exactly recovers a poison-free majority-sized context. Experiments across three question-answering datasets, three LLM families, and multiple poisoning attacks show that RAGSentinel consistently achieves low attack success rates while preserving competitive accuracy and remaining effective against adaptive attacks with full pipeline knowledge.

</details>


### [166] [WebMCP-Phalanx: Enforcing and Characterizing Trust Boundaries for Browser-Integrated LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24017)
*Lin-Fa Lee,YI-YU Chang,Kuo-Hui Yeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: The emerging W3C WebMCP proposal enables LLM agents to invoke tools exposed by web pages. In multi-party web environments, however, integrating agent execution into a browser security model centered on the Same-Origin Policy (SOP) leaves insufficient provenance and lifecycle guarantees for agent-accessible tools, creating three risks: subject-attribution spoofing, uncontrolled tool lifecycles, and semantic prompt injection. We propose WebMCP-Phalanx, a dual-layer agent runtime architecture. Its first layer provides a browser-native trust anchor that binds each tool to its registering principal through cryptographically protected capability credentials and propagates provenance labels throughout the tool lifecycle. Its second layer separates semantic inspection from privileged tool use. A Quarantine Agent (Q-LLM), without tool invocation authority, inspects tool metadata, outputs, and page-supplied content for prompt injection. Validated content is then forwarded to a Privileged Agent (P-LLM) for execution, while the Q-LLM's internal state remains hidden from page scripts. Empirical evaluation shows that the browser-native ownership mechanism reduces revocation and overwrite attack success from 100\% to 0\%. The dual-agent runtime blocks all 80 prompt-injection attempts embedded in tool descriptions and limits tool-return attacks to 2 successful cases out of 80. Across experiments, task utility remains statistically indistinguishable from the no-attack baseline. Under a white-box adaptive attacker, however, description-based filtering can be bypassed through malicious tool names invoked before inspection. This finding motivates a call-timing gate that delays tool invocation until all agent-visible tool metadata has been validated.

</details>


### [167] [What Guides the Agent? Adjudicating Unauthorized Behavior via Localizing Behavior-Guiding Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24022)
*Yichao Gao,Yumo Zhang,Yunhao Yao,Haohua Du,Puhan Luo,Ruiqi Li,Zhiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Attnlocate：一种面向LLM智能体的运行时防护框架，通过分析注意力矩阵中的激活轨迹，定位真正影响工具调用决策的上下文片段，并结合来源权限判定可疑调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接入外部资源的LLM智能体共享自然语言上下文通道，不可信数据可能在推理时被解析为指令，从而干扰工具调用决策。现有输入输出层的静态检测或隔离方法难以识别推理过程中动态形成的行为诱导内容。

Method: 作者将行为引导指令的定位建模为一维目标检测问题：首先聚合多头、多层注意力信息，构造面向词元的特征空间；随后使用带无锚检测头的1-D U-Net识别影响决策的连续文本跨度；最后依据检测片段所属内容提供方的权限等级，对工具调用请求进行动态裁决。

Result: 在覆盖5个LLM家族、10种智能体配置的评测中，Attnlocate针对间接提示注入和工具投毒场景取得0.743的平均IoU、0.956的平均AUROC，以及在0.067假阳性率下0.934的真阳性率。该方法还能迁移至未见模型，并可在不重新训练的情况下适配权限策略。

Conclusion: Attnlocate表明，注意力模式可作为识别行为引导上下文的有效运行时信号。通过将细粒度定位与来源权限结合，框架相较仅依赖静态内容审查的方案，更适合保护具有工具调用能力的LLM智能体。

Abstract: LLM agents integrated with external resources gain complex task capabilities, yet the unified natural-language context channel makes them vulnerable to injection attacks: untrusted external data may be dynamically parsed as behavior-guiding instructions during LLM inference, thereby subverting the agent's decision. Existing defenses focus on static detection or isolation of malicious content at the input/output level, remains insufficient for detecting such dynamic inducements that arise during model reasoning. We propose Attnlocate, a runtime framework for fine-grained localization of context spans that genuinely influence tool-calling decisions, i.e., behavior-guiding instructions. Attnlocate casts this localization problem as an object detection task, aiming to detect the distinctive activation traces induced by behavior-guiding instructions within the attention matrix. Specifically, we design a multi-head, multi-layer attention aggregation scheme to construct a token-level feature space tailored for object detection. Then, a 1-D U-Net equipped with an anchor-free detection head is deployed to detect these spans. Finally, based on the authority of the provider from which the detected behavior-guiding spans originate, Attnlocate dynamically adjudicates malicious invocation attempts. We evaluate Attnlocate across ten agent configurations from five LLM families, covering scenarios involving indirect prompt injection and tool poisoning. Attnlocate achieves a mean IoU of 0.743, an average AUROC of 0.956, and a 0.934 true-positive rate at 0.067 false-positive rate. It also transfers effectively across unseen models and supports authority policy adaptation without retraining.

</details>


### [168] [Anatomy of a Scam Call: What 10,000 real scam and spam calls reveal about how phone scammers operate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24127)
*Ethan Traister,Ankit Raj,Jiaqi Gan,Xingyu Shen,Tyler Wu,Yuchen Zhou,Tommy Duong,Kidus Zewde,Siying Chen,Simiao Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Telephone fraud is pervasive and costly, but its inner workings are rarely observed at scale. We analyze a complete corpus of 10,211 inbound scam and spam calls -- 913 hours of audio and 330,956 transcribed turns from 5,780 distinct numbers -- collected over 54 days by an AI voice-agent honeypot that answered callers and kept them talking, and introduced in a companion data descriptor. We separate outright scams, which solicit sensitive information, from the larger stream of predatory but legal lead generation ("spam") that feeds them. Scam operations keep office hours (6.6x more calls per weekday than weekend day); thousands of disposable numbers run a small catalog of recycled scripts (thirty opening clusters, half the traffic in the top five); and callers solicit identity anchors -- a home address and a date of birth -- far more often than payment credentials, pressing through persistence and manufactured authority rather than overt threats. Our central experiment asks: does it matter who picks up? Every seeded lead carried one of ten fictitious identities drawn uniformly at random, so the identity a fraud operation reaches is fixed before the caller exists. Across 1,823 randomized calls, scammers spent about 15% more conversational turns per decade of the target's apparent age (rate ratio 1.15, 95% CI 1.08-1.23; randomization p = 0.005) -- yet what they asked for did not change (26.3% of calls reached a request for sensitive information; odds ratio 0.99 per decade, 95% CI 0.90-1.08). A second experiment casts early detection as a benchmark: from a scammer's opening lines alone, on a caller-disjoint split, escalation is predictable at 0.72 ROC-AUC from the first line and 0.87 by the eighth, and a plain bag-of-words classifier matches a fine-tuned on-device language model. Telephone fraud emerges as a templated industry that varies how hard it works a target, but not what it wants.

</details>


### [169] [Not All Tokens Are Equal: Region-Aware Consistency Repair of Backdoors in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24354)
*Jiali Wei,Ming Fan,Mingkun Zhang,Haoyu Wang,Jun Sun,Guoheng Sun,Xiaoning Ren,Haijun Wang,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出RACER，一种面向多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的模型级后门修复框架。其利用后门导致的跨层表征演化异常，在仅使用100个干净样本、无需已知触发器或攻击目标的条件下显著降低攻击成功率，同时保持正常任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型级后门清除方法主要针对传统分类器，难以有效处理视觉与文本表征深度融合的MLLM；而现有MLLM防御多在推理阶段过滤可疑输入，不能消除模型内部潜伏的后门。因此，需要一种直接修复模型参数与内部表征、并适用于图像、文本及跨模态触发模式的方法。

Method: RACER基于如下观察：后门会造成隐藏表征在相邻网络层之间出现异常的方向性变化，即“逐层不一致性异常”。该异常具有模态依赖性，通常集中于模型实际用于识别触发特征的视觉或文本标记区域。方法先将融合表征拆分为视觉标记区与文本标记区，分别归一化其逐层不一致性；再在深层窗口内按模态感知权重重新组合，构造区域感知不一致性目标。随后通过最小—最大优化生成最坏情况扰动，并以该扰动进行对抗微调，从而抑制支撑后门行为的深层表征偏移并完成模型修复。

Result: 在3个开源MLLM、共36种后门配置上，RACER覆盖图像触发、文本触发与多模态触发情形。实验显示，其平均攻击成功率降至1.1%，其中32种配置达到0%；同时，对原本带后门模型及干净模型均能较好保持正常任务效用。

Conclusion: RACER将MLLM后门防御从输入过滤推进到模型级修复。其核心贡献是利用模态分区后的逐层表征不一致性定位并削弱局部后门依赖，在极少干净数据和未知后门条件下取得较强的通用修复效果。摘要所述结果表明，该方法兼顾后门清除能力与干净性能保持，适合作为MLLM部署前或维护阶段的模型安全修复手段。

Abstract: MLLMs are increasingly deployed in user-facing applications, yet they inherit backdoor risks from the pipelines used to construct them: triggers may reside in images, texts, or both. Existing model-level backdoor removal methods, largely designed for conventional classifiers, show limited effectiveness on MLLMs, while MLLM-specific defenses mainly operate at inference time, filtering suspicious inputs without removing the backdoor embedded in the model. To address this gap and eliminate latent backdoors from MLLMs at their source, we present RACER, a model-level repair framework motivated by a key observation: backdoors induce abnormal layer-to-layer evolution in internal representations, which we term the layer-wise inconsistency anomaly. Importantly, this anomaly is modality-dependent, concentrating primarily in the token region encoding the trigger features that the backdoor model actually relies on. RACER therefore decomposes the fused representation into visual and textual token regions, normalizes their layer-wise inconsistency separately, and recomposes them using modality-aware weights over a deep-layer window, yielding a region-aware inconsistency objective that better captures localized backdoor-induced anomalies. Through a min-max optimization, this objective drives worst-case perturbation synthesis and adversarial fine-tuning against the resulting perturbation to repair the model, suppressing the deep representational directional shifts on which backdoor behaviors rely. RACER requires only 100 clean samples and no knowledge of the trigger, attack objective, or even whether the input model contains a backdoor. Evaluations on three open-source MLLMs across 36 backdoor settings spanning image, text, and multimodal triggers show that RACER reduces the average ASR to 1.1%, reaching 0% in 32 settings, while preserving clean-task utility on both backdoor and clean models.

</details>


### [170] [A Drop-in KEM Replacement for Client Signatures in Post-Quantum SSH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24447)
*Hongbo Liu,Yufan Su,Jiangxia Ge,Qionglu Zhang,Zhaoxuan Li,Xianhui Lu,Li Song,Wenhua Gao,Li Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可直接集成到SSH的、基于KEM的用户认证方法，以会话绑定的挑战—响应证明替代客户端公钥签名。在OpenSSH与liboqs上的实现和评测表明，该方案在典型网络条件下可与紧凑型签名认证竞争，并相对大签名混合基线降低最高约10%的中位握手时延，同时显著降低部分配置下的传输与服务端计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSH的后量子迁移目前主要集中于密钥交换，用户认证仍多采用将传统签名直接替换为后量子签名的路线。该路线保留既有公钥凭证模式，但后量子签名的密钥、签名尺寸及计算成本可能增加认证流量、握手时延和服务端负载。由于SSH用户认证层具有可扩展性、与传输层密钥交换和主机认证相分离、且已处于受保护信道内，因此适合探索以KEM替代签名的认证机制。

Method: 作者设计了一个可插拔的KEM用户认证方法：客户端仍使用公钥作为凭证，但不再生成签名，而是完成与当前会话绑定的挑战—响应式证明。设计保持SSH现有用户认证框架和公钥凭证模型，可与已有认证方式并行部署。论文在后量子ACCE安全框架下给出归约式安全论证，并基于OpenSSH和liboqs完成实现；实验覆盖不同往返时延、TCP初始拥塞窗口设置及多种后量子迁移组合。

Result: 实验显示，KEM认证在代表性网络设置下与紧凑型签名认证具有竞争力。相对于采用大尺寸后量子签名的混合认证基线，中位握手时延最高降低约10%。在较小TCP初始窗口下，相比ML-DSA，中位时延最高降低7.3%；相比SLH-DSA，最高降低17.9%。在相同NIST安全类别中，服务端在线密码学计算成本比ML-DSA低59.1%。

Conclusion: KEM可成为SSH后量子用户认证中签名替换路线的有效补充。该方案无需改变SSH的公钥凭证使用方式，支持渐进部署，并在签名尺寸或计算代价成为瓶颈时表现出更好的网络与服务端效率。其价值主要在于为SSH后量子迁移提供了兼顾安全性、兼容性和性能的认证选项，而非完全取代所有基于签名的认证机制。

Abstract: The transition to post-quantum cryptography is reshaping the Secure Shell (SSH) protocol for remote administration. Post-quantum key exchange has been deployed in OpenSSH and is being standardized, while SSH authentication largely remains a signature-replacement effort. This path preserves the familiar public-key credential model, but inherits the size and computation overhead of post-quantum signatures, which can increase latency, traffic, and server-side load. KEM-based authentication offers a natural alternative to this signature-centric path, and SSH makes this especially attractive at the user-authentication layer, which is method-extensible, separated from transport-layer key exchange and host-key authentication, and already protected by the established channel.
  We present a drop-in KEM-based user-authentication method for SSH that replaces client public-key signatures with a session-bound challenge-response proof. The method fits into SSH's existing user-authentication framework, preserving the public-key credential model and enabling incremental deployment alongside existing methods. We provide a reduction-based security argument in the post-quantum ACCE framework, implement the design in OpenSSH using liboqs, and evaluate it under representative RTTs, TCP initial-window settings, and post-quantum migration configurations. Our results show that KEM-based authentication is competitive with compact signature-based authentication under representative network settings, while reducing median handshake latency by up to about 10% against large-signature hybrid baselines. The advantages are clearer when post-quantum signatures stress transmission or computation: median latency under small TCP initial windows falls by up to 7.3% versus ML-DSA and 17.9% versus SLH-DSA, while server-side online cryptographic cost is 59.1% lower than that for ML-DSA in the same NIST category.

</details>


### [171] [Defending Network Intrusion Detection Systems Based on Graph Neural Networks Against Structural Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24454)
*Dimitri Galli,Andrea Venturi,Dario Stabili,Mauro Andreolini,Mirco Marchetti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向图神经网络网络入侵检测系统的结构对抗训练框架，通过生成模拟边注入的对抗流量样本，提升E-GraphSAGE在正常图与受结构扰动图上的检测性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN可同时利用网络流特征与拓扑关系，适合网络入侵检测；但尽管其对基于特征的对抗扰动较稳健，仍易受边注入、节点插入等结构扰动影响。现有防御方法往往依赖不符合实际部署条件的假设，因此需要更贴近真实网络流场景的防御机制。

Method: 方法以E-GraphSAGE为基础分类器，采用对抗训练。其关键是对良性网络流中的源节点和目的节点进行策略性替换，构造可高效模拟图中边注入效果的对抗样本；随后利用原始样本与结构对抗样本共同训练检测器，并在CTU-13和TON-IoT数据集上评估。

Result: 实验表明，经该方法训练的检测器在干净图上的检测表现优于对照方案，同时在结构对抗扰动下具有更强鲁棒性。摘要未提供具体的准确率、F1值、攻击成功率或性能提升幅度，因此尚无法量化其收益。

Conclusion: 该工作将结构攻击建模为对网络流端点的替换，并以此实施对抗训练，提供了一种计算上较高效、且更符合网络流图构建方式的GNN检测器加固路径。其主要价值在于兼顾正常场景检测能力与结构扰动鲁棒性；后续仍应考察对不同图构建策略、攻击强度、未知攻击类型及跨数据集迁移条件的泛化能力。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) represent a promising solution for Machine Learning (ML) based Network Intrusion Detection Systems (NIDS), thanks to their ability to leverage both network flow features and topological patterns. While GNN classifiers demonstrate superior robustness against feature-based adversarial attacks compared to other ML detectors, they remain vulnerable to structural adversarial attacks, where an attacker perturbs the underlying network graph topology by injecting edges or inserting nodes. Such attacks pose a realistic and severe threat, undermining the reliability of GNN-based NIDS in practical deployments. While countermeasures have been proposed in the literature, they often rely on assumptions that are unrealistic in real-world cybersecurity scenarios. In this paper, we propose a defense framework based on adversarial training to strengthen GNN-based NIDS against structural attacks.
