We generate adversarial samples by strategically replacing the source and destination nodes in benign network flows, thereby efficiently mimicking edge injection attacks. We evaluate our approach on two widely used datasets (CTU-13 and TON-IoT) using E-GraphSAGE as the base GNN classifier. Experimental results show that our approach produces hardened detectors with superior detection performance on clean graphs and enhanced robustness against structural adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [172] [Do System Prompts Leave Behavioral Fingerprints? A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Clone Detection via Output Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24461)
*Linghan Chen,Yudong Gao,Jiyao Wang,Kaiyan Ji,Honglong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出黑盒行为指纹（BBF），用于验证可疑大语言模型部署是否复用了某一系统提示词。方法仅需API访问，在同模型场景下检测效果较好，但跨模型泛化受限，且对针对性的风格前缀攻击脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示词可能被高成功率提取并被零成本复用，但提示词所有者缺乏识别疑似克隆部署的验证手段。现有需求是在不访问模型参数、训练数据或内部提示词的条件下，建立可操作的归属检测机制。

Method: BBF先从目标部署对一组查询的输出中登记行为签名，再将可疑部署的输出与该签名及无关基线进行比较，判断其是否具有更高相似性。研究在4个模型家族、8个基准上收集28.8万条响应，评估同模型与跨模型检测、提示词释义改写、非完美提取和风格前缀扰动；并提出零成本的诊断查询优化策略以选择更具区分力的查询。

Result: 提示词选择可解释24.4%的输出方差。同模型检测的AUC达到0.876。跨模型检测明显依赖检测器身份：非对角AUC从Claude检测器的0.845到Qwen检测器的0.665，平均为0.725。对非自适应提示词释义改写具有较强鲁棒性，AUC不低于0.889，对不完全提取也较稳健。诊断查询优化可使跨模型AUC提升0.120。但在短且结构化的输出任务中，单句正式文风前缀可使MNLI检测AUC由0.978降至0.547。

Conclusion: BBF证明了基于输出行为的黑盒系统提示词归属检测具有可行性，尤其适用于同模型或行为相近的部署。然而，其跨模型稳定性有限，并易受刻意风格操控影响。未来核心问题是构建对表层文体变化不敏感、能够捕捉提示词语义和决策行为本质的风格不变检测方法。

Abstract: System prompts can be extracted from commercial LLMs with over 80\% success and redeployed at zero cost, yet a prompt owner has no way to verify whether a suspected deployment is a clone. We propose Black-Box Behavioral Fingerprinting (BBF): the prompt owner registers a behavioral signature from model outputs and later tests whether a suspect deployment matches that signature more closely than an unrelated baseline. BBF requires only black-box API access. Through a large-scale study (4 model families, 8 benchmarks, 288{,}000 responses), we find that prompt choice explains 24.4\% of output variance and same-model detection reaches AUC 0.876. Cross-model performance is bounded by detector identity, with off-diagonal AUC ranging from 0.845 (Claude as detector) down to 0.665 (Qwen) and overall mean 0.725. BBF resists non-adaptive prompt paraphrasing (AUC $\geq 0.889$) and is robust to imperfect extraction, but a single-sentence formal-tone prefix can collapse detection on short structured outputs (MNLI 0.978 $\to$ 0.547), isolating style-invariant detection as the key open problem. Diagnostic Query Optimization, a zero-cost query selection rule, adds $+0.120$ to cross-model AUC.

</details>


### [173] [SeriCrypt: An LLM-Driven Context-Aware Serialization Framework for Cryptographic Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24498)
*Maosong Chen,Xi Chen,Mengcheng Ju,Dongliang Zhao,Chunxiang Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SeriCrypt：一种由大语言模型驱动、面向密码协议的上下文感知序列化框架。它从非结构化协议规范中提取字段约束、跨消息状态依赖和密码计算规则，并自动构造语法正确且密码学有效的消息序列，用于状态机学习、一致性测试与模糊测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码协议的消息构造不仅要求满足语法，还需保持跨消息状态一致性、正确调用密码原语并满足密钥与认证数据之间的依赖关系。现有自动化方法主要适用于明文或文本协议，密码协议测试通常依赖人工构造报文，因此需要一种可从规范中提取规则并自动生成有效消息的通用机制。

Method: SeriCrypt首先使用大语言模型从协议规范中抽取字段约束、状态依赖和密码计算规则，并将其统一表示为密码协议领域专用语言CDSL。随后，协议无关执行引擎解析CDSL声明，自动完成字段取值解析、密码原语调用及字节流序列化。论文进一步将该框架用于构造违反规范安全约束的测试消息，并作为模糊测试中的有效输入生成能力。

Result: 在TLS 1.2/1.3、IKEv1/v2、SSH和TLCP的主流实现上，SeriCrypt生成的消息序列均可被实现接受，并在全部测试场景完成握手。基于规范约束的测试发现了5项规范违反；在相同时间预算下，模糊测试能够进入更深的协议状态，并获得高于主流模糊测试工具的代码覆盖率。

Conclusion: SeriCrypt表明，以LLM辅助规范理解、以CDSL承载可执行语义、再由通用执行引擎完成密码消息构造的路线具有可行性。该框架降低了密码协议有效报文生成的人工成本，并能提升一致性测试和模糊测试的深度与效率；其关键价值在于将非结构化规范中的密码学与状态语义转化为可自动执行的构造规则。

Abstract: Constructing syntactically correct and cryptographically valid message sequences is essential for protocol state machine learning, conformance testing, and fuzzing. Unlike plaintext protocols, cryptographic protocols involve complex cross-message state dependencies and cryptographic computation constraints. Existing automated approaches predominantly target text-based or plaintext protocols, leaving cryptographic message construction largely manual. We present SeriCrypt, an LLM-driven, context-aware serialization framework for cryptographic protocols. It employs a large language model to extract field constraints, state dependencies, and cryptographic computation rules from unstructured protocol specifications into a unified structured intermediate representation, formally characterized by a domain-specific language for cryptographic protocols (CDSL). A protocol-agnostic execution engine parses CDSL declarations, automating field value resolution, cryptographic primitive invocation, and byte-stream serialization. As case studies in protocol security testing, we use the framework to construct violation messages targeting specification-defined security constraints and to support protocol fuzzing, evaluating it on mainstream implementations of TLS 1.2/1.3, IKEv1/v2, SSH, and TLCP. SeriCrypt generated message sequences accepted by all evaluated implementations and completed handshakes in every scenario. Security constraint testing revealed five specification violations, and fuzzing reached deeper protocol states with higher code coverage than mainstream fuzzers under the same time budget, demonstrating the framework's practical value for cryptographic protocol security testing.

</details>


### [174] [Who Falls for SMiSh? Learning Through Survey Data Where to Best Target Awareness Training for Mobile Messaging Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24669)
*Cori Faklaris,Sarah Tabassum,Heather Richter Lipford*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过两项大规模、具代表性的美国成年手机用户调查，评估短信钓鱼（SMiShing）易感性。结果显示，年轻人及大学生更易受骗，且受试者普遍难以准确辨别合法短信；研究建议针对高风险群体部署移动端专属干预、验证机制与警告提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短信钓鱼随手机普及而增长，但不同人群的易感性及其影响因素缺乏系统数据。既有电子邮件钓鱼研究提示，人口统计学特征和使用情境可能影响风险，因此需要在移动短信场景中获得具有代表性的实证证据。

Method: 研究开展两项大规模问卷调查，样本为具有代表性的美国成年手机用户，并采用既有的模拟测量方法评估参与者对短信真实性的判断能力。第二项研究进一步比较了不同金融类短信版本，以考察消息表述差异下的识别表现。

Result: 年龄较低者与大学生表现出更高的短信钓鱼易感性。参与者不仅会误判可疑短信，也难以正确识别合法短信，表明短信真实性判断存在普遍困难。金融短信变体的比较结果进一步支持：消息设计和呈现方式会影响用户判断。

Conclusion: 短信钓鱼防护不应直接沿用电子邮件场景方案，而应设计面向移动端的干预措施。研究者、监管机构和电信运营商应重点服务24岁以下用户及高校客户，并在短信交互中增加身份验证、风险警告和可理解的真实性提示。

Abstract: As mobile phone adoption has surged, so have scams involving these devices. One such scam, known as SMiShing (or smishing) after Short Message Service (SMS), involves fraudsters sending phishing links via mobile texts. Despite the prevalence of SMiShing, there is a lack of data on who is most vulnerable to these attacks. Prior research on phishing (its email counterpart) suggests that susceptibility may vary by demographic and contextual factors. In two large-scale surveys, we use a previously published simulation method to collect data from representative samples of U.S. adult mobile phone users. Our findings indicate that younger individuals and college students are particularly vulnerable. Participants struggled to correctly identify legitimate messages, with the second study providing comparisons of financial message variants. Researchers, regulators, and telecoms can help users by creating mobile-specific interventions for under-24 and university customers and adding verifications and warnings.

</details>


### [175] [ICS Cybersecurity Datasets: A Systematic Meta-Review of Coverage, Evaluation Practice, and Structural Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24757)
*Konstantinos E. Kampourakis,Vyron Kampourakis,Georgios Kambourakis,Sokratis Katsikas,Stefanos Gritzalis,Mario Rodríguez-Béjar,José Luis Hernández-Ramos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文通过对2019—2026年18项研究的元综述，汇集并统一刻画83个工业控制系统网络安全数据集，指出现有数据与评测体系存在显著结构性偏差，难以充分支撑当前入侵检测研究的泛化性与可信度主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制系统入侵检测高度依赖公开数据集，但既有研究缺乏对数据集整体覆盖范围、真实性及其与评测实践匹配程度的系统审计。论文旨在回答现有数据语料是否足以支持研究中常见的评测结论。

Method: 作者从18项研究中识别83个工业控制系统或直接相关的数据集，采用统一的五维分类法进行标准化与特征标注；同时审计对应研究的评测做法，包括流式评测、训练测试划分和可复现性，并进一步分析数据集分类特征与评测可行性之间的耦合关系。

Result: 数据集高度集中于后期OT扰动阶段，占85.5%；覆盖IT/OT跨阶段演进序列的数据仅占8.4%；Purdue架构0级现场设备证据几乎缺失；源自真实运行环境的数据仅占15.7%。评测方面，没有研究报告流式评测，不足半数采用严格训练测试划分，仅两项达到可复现性要求。数据覆盖失衡进一步限制了多类评测方法的适用范围。

Conclusion: 论文归纳出三类核心失衡：架构浅层化、演进过程压缩和跨域替代。为改善研究基础，作者提出协同议程：构建跨阶段数据语料、建立具时间结构的基准测试、制定事件级标签标准，并建设运行数据共享的治理框架。

Abstract: Intrusion detection research in Industrial Control Systems (ICS) heavily depends on public datasets, yet no prior work has systematically assessed whether the collective dataset corpus supports current evaluation claims. This paper addresses this gap through a meta-review of 18 studies between 2019 and 2026, from which 83 ICS, or ICS directly related, cybersecurity datasets are identified, harmonised, and characterised using a unified five-dimensional taxonomy. The taxonomy reveals that the corpus is structurally skewed: 85.5% of datasets concentrate on late-stage OT Disruption tactics, cross-stage IT/OT progression sequences are present in only 8.4% of cases, field-device evidence at Level 0 of the Purdue hierarchy is effectively absent, and operationally sourced data accounts for only 15.7% of the collection. A parallel audit of evaluation practices shows that zero report streaming evaluation, fewer than half apply disciplined train/test partitioning, and only two satisfy reproducibility requirements. Furthermore, a taxonomy-evaluation coupling analysis shows that dataset imbalances constrain the scope and feasibility of several evaluation practices. Based on these findings, we identify three structural imbalances: i) architectural shallowness, ii) progression compression, and iii) cross-domain substitution, and derive a coordinated research agenda which covers cross-stage corpus construction, temporally structured benchmarking, event-level label standards, and governance frameworks for operational data sharing.

</details>


### [176] [Research Methodologies for Cybersecurity in Enterprise Environments: A Narrative Review, Synthesis and Executable Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24850)
*Tran Duc Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理企业网络安全研究中的方法选择问题，基于151篇文献归纳出11类方法，并将其转化为可执行、可评估和可复现的研究协议。研究指出，检测算法结果的不一致主要源于评价设计差异，而非算法本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业网络安全研究涉及系统综述、设计科学、受控实验、访谈和攻击图建模等多种方法，但研究者缺乏统一的方法选择与实施指南，导致结果难以比较、复现和推广。

Method: 作者对经核验的151篇研究进行叙述性综述与综合，将实践归纳为11个方法族，并针对每类方法说明适用问题、证据强度和常见失效模式。在此基础上，为每类方法设计包含执行步骤、所需工具、评价标准、效度威胁和报告清单的协议，并以可视化方式呈现流程、决策点与威胁。作者还比较了入侵检测算法研究中的矛盾结果，并从评价设计差异角度进行解释。

Result: 研究发现，不同研究对入侵检测算法的排序存在显著不一致；这些差异更可能由数据集、实验设置、评价指标和其他设计维度造成，而不是由算法性能本身造成。方法协议能够帮助研究者明确研究问题、评价条件、证据边界与潜在效度威胁。

Conclusion: 企业网络安全研究应采用受明确效度推理约束的方法多元主义。研究者应根据待决策的问题匹配评价设计，结合技术证据与组织层面证据，明确结果可推广的总体，并报告结果失效的适用条件。

Abstract: Enterprise cybersecurity research draws on a wider range of methods than any single community routinely teaches. Researchers face a selection problem before they face a technical one: a study may simultaneously need a systematic review, a design-science artifact, a controlled detection experiment, an interview study, or an attack-graph model. This paper addresses that problem in two ways. First, it provides a narrative review and synthesis of methodological practices across a verified corpus of 151 works. We organise these practices into eleven methodology families, detailing for each what questions it answers, the strength of its supporting evidence, and its common failure modes. Second, we convert each family into an executable protocol comprising ordered steps, required instruments, evaluation criteria, common validity threats, and a reporting checklist. Every protocol is also visually mapped to make the sequence, decisions, and threats legible at a glance. We also treat contradictions in the literature as evidence. For example, reported rankings of intrusion-detection algorithms are wildly inconsistent across individually careful studies. We argue this pattern is most parsimoniously explained by variations in evaluation design rather than the algorithms themselves, as these studies differ in design dimensions known to shift results by more than the margins separating the algorithms. Ultimately, the evidence supports methodological pluralism disciplined by explicit validity reasoning. We conclude that researchers must match their evaluation design to the decision under study, triangulate technical against organisational evidence, explicitly state the population a result generalises to, and report the conditions under which the result would not hold.

</details>


### [177] [Prompt Structure Redistributes, Not Reduces: An Empirical Analysis of Security-Weaknesses in LLM-Generated Python Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24857)
*Maitreyee Das Urmi,Jessica Pourleyli,Fabio Santos,Glaucia Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究表明，结构化提示词能显著提升大模型在安全敏感编程任务中的有效输出率，但加入更强的安全导向约束并不能稳定降低代码弱点总体出现率；其主要作用更像是改变风险的严重度与类型分布，因此不能替代独立的安全控制与审查机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型被广泛用于从自然语言生成代码，提示工程成为影响生成软件安全性的重要手段。现有实践常假设结构化或安全提示词能够生成更安全的代码，但仅以“是否存在弱点”衡量效果过于粗糙。该研究旨在同时考察提示词对生成合规性、弱点发生率、严重度及CWE类别分布的影响，并识别安全约束可能引发的语义偏移。

Method: 研究选取424个安全敏感的Python任务，分别使用GPT-4o和LLaMA 3.1-8B，在5种由基础提示逐步增强结构化与安全指导的提示词变体下生成代码。随后利用Bandit和CodeQL进行静态分析，从生成合规性、弱点总体 prevalence、严重度分布和CWE分布等维度比较不同提示策略；同时人工或语义层面观察严格安全提示是否改变了任务要求中的显式不安全构造。

Result: 结构化提示词显著减少模型拒答或无效输出。例如，GPT-4o的无效输出数量从424个任务中的338个降至37至52个，因而更适合开展大规模安全评估。然而，安全导向提示并未一致性地降低总体弱点发生率。对于GPT-4o，更强提示主要导致风险重新分布：高严重度发现占比从20.8%降至13.6%，而低严重度发现占比从32.0%升至43.5%。LLaMA 3.1-8B的变化较弱且缺乏稳定一致性。研究还发现安全驱动的语义漂移：更严格的提示会在未明确说明的情况下删除、替换或改写任务中明确要求的不安全构造。

Conclusion: 提示词结构化对提升代码生成任务的完成与格式合规具有明显价值，但安全提示词不是可靠的安全保障。它可能降低部分高严重度问题，却同时转移风险类型，并可能牺牲对原始功能需求的忠实性。LLM辅助开发应将提示工程视为前置引导，而非安全边界；仍需结合静态分析、测试、人工审查及其他稳健的软件安全控制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly generate code from natural-language prompts, making prompt engineering a key mechanism for shaping the security of generated software. Structured and security-oriented prompts are widely used to encourage safer code, yet their effects extend beyond whether detected weaknesses are simply present or absent. Using 424 security-sensitive Python tasks, we generate solutions with GPT-4o and LLaMA 3.1-8B under five prompt variants that progressively add structural and security guidance, and evaluate them with Bandit and CodeQL along two axes: generation compliance and security weakness prevalence, severity, and CWE distributions. Structured prompting substantially reduces refusals (e.g., GPT-4o invalid outputs drop from 338 of 424 to 37-52), enabling large-scale analysis, but security-oriented refinements do not consistently reduce overall weakness prevalence. For GPT-4o, stronger prompts primarily redistribute risk: high-severity findings fall (20.8% to 13.6%) while low-severity findings rise (32% to 43.5%); LLaMA shows weaker, less consistent shifts. We also observe security-driven semantic drift, where stricter prompts silently remove or rewrite explicitly requested unsafe constructs. Overall, prompt structure improves compliance but is an unreliable substitute for robust security controls in LLM-assisted development.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [IncSFS: Incremental Full-Sparse Flow-Sensitive Pointer Analysis for C/C++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24391)
*Kunlin Liu,Zhenbang Chen,Piyi Zu,Yide Du,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出IncSFS，一种面向C/C++持续演化软件的增量式全稀疏流敏感指针分析算法，在保证分析精度的同时显著提升代码变更场景下的分析效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流敏感指针分析具有较高精度，但难以扩展到大型项目。传统方法在代码插入或删除后通常需要重新计算，无法满足快速迭代软件开发对高效增量分析的需求。

Method: IncSFS首先将值流图转换为约束图，并通过强连通分量检测保持分析精度；随后以交错方式传播指向集的增加与减少变化，从而在单次分析过程中同时支持代码删除和插入。当分析期间指针关系保持对象无环时，该算法能够保证终止并计算最小不动点。

Result: 在六个大型真实项目上的实验表明，IncSFS相比完整流敏感指针分析平均加速9.60倍，相比传统的重置—重新计算方法平均加速5.84倍；与现有通过传播指向集变化的增量式指针分析算法相比，效率进一步提升15.8%。

Conclusion: IncSFS实现了面向代码持续变更的增量式全稀疏流敏感指针分析，在保持精度和理论收敛保证的同时，显著降低了大型C/C++项目的分析开销，适合快速迭代场景。

Abstract: Pointer analysis is a fundamental technique for compiler optimization and program analysis. Flow-sensitive pointer analysis provides high precision but is difficult to scale to large projects. Tailored for rapid iteration scenarios where software evolves continuously, we introduce IncSFS, the first incremental full-sparse flow-sensitive pointer analysis algorithm for C/C++ programs. IncSFS first transforms the value-flow graph into a constraint graph and performs strongly connected component detection to ensure precision. It then propagates increases and decreases in points-to sets in an interleaved manner, supporting code deletion and insertion within a single analysis pass. IncSFS is guaranteed to terminate and compute the least fixed point when the points-to relation remains object-acyclic during analysis. Experiments on six large-scale real-world projects show that IncSFS is precise and efficient, achieving average speedups of 9.60x over full flow-sensitive pointer analysis and 5.84x over the traditional reset-recompute approach. It also improves efficiency by 15.8% over state-of-the-art incremental pointer analysis algorithms that propagate points-to-set changes.

</details>


### [179] [NoC-Out: A Formally-verified Network-on-Chip Library for Rule-based Hardware Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24478)
*Max Kurze,František Farka,Sebastian Ertel*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出NoC-Out，这是首个可生成形式化验证k维网络的库与生成器，可根据配置生成k维环面NoC，并自动提供满足形式化规约的精化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 片上网络是多处理器芯片的通信骨干，其故障会影响整个系统。现有方法难以同时支持参数化、并发且高效的硬件设计，以及模块化、低成本的形式化验证，因此缺乏具备强保证且无需繁琐验证工作的NoC生成方案。

Method: 作者扩展基于Rocq定理证明器的规则式硬件描述语言Kôika，使其支持并发且高效的NoC设计，并加入用于模块化和自动化推理的程序逻辑。在给定配置后，NoC-Out生成Kôika实现的k维环面NoC，随后可编译为Verilog；每个生成的NoC均附带其精化形式化NoC规约的证明。

Result: 生成的所有NoC都继承形式化规约中的强活性保证，无需额外验证工作。评估表明，该验证方法不仅能够验证生成结果，甚至是使用规则式硬件描述语言综合高效NoC所必需的。

Conclusion: NoC-Out通过将参数化硬件生成、自动化形式化证明和Verilog编译结合起来，解决了高可靠NoC设计中验证成本高与设计表达能力不足的问题，为可扩展、具备强活性保证的形式化NoC生成提供了实用方案。

Abstract: The Network-on-Chip (NoC) is the communication backbone of any multiprocessor chip. A failure of the NoC has severe consequences for the whole system. Yet, no approach exists that provides formally-verified NoCs with strong guarantees but without tedious verification effort.
  Any library that generates formally-verified NoCs needs to be parametric in the structure of the NoC. This requires a hardware description language (HDL) that allows for parametric and concurrent yet efficient hardware designs as well as the necessary program logic to reason about them in a modular fashion. So far, HDLs fall short in both aspects.
  In this paper, we implement NoC-Out, the first library/generator for formally-verified k-dimensional NoC designs. In order to build NoC-Out, we extended Kôika, a rule-based HDL in the Rocq theorem prover, with support for concurrent yet efficient NoC designs and a program logic for modular, automated reasoning. Given a configuration, NoC-Out produces a k-dimensional torus NoC in Kôika, which can then be compiled to Verilog. Each produced NoC is equipped with a proof that it refines our formal NoC specification; no additional verification effort is required. Our specification proves a strong liveness guarantee, which consequently applies to all generated NoCs. In our evaluation, we find that our verification approach is even required to synthesize efficient NoCs in rule-based HDLs.

</details>


### [180] [MGQL: An Executable, Small-Step Semantics of GQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24565)
*Aditya Thimmaiah,Tong-Nong Lin,Milos Gligoric*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出MGQL，这是首个基于ISO/IEC 39075标准、面向GQL实质性只读子集的机械化小步操作语义，旨在弥合GQL非形式化规范与可验证实现之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISO/IEC 39075以600多页散文形式描述GQL语义，难以进行形式化推理和标准一致性实现。已有形式化工作要么忽略包语义、模式及多图复合查询等语义复杂性，要么仅覆盖模式匹配等孤立语法片段，未能形式化完整查询流程。

Method: 构建MGQL形式化框架，建模具有混合边方向性的多图属性图，并采用小步操作语义支持量化路径与边、定向和无向匹配、标签表达式、模式列表及复合查询。引入模式感知类型系统，通过封闭图模式细化变量类型、跟踪可空性、支持多种复合查询操作符，并以列表类型表示量化路径绑定；同时对类型系统进行健全性证明。

Result: MGQL覆盖了GQL大部分关键模式构造，并实现了对多图、包语义相关结构、复合查询和类型约束的统一形式化。所证明的类型系统健全性表明，良构查询能够产生符合声明模式的结果。

Conclusion: MGQL首次为GQL提供了与ISO/IEC 39075相衔接的机械化操作语义，为查询正确性、标准一致性和实现验证提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: ISO Graph Query Language (GQL) is the first international standard for property graph-based graph query languages, standardized as ISO/IEC 39075 in 2024. However, ISO/IEC 39075 codifies its semantics informally across 600+ pages of prose, making it difficult to formally reason about the standard or for a standard-faithful implementation. Existing formalizations are not adequate because they either: (1) significantly reduce the semantic complexity by omitting bag semantics, schemas, and composite queries on multiple graphs; (2) or significantly reduce the syntactic complexity by only considering isolated fragments such as pattern-matching, leaving the full query pipeline unformalized. Yet it is these semantic-syntactic features that make formalizing GQL non-trivial. We present MGQL, the first mechanized, small-step operational semantics for a substantial read-only fragment of GQL that is grounded in the ISO/IEC 39075 standard. Our formalization models multi-graph property graphs with mixed edge directionality and supports a large fraction of GQL pattern constructs: quantified paths and edges, directional and undirected matching, label expressions, pattern lists, and composite queries. The semantics is supported by a schema-aware type system that refines variable types via closed-graph schemas, tracks nullability, supports multiple composite query operators, and models quantified-path bindings with list types. We prove that our type system is sound, ensuring an end-to-end guarantee of well-formed queries yielding results that conform to their declared schemas. MGQL provides the first bridge between GQL's informal specification and a mechanized implementation, enabling formal reasoning about correctness.

</details>
